# 📖 Master Glossary — *The Path to Ascension Begins with the Exam* (《飞升从考试开始》)

> **How to use this file**:
> - This file is the **Single Source of Truth** for all names, pronouns, titles, locations, cultivation terms, and recurring jokes.
> - **Override Rule**: By default, the translator uses `Character Name` (or `Location Name`). If you fill in `English Name (optional)` with a value other than `(leave blank...)`, **the translator MUST use your `English Name (optional)` value instead** everywhere in the translation.
> - **Auto-Update Rule**: Whenever the AI translates a new chapter and encounters a new named character, location, sect, or key term, it must automatically append a new block to the appropriate section below and notify you in chat.

---

## 1. Characters (人物)

### Main Cast — Heavenly Spirit Sect (天灵派)

```yaml
Character Name: Zhou Hui
Chinese Name: 周诲
English Name (optional): 
Sex: Male (he/him)
Titles / Epithets: Untiring Immortal Venerable (不倦仙尊), Master (师尊), Grandmaster (师祖 / 太上长老), Professor Zhou (周教授), Young Master Zhou (周公子)
Sect & Role: Heavenly Spirit Sect — Grandmaster / Founder of the Exam System
Aliases & Forms of Address: 
  - Called "Master" (师尊) by his seven direct disciples
  - Called "Grandmaster" (师祖) by regular Heavenly Spirit Sect disciples
  - Referred to externally as "Untiring Immortal Venerable" (不倦仙尊)
Relationships: Master of Yuwen Xuan, Qu Qianxing, Ran Deng, Guan Ye, Lu Shulong, Mo Bei, and Mo Xi. In Chapter 198, admits to Ren Ran and Ruwang that he used his self-created *Seven-Life Reincarnation Wheel Sutra* (《七世转轮诀》) to rewind time seven times; explains that his peerless power derives from the qualitative leap of accumulating strength across seven lifetimes; notes that Guan Ye's muscle memory and research achievements from past cycles stayed with him, while wondering why Qu Qianxing's memories were wiped the cleanest. In Chapter 232, absorbs the concentrated cosmic energy of the divine feast channeled by Yuwen Xuan's devouring, briefly taking on a transcendent divine form with an energy corona and silver aerial roots; decisively rejects becoming a god ("living beings do not truly need gods"); devises a plan to upgrade the mountain gate's ancient peach tree with divine power to torture Yuwen Xuan with endless-eating illusions; reveals that "divine authority" is merely administrative clearance passwords created by an ancient civilization for the Genesis Machine, which the Heavenly Court monopolized and degraded across True Immortals, cultivators, and mortals; siphons and stores the divine energy with Peilan's help under the pedagogical motto: "Teach them until they master it!"
Speech Style & Personality: Calm, deadpan, scholarly, gentle on the surface but ruthlessly pedantic like a veteran university professor or high-school homeroom teacher. Speaks in measured, polite, slightly clinical sentences even when dropping devastating academic workloads or facing heavenly lightning tribulations.
```

```yaml
Character Name: Yuwen Xuan
Chinese Name: 宇文轩
English Name (optional): 
Sex: Male (he/him)
Titles / Epithets: Eldest Senior Brother (大师兄)
Sect & Role: Heavenly Spirit Sect — 1st Direct Disciple of Zhou Hui (Void Refining Stage / 洞虚期 / 炼虚期; formerly Soul Formation Peak / 化神期大圆满)
Aliases & Forms of Address: 
  - Called "Yuwen" (宇文) by Zhou Hui
  - Called "Eldest Senior Brother" (大师兄) by junior disciples
  - Later cursed/transformed form: Black Lamb (黑羊)
Relationships: 1st Direct Disciple of Zhou Hui; senior brother to Qu Qianxing, Ran Deng, Guan Ye, Lu Shulong, Mo Bei, and Mo Xi; forged a temporary master-servant contract with the former female sect master of Thousand Beasts Hall 200 years ago to sneak into their Beast Soul Tower ("neighbor's cafeteria"). In Chapter 159, overeats divine remnants, dreams into the higher dimension and bites the bound Taotie true form, reclaiming 7 lifetimes of devoured memories and cultivation base via Zhou Hui's Heavenly Dao aura tether, triggering his Void Refining breakthrough. In Chapter 160, his origin is revealed: 200 years ago, Zhou Hui and Tao Zhu rescued him as an infant from an artificial hell beneath the Abyssal Ocean designed to breed a Taotie soul, where dozens of pregnant women had been devoured by their unborn fetuses; born to a dying madwoman who stubbornly loved him as her own child; Zhou Hui insisted on raising the silent, weeping infant despite Tao Zhu and others believing it far safer to kill it. In Chapter 161, adopts an impeccably modest, polite "model youth" persona to appease the Immortal Arts Assembly into letting him enter the Void Refinement bracket (baiting Void Refinement powerhouses into underestimating him), then sweet-talks Tao Zhu into taking him to the Abyssal Ocean Society, while Peilan suspects he is eyeing Tao Zhu's symbiotic golden toad to make 'stir-fried bullfrog'. In Chapter 163, his ancient origin is further unveiled: the original feral Taotie was born near the Heavenly Court, ate a squad of celestial soldiers, and hid at the confluence of the Immortal and Demon Realms for millennia; suffering from demonic qi corruption, it deliberately let itself be captured by a True Immortal as an invasion weapon and orchestrated its own death and soul-splitting to cleanse itself in a new body; however, it miscalculated when its soul was adopted and domesticated by Zhou Hui, overwriting its old memories; terrified that Zhou Hui will discover it fed intel to the Heavenly Scripture writer and wrote itself into the plot, Yuwen Xuan tricks Tao Zhu into helping him breach the Langhuan library under the pretext of a Tenth Month Festival gift, secretly plotting to alter the records and destroy all evidence. In Chapter 165, breaches the heart of Langhuan with Tao Zhu to find the Heavenly Scripture manifested as a withered ancient tree with a struggling newborn bough; continuously devours its leaves until Zhou Hui channels power through the Heavenly Scripture Pagoda to counter-hack it; devours a glowing silver leaf, tastes Zhou Hui's spiritual energy, and freezes in absolute terror; caught red-handed when Zhou Hui steps out of the torn space, and is subsequently hung from the ancient peach tree at the mountain gate during the sect's grand recruitment day. In Chapter 166, is impaled by a spear of light and hung from the highest bough of the ancient peach tree draped in a school opening banner ("New Academic Year, New Starting Point, New Endeavor, New Harvest!") to publicly greet incoming recruits, causing domain-wide rumors that Zhou Hui is an impossibly demanding master who hangs even newly minted Void Refinement powerhouses for poor academic marks, paradoxically elevating his public reputation and garnering widespread sympathy from outside sect leaders. In Chapter 184, his 6th-cycle collusion with Qu Qianxing to push the counterfeit Master's ascension is exposed by the past-life Mo Xi. Still hanging from the peach tree at the mountain gate, he abruptly jolts awake from a dream of eating dragon eggs, sensing phantom malice and accurately deducing through void-lock intuition that Mo Xi has tattled to Master, though he mistakes it for ordinary sibling rivalry and squanders his chance to flee. In Chapter 187, still hanging from the peach tree at the mountain gate, he is woken in the dead of night by Ran Deng's howling grievances over Guan Ye's teleportation array project; is bribed by Ran Deng with Qu Qianxing's stolen Heavenly Robe (capable of deceiving even Zhou Hui) to detach a sliver of his soul and act as an intimidating enforcer against haughty Void Refinement and Soul Formation array masters. In Chapter 190, contacts Ran Deng via bronze mirror from the Yingju Hall of the Heavenly Dao Alliance, boasting that the Heavens rewarded his virtue by dropping "delicious meat" (Lin Qi) from the sky, who lured Guan Xinguang into joining the qualification examination; refuses to return immediately to guard his meat from being stolen. In Chapter 194, while cloaked by the Heavenly Robe, dangles his legs from Goddess Ren Ran's canopy right in front of her face and winks at Ran Deng; gets knocked flat on his face when Zhou Hui unleashes spiritual pressure; plays dead using the 'ostrich strategy' on the floor; bolts out after Ren Ran to pursue his 'delicious meat' (Lin Qi); sneers invisibly at Guan Xinguang's fortune blessing from the doorway of the treasure ship. In Chapter 202, transforms the mountain gate mirror realm into a titanic maw, baiting Lin Qi into the esophagus. In Chapter 203, coordinates with Mo Xi to trick Lin Qi into jumping into a vat of seasoning paste to marinate himself. In Chapter 204, lures Lin Qi into donning Qu Qianxing's celestial robe and summoning his complete True Immortal physical vessel directly into the Rare Beast Enclosure, devouring Lin Qi whole ("Plating complete. Ready to eat"); bloats into a room-sized giant fleece cube; whipped by Zhou Hui with the Immortal Binding Rope to shrink back, vomiting up Qu Qianxing's saliva-drenched celestial robe and frantically claiming he was merely "catching a thief and expelling a trespasser." In Chapter 229, dispatched by Zhou Hui to find Qu Qianxing on the God-Demon battlefield, he procrastinates and follows ancestral Taotie memories through interdimensional rifts into a primordial realm of liquid spiritual energy outside the Genesis Machine's domain; navigates an isolated cliff face and squeezes through a fissure into Xuanpu (the Hanging Gardens) where the Sky-Tour Ceremony is concluding; restrained from devouring the deities by Master's 'no eating humans' rule, he bluffs past the Imperial Celestial Guards who mistake his custom robes and arrogant swagger for an Imperial Prince / celestial nepo baby backed by Lord Tian Yan; thoroughly breaks celestial maid Xiao Cui's emotional composure with blunt existential questions about immortal servitude; and boldly seats himself at the low table situated between the Celestial Emperor and Tian Yan to feast on both menus.
Speech Style & Personality: Loud, impulsive, dramatic, battle-maniac who would rather fight ten lightning tribulations than sit through one written exam. Frequently puts his foot in his mouth and gets his lifespan/cultivation docked by Zhou Hui. In Chapter 85, codifies his "Five Principles of Being Human": (1) Master is paramount—rather eat himself than Master; (2) Siblings are important—eating them loses Master; (3) Don't eat humanoid intellect or beings in human form to avoid Master's wrath; (4) Eat rare items quickly before they turn into junior brothers or sisters; (5) Eat just enough to sustain bodily functions—overeating looks like a glutton (rice bucket) and is less cute than troublesome siblings.
```

```yaml
Character Name: Qu Qianxing
Chinese Name: 曲千星
English Name (optional): 
Sex: Male (he/him)
Titles / Epithets: Second Senior Brother (二师兄), Sect Master Qu (曲掌门), Imperial Third Scholar / Tanhua (探花)
Sect & Role: Heavenly Spirit Sect — 2nd Direct Disciple of Zhou Hui & Acting/Future Sect Master (Nascent Soul Stage / 元婴期; formerly Golden Core Late Stage)
Aliases & Forms of Address: 
  - Called "Qianxing" (千星) by Zhou Hui and Yuwen Xuan
  - Called "Second Senior Brother" (二师兄) by junior direct disciples
  - Called "Sect Master Qu" (曲掌门) by regular disciples and outsiders
Relationships: 2nd Direct Disciple of Zhou Hui; fiercely protective of the sect and his junior brothers/sisters; former mortal civil service examinee appointed as Tanhua due to his striking looks and intellect. When mortals attempted to sacrifice him to a "great demon," he researched ancient texts while engineering irrigation canals and enacted an archaic sacrificial rite, burning his own flesh and blood to drag the demon down, which accidentally summoned Zhou Hui. Observed Zhou Hui in disguise at the base of the sect before formally kneeling as his disciple. Possesses the "Talent of Wenqu" (文曲之资). Deeply obsessed with Zhou Hui's ascension ("wishing for one's master to become a dragon" / 望师成龙) because mortal folklore claimed the severed Celestial Path prevented someone with the Talent of Wenqu from ascending, leading him to dedicate everything so his master could achieve it in his stead. Traded to Eternal Villa as an exchange student, effortlessly mastering their True Immortal Blood refinement. In Chapter 155, converts his Nascent Soul breakthrough tribulation lightning and Villa Master Qingyang's refined True Immortal Blood to refine the 'True Immortal' bloody tumor into a multicolored Heavenly Robe (天衣), breaking through to Nascent Soul and awakening the mark of an astral constellation (星宿印记) upon his forehead. In Chapter 184, his 6th-cycle actions are exposed by the past-life Mo Xi: he secretly colluded with the 'missing' Yuwen Xuan, supplied him with food rations and spent mysterious sect treasuries, carried a scrying mirror to cultivate demonic arts and communicate with Yuwen Xuan, and aggressively expanded the sect while feeding resources to help the counterfeit 'Master' ascend, though present-day Zhou Hui unilaterally acquits him due to supreme mentor-disciple trust and lack of physical evidence. In Chapter 231, his true celestial lineage is unveiled: he is the nephew of Lord Tian Yan (Chief of the Ministry of Celestial Destiny), born to Tian Yan's younger sister and the Celestial Emperor himself, and was cast down into Langhuan; currently manifests as the dazzling Demon Sovereign leading the demon army against the Heavenly Court. In Chapter 233, feels divine power channeled from Master through the Mountain-Protecting Grand Array, assuming Master has 'feathered' (catastrophically overthinking as usual); contacts Ran Deng via remote call; stationed as the 'Demon Sovereign' on the frontlines of the God-Demon War, having categorized demonic hordes into brainless 'line-fillers' (cannon fodder), formation tools, bioweapons, and technical specialists via 'diagnostic placement exams'; conquered three strategically critical minor worlds; learns that Heaven-Protecting Heavenly King has arrived at the front and plans to evade to let the Heavenly King and the arriving Demon Emperor clash; receives spatial telemetry from demon surveyors showing the entire battlefield's coordinates shifting, discovering from Ran Deng and Mo Xi that Mo Xi's script is being hacked and the two battlefields are overlapping, with Mo Xi asking if he's willing to fulfill the prophecy of the Demon Sovereign who annihilates the Heavenly Court. In Chapter 235, transmits an authoritative order across spatial boundaries commanding all Heavenly Spirit Sect disciples to anchor their spirits as the Mountain-Protecting Grand Array activates; emerges within the monumental light of the Sundial Clock as the breathtaking, dazzling Second Demon Sovereign commanding tens of millions of demonic troops, shocking both mortal defenders and high-tier cultivators alike. In Chapter 237, descends amidst torrential purple-gold lightning, effortlessly pulverizing the Giant Spirit God and the besieging celestial army into flying ash to rescue Mo Bei, Qingyang Qingyu, and Jian Nuo inside Sky City's core. In Chapter 238, suffers spiritual backlash and coughs up blood after Tian Shou shatters his purple-gold ball lightning, having overdrawn his vessel by channeling all the new power transmitted by Master through the Mountain-Protecting Grand Array; discovers through the agony that his divine soul possesses a mysterious latent constitution bolstering his physical body beyond known theoretical limits; recalls his mortal past as imperial Tanhua locked in an opulent gilded birdcage by his secular mentors and emperor to be gifted as a pet to a Carefree Palace cultivator, where he reverse-engineered forbidden sacrificial arts from imperial archives and burned his own flesh and blood to summon ancient gods, being answered and saved by Zhou Hui ("...Is it you who is calling for help?"); refuses Ran Deng's plea to retreat to the Heavenly Spirit Sect, fearing withdrawal will expose the sect's genuine coordinates to the Heavenly Court hacker behind the scenes; and rallies the awed demon vanguard to charge the celestial forces. In Chapter 239, his origin is further detailed: he is the nephew of Tian Yan, born to Tian Yan's younger sister and the Celestial Emperor; born with the supreme divine authority Tian Yan coveted, he refused to submit to Tian Yan, who suppressed his divine power with the 'Wenqu' fate star and cast him into Langhuan to learn 'how to be a proper human'; upon receiving Ran Deng's confirmation that she has located the mastermind's spatial power vectors, he resolves to turn the tables and beat him at his own game.
Speech Style & Personality: Sharp-tongued, sarcastic, deeply responsible, overworked administrator energy. Constantly stressed about the sect's survival and his siblings' antics, yet deeply loyal to Zhou Hui. Possesses a Gold Heavenly Spirit Root (金系天灵根); excels in Astromancy (观星) and Mathematics (算学). Terrifyingly utilitarian with a rock-bottom moral baseline, yet holds Master's opinion as his ultimate moral anchor.
```

Character Name: Ran Deng

Titles / Epithets: Eldest Senior Sister (大师姐), Third Senior Sister (三师姐)

Aliases & Forms of Address: 
  - Called "Ran Deng" (冉灯) by Zhou Hui, Yuwen Xuan, and Qu Qianxing
  - Called "Eldest Senior Sister" (大师姐) by junior disciples
Relationships: 3rd Direct Disciple of Zhou Hui; born into a prominent cultivation clan. In Chapter 187, infuriated and half-dead from refining soul-fire when Guan Ye's large-scale teleportation array assignment dumps massive array-construction grunt work onto her Array Pavilion; sneaks into Qu Qianxing's cave dwelling to steal the True Immortal-refined 'Heavenly Robe' (capable of deceiving Master), using it to bribe Yuwen Xuan to detach a sliver of his soul to intimidate high-level array masters from her clan. In Chapter 190, arrives at the Fire Abyss Sacred Tree via Kunpeng and white crane accompanied by Old Ancestor Jiu Zhai; receives Yuwen Xuan's transmission confirming he baited Guan Xinguang into entering; confronts Guan Ye at the Sun Wheel Palace, revealing she created the Artifact Refiner and Formation Master Qualification Examinations to draw outside talents and secretly set up an arena for Guan Ye to crush his mortal enemy Guan Xinguang in artifact refinement to conquer his inner demons; jabs a command flag into Guan Ye's forehead when he calls her a 'spoiled playboy' (纨绔); terrifies herself when Zhou Hui reveals Guan Xinguang's registration, triggering Guan Ye's dormant vengeful ghoul persona. In Chapter 199, serves as commentator alongside Fairy Maogui; explains that Buyan Palace's arrays were tested by Master to withstand heavenly lightning and designed based on an emergency contingency list drafted by Guan Ye; snaps her fingers to activate an Observation Array powered by Langhuan that displays real-time telemetry of arena spiritual energies, deliberately making Guan Xinguang's disruptive violet aura and foul-play tactics visible to incite public outrage and permanently ruin Guan Xinguang's reputation so Guan Ye's hands remain clean; praised by Ruwang as a heart-manipulating prodigy, while Zhou Hui deadpan defends her as a harmless child who literally lazed herself to death in seven past lives. In Chapter 205, watches in horror as Yuwen Xuan vomits up Qu Qianxing's saliva-drenched celestial robe bearing her personal spiritual tracking sigil; attempts to shrink into the background to avoid being hung from the peach tree, but is caught by Zhou Hui and sentenced to manage the hospitality and public relations for the Immortal Arts Assembly alongside Guan Ye under threat of being hung alongside Eldest Senior Brother. In Chapter 232, the cosmic mechanic of her fate is revealed: the Ran Clan's generational cultivation inheritance links their soul-fire so that as long as the bloodline lives, the soul-fire never extinguishes; her living soul-fire was sustaining her trapped father Ran Zhen in the mystic realm, explaining why she was arbitrarily assigned a doomed villainess role in the original Heavenly Dao script (her death was designed to resurrect Ran Zhen and link the sealed realm to the outside world). In Chapter 233, oversees the Mountain-Protecting Grand Array during Zhou Hui's absence, managing the influx of divine power; rushes to verify Master's soul lamp; connects via remote call with Qu Qianxing across spatial dimensions thanks to her family bloodline inheritance and divine array authority; nearly loses her SAN value seeing Qu Qianxing's grotesque demon horde before calming down by clutching Master's life lamp; recognizes the shifting spatial telemetry of Qu Qianxing's frontlines as matching the Immortal-Demon battlefield script on their side and fetches Mo Xi. In Chapter 240, inside Water Mirror Tower, deduces that in the 1st cycle, Master earned ascension when the Heavenly Gate opened, but gave it up and rewound time 6 times to save his disciples, causing the Heavenly Court to hold a grudge and attempt to erase Master in subsequent cycles (world-ending lightning in cycle 5, impostor in cycle 6); overcome with guilt, her soul-fire flares up; stirs up Lu Shulong's draconic rage against their common enemy; weathers Qu Qianxing's blinding golden lightning through her stabilized soul-fire while the water mirrors shatter around her.
Speech Style & Personality: Easygoing, chronically lazy "salted fish" (slacker) who loves gossip, folklore, and miscellany. Has broad, eclectic trivia knowledge because she reads everything except what she's supposed to practice, allowing her to barely scrape a passing grade on exams.
```

```yaml
Character Name: Guan Ye
Chinese Name: 关业
English Name (optional): 
Sex: Male (he/him)
Titles / Epithets: Fourth Senior Brother (四师兄)
Sect & Role: Heavenly Spirit Sect — 4th Direct Disciple of Zhou Hui & Head of Artifact Refinement Hall (Foundation Establishment Late Stage / 筑基后期; formerly Foundation Establishment Middle Stage)
Aliases & Forms of Address: 
  - Called "Guan Ye" (关业) by Zhou Hui and senior disciples
  - Called "Fourth Senior Brother" (四师兄) by junior disciples
  - Called "Ye-zi" (业子) affectionately by Ran Deng
Relationships: 4th Direct Disciple of Zhou Hui; deeply close bond with Lu Shulong (whom he practically raised); former fire-tending boy (看火童子) in a subordinate cultivation clan's forge. In Chapter 186, arrives at the Fire Abyss Sacred Tree via Master's shadow conveyance to inspect the pedagogical program and assist in creating the immortal remains 'cloud server'; gifts custom-forged bronze bells with mustard seed spaces, a top-grade crimson dragon marrow jade calculation abacus, and sect-wide emergency summoning runes to Mo Xi and Mo Bei for their Golden Core advancement; presents several tons of self-drafted teleportation array blueprints drawn during his 'idle time', earning Zhou Hui's clearance jade pendant to the sect's secret treasury to lead the construction of the large-scale teleportation array connecting the Heavenly Spirit Sect and the Fire Abyss Sacred Tree. In Chapter 190, meets Ran Deng at the Sun Wheel Palace to question the qualification examinations; willing to surrender all credit and leadership of the large-scale teleportation array to Ran Deng's Formation Hall without resentment; takes a command flag jabbed into his forehead without flinching; when Zhou Hui announces that Guan Xinguang and the Heavenly Dao Alliance's Department of Works are entering the examination, he realizes Ran Deng's true intention, his external spine rattling against the floor as he laughs like a ravenous, ancient vengeful ghoul, vowing to "treat this examination very well." In Chapter 191, his terrifying past-cycle history of extreme, stubborn vengeance is revealed: in the 3rd cycle, fostered with Mo Bei and Mo Xi under Ruwang Ghost Emperor at Bi'an Tian, he cultivated ghost arts to Nascent Soul Middle Stage, obliterated Heavenly Dao Alliance branches, refined his mortal enemy into a ghost slave, and traded intel with Qu Qianxing; in the 4th cycle, furious at his martial siblings for tormenting Master, he refined his abusive clan into ghost slaves and, before Master's seclusion, stabbed all feuding siblings to death (except missing Yuwen Xuan and slacker Ran Deng), refining the entire sect into ghost slaves and inverting the mountain array to assist Master's ascension (giving Zhou Hui lasting PTSD); in the 6th cycle, believing all his siblings were dead, he refined himself into a puppet, took over the realm's largest villainous faction, and served as a ruthless hidden side-boss driving the 'protagonist' to the brink of despair until the protagonist cheated with heavenly lightning. In Chapter 198, during the Artifact Refiner Qualification Examination at Platform 44, his Samadhi True Fire suffers an abrupt mutation into an explosive cyan-red flame; calmly carving Wenyao fish bone without deviating a single micrometer, he calculates the furnace's endurance to the second, applying seven frost-covered wood blocks with accelerant to transmute the flame into legendary Liuding Divine Fire—a technique he independently invented in the 6th cycle; when his platform ascends to the same level as Guan Xinguang's, their opposing forging spiritual fields violently clash, and Guan Ye's Liuding Divine Fire overwhelms Guan Xinguang's Extremity Golden Flame, blasting Guan Xinguang's platform down from the sky. In Chapter 200, at Platform 44, Guan Ye maintains an impenetrable, golden-crimson spiritual energy field akin to a dragon's egg shell while steadily refining a segment of Flame Dragon Tendon (炎龙龙筋) with Liuding Divine Fire; because his Foundation Establishment spiritual reserves are insufficient to refine the dragon tendon alone, his technique is designed to absorb, purify, and refine all surrounding ambient spiritual energy—turning Guan Xinguang's disruptive spiritual field and Lin Qi's invading golden puppet threads into siphoning straws that funnel pure celestial energy directly into his furnace! In Chapter 201, when Guan Xinguang panics and throws Guan Ye's original mortal spine into his own furnace, Guan Ye uses the bone as a perfect conductor to refine Guan Xinguang's furnace from within, causing it to explode; as Guan Xinguang self-detonates to flee, Guan Ye's external spine reaches into the debris, locking Guan Xinguang's soul and refining him alongside the materials into a supreme treasure: the Immortal Binding Rope (捆仙绳); the refinement transmutes Guan Ye's oversized spine into a vibrant, warm coral crimson wrapped in sacred luminescence that he can now retract and shape at will, marking the complete perfection of his Dao Bone (道骨成). In Chapter 205, discovers his entire skeleton has evolved into Dao Bone, granting him effortless control of his tailbone spine and completely unblocking his spiritual energy absorption; handily offers a fire-asbestos towel and spirit spring for Zhou Hui to wash off Yuwen Xuan's drool; recognizes the array disc retrieved from Yuwen Xuan's throat as Dao Bone material; assigned by Zhou Hui to lead the Immortal Arts Assembly tournament trial integration with the Fire Abyss Sacred Tree learning system, aided by Ruwang.
Speech Style & Personality: Low, gloomy voice; quiet, patient, methodical, and hardworking; suffers from severe imposter syndrome, considering himself "utter trash" (渣渣) and a fish eye among luminous pearls. Born to a mortal blacksmith family with a trash Five-Element Spirit Root. Severely mangled, tendons severed, dantian shattered, and spine ripped out after failing to regulate the forge temperature and incinerating a Dragon Tendon Whip; rescued from a mass grave by Zhou Hui, who hand-forged a replacement spine from blood coral, blood scorpion tails, and jade marrow (which ended up slightly oversized, leaving half protruding outside his back). After obsessively studying industrial blueprints from Zhou Hui's library, he engineered the sect's first hydraulic plumbing system and aerial geothermal bathhouses. By continuously pushing his crafting volume and repetition without stopping, he achieved "Entering the Dao through Craftsmanship" (由器入道), drawing cosmic Dao manifestations and breaking through his bottleneck to Foundation Establishment Late Stage.
```

Character Name: Lu Shulong

Titles / Epithets: Fifth Senior Brother (五师兄), Fifth Junior Brother (五师弟), Five-Domains Dragon Sovereign (五境龙君)

Aliases & Forms of Address: 
  - Called "Shulong" (书泷) or "Lu Shulong" (路书泷) by Zhou Hui and seniors
  - Called "Fifth Senior Brother" (五师兄) by Mo Bei and Mo Xi
  - Revered by dragons as "Five-Domains Dragon Sovereign" (五境龙君)
Relationships: 5th Direct Disciple of Zhou Hui; True Dragon who originally separated a portion of his form; caught slacking off while retaining past-cycle memories and penalized by Zhou Hui to participate in the Immortal Arts Assembly. In Chapter 206, arrives at the Heavenly Spirit Sect undersea harbor on the opening day of the Immortal Arts Assembly alongside Jian Nuo, riding between the horns of the lost Eastern Dragon King Dansha; manifests in a half-transformed True Dragon form with silver horns, silver-blue facial scales, and a colossal dragon tail, exuding transcendent draconic pressure that compels all onlookers to bow; desperately attempts to hide his condition from Zhou Hui; pulls Mo Bei aside and reveals that Second Senior Brother Qu Qianxing has become the Demon Sovereign and is leading fifty million demon troops to invade the Heavenly Realm. In Chapter 240, his true higher-dimensional biology and reincarnation trauma are revealed: as a higher-dimensional entity, his memories reside in his true form beyond single-dimensional time resets, meaning Zhou Hui's time rewinds never erased his memories; every rewind stripped his cultivated power, painfully shrinking and deconstructing his grown body back into a fertilized egg like being flayed and beaten to paste—agony he hid from Master to spare his feelings. The bone dragon in the God-Demon War trial is revealed to be his biological father, whose remains were sealed under Thousand Beasts Hall by True Immortal Blood, condensing an egg under Zhou Hui's ambient spiritual energy that Yuwen Xuan dug out and Zhou Hui hatched ("Ah, I knew it—I really was born of Master"). Empowered by Zhou Hui's channeled power through the trial system, he clears 1,000 li of demonic qi with a claw swipe, daydreams of beating Eldest Senior Brother until he bleats like a sheep, and returns to Water Mirror Tower only to become hilariously jealous of Qu Qianxing's absurd human genius; when Ran Deng deduces that the Heavenly Court targeted Master after he rejected ascension in the 1st cycle, his eyes snap into razor-sharp vertical slits with incandescent draconic fury, cracking water mirrors; roasts Qu Qianxing's golden lightning barrage as proof his head is just a decorative ornament.
Speech Style & Personality: Specialized nerd; a natural genius when it comes to spiritual plants and alchemy (灵植丹药), but completely hopeless and panicky on general-knowledge subjects outside his specialty. Perpetually exploits his dragon constitution's natural poison resistance to handle toxic herbs bare-handed.

Relationships: 6th Direct Disciple of Zhou Hui; older twin sister of Mo Xi (dragon-phoenix twins / 龙凤胎); former high-grade cultivation furnace captive in Fallen Immortal Palace alongside Mo Xi, rescued and taken in as a disciple by Zhou Hui when he annihilated the palace. In Chapter 171, watches over Mo Xi during his breakthrough at the Sun Wheel Palace with hand on hilt; when a sinister demonic whisper attempts to sow discord and jealousy, she dryly deadpans that while she should logically envy her idiot brother for condensing a core before her, she is unmistakably proud of his flawless golden core. In Chapter 173, undergoes the Sacred Tree's trial of desire, battling continuously in a branch labyrinth for 79 days of subjective time; exhausts her stamina and fasting pills but reaches the brink of breakthrough in her sword Dao, driven by latent envy of Mo Xi's earlier breakthrough; when a handsome white-robed youth appears as her 'fated lover' to dramatically rescue her by slaying the giant lizard demon, she is so furious that his interference ruined her breakthrough opportunity that she instantly bisects him with her sword, shocking Shui Huang'er (who fears her idol has fallen into senseless slaughter) and bickering with Mo Xi who reappears disguised in her form. In Chapter 174, hears Mo Xi's accusations about her past-life self backstabbing Master in the counterfeit cycle and bursts into unprecedented, uncontrollable tears of profound agony and sorrow; when the bisected lover's corpse attempts to detonate, she reflexes with exquisite swordsmanship to dice it into frozen minced meat, which Mo Xi feeds into Yuwen Xuan's Taotie book artifact; teases Mo Xi when he differentiates once more into an alluring, slender feminine beauty. In Chapter 178, follows Mo Xi into his desire-constructed illusion of a magnificent treasure vault, relentlessly bantering with him and admiring an Ice Feather Orchid; attempts to meditate to recapture her lost breakthrough state; quickly detects something amiss when Mo Xi mentions Master assigned him a task, pointing out that Master prioritizes disciples' cultivation over busywork. In Chapter 180, manipulates Mo Xi into testing his simulation program against his Void Refinement future self while agreeing to monitor and maintain the system; while seated cross-legged before the screen, her unique constitution draws the heavy karma and entanglements of past cycles, causing vibrant Snow Glow Grass to sprout and envelop her as she enters a profound meditative breakthrough, with Zhou Hui proud of her stubborn resolve to face it alone. In Chapter 181, while guarding Mo Xi's trial, her Extreme Yin Constitution draws the crimson icy mist shed from Mo Xi's breakthrough, pulling her into an illusion of blooming scarlet Snow Glow Grass. Emptying her mind, her peerless sword intent automatically annihilates the encroaching crimson mist. When the mist manifests numerous versions of her "future self" embodying her swordplay's flaws—including versions dressed in sect robes, Fallen Immortal Palace furnace garb, prisoner shackles, and a blood-stained bridal phoenix coronet—she ruthlessly bisects them all without hesitation, using the combat to refine her sword intent and trigger a massive breakthrough straight to Golden Core Great Perfection, turning her brown eye into ice-blue. Confronted with the crammed memories of the previous counterfeit cycle where she was manipulated into betraying Master and the sect, she shatters the entire desire trial illusion in a cataclysmic explosion of ice. Rather than succumbing to sorrow, she furiously declares her desire to butcher her foolish past self, Eldest and Second Senior Brothers, and the scumbags who puppeteered her fate, before breaking down in tears in Zhou Hui's arms, vowing to protect Master and the Heavenly Spirit Sect. In Chapter 182, deeply shaken and disgusted by memories of throwing herself at the 'Child of Destiny' and leaking Master's seclusion dates in the previous cycle, she nearly runs herself through with her sword before deciding to live to avenge Master and the sect. When Zhou Hui releases the blood bead containing the past-life Mo Xi phantasm, she watches the phantasm try to sow discord by alleging Eldest and Second Senior Brothers captured and performed 'defying the master' experiments on the counterfeit Master. Shocked when Zhou Hui lies to present-day Mo Xi that the phantasm is merely purged Extreme Yin dregs to spare his feelings, she finally brings an end to the twin Mo Xis' jealous clinginess by threatening them with Yuwen Xuan's violent beatings. In Chapter 184, listens to the past-life Mo Xi phantasm lodge complaints about Eldest and Second Senior Brothers, privately concluding that knowing too much only makes her want to stab them both. Follows Zhou Hui through the earth veins to the southernmost reaches of the Southern Domain, where she is greeted by a breathtaking, boundless sea of multicolored Snow Glow Grass. Learns from the Fire Abyss Sacred Tree and Zhou Hui that when the land fell from the Heavenly Court in antiquity, countless immortals perished and their remains were frozen here; their lingering resentment coalesced into persistent yin spirits, which were stolen by Fallen Immortal Palace cultivators to mold her and Mo Xi's physical bodies and souls—making the celestial graveyard grass the literal source of their existence. In Chapter 185, when the missing ritual vessel fails to be found, she swiftly subdues a rampaging past-life "Mo Xi" beneath her boot and investigates the yin spirits with Zhou Hui's moonlight cloak. Discovers from "Mo Xi's" memories that blooming Snow Glow Grass and ritual vessel resonance were designed by Carefree Palace to factory-reset Extreme Yin puppets' minds and allow aphrodisiacs to brainwash them and their partners. Refusing to let external artifacts control her, she vows to master her own body and mind, prompting Zhou Hui to multiply her curriculum tenfold (including dissection under Lu Shulong). In Chapter 236, inside White Jade Capital, sneezes in sync with Jian Nuo; maintains a blank, vacant expression and guides Qingyang Qingyu to launch the ancient prototype of Sky City as required by the trial script; revealed through Fairy of the Sacred Mausoleum's 6th-cycle memories to have been the rumored mother of the child carried by Qingyu's daughter. In Chapter 237, guides Qingyang Qingyu and Jian Nuo into Sky City's core; coached by Lu Shulong via divine sense; wields razor-sharp sword intent to single-handedly battle the materialized killing intent of descending True Immortals; defends Jian Nuo and Qingyu until Qu Qianxing intervenes.
Speech Style & Personality: Spirited, mischievous, and fond of storybooks featuring handsome young swordsmen coming to save the damsel; quick to banter and tease her twin brother Mo Xi. Possesses an easygoing mindset, content to enjoy life and tasty immortal peaches while teasing her stressed brother.
```

Relationships: 7th Direct Disciple of Zhou Hui; younger twin brother of Mo Bei; former high-grade cultivation furnace captive in Fallen Immortal Palace alongside Mo Bei, rescued by Zhou Hui. In Chapter 169, visits the Fire Abyss Sacred Tree with Zhou Hui, where he is assaulted by visceral, terrifying illusions and past-life/original Heavenly Dao script memories of the Sacred Tree burning to ashes and dying demons wailing, feeling overwhelming destructive malice and terror as the 'culprit'. Upon hearing of the Sun Wheel Palace, slips into fragmented memories of having failed to enter it even as the Saintess's lover and having used a desperate measure (burning the tree). Encouraged by Zhou Hui, who has faith in his strength, to step into the Sun Wheel Palace without external protection to confront and fight those illusions. In Chapter 170, steps into the Sun Wheel Palace's demon fire without external protection. Overcoming the excruciating spiritual agony and fiendish demonic qi stemming from past-life/script memories as the regional final boss who once burned the Sacred Tree, he is galvanized by Mo Bei's warning that failure means "failing an exam" and by Zhou Hui's faith that Master never assigns questions beyond a disciple's syllabus. Channeling his golden-crimson dragon markings and molten-iron Yang spiritual energy refined from the mine spirit, he dissolves the surrounding demon fire and initiates a cultivation breakthrough inside the flames under Zhou Hui's protective matrix. In Chapter 171, completes refining the pure demon fire, harmonizing his masculine Extreme Yin Constitution with the flame's pure Yang, and successfully condenses a supreme-grade golden core, opening the gates of the Sun Wheel Palace. In Chapter 173, reappears inside the Sacred Tree's illusion realm disguised as Mo Bei, teasing her for ruthlessly bisecting her 'fated lover', and jumps up in indignant outrage when Mo Bei teases him about finally differentiating into a male. In Chapter 174, chases a treasure-stealing thief into the branch maze and brings the stolen 'fated lover' to Mo Bei, only for his consciousness to be clouded by lingering cycle memories, angrily accusing Mo Bei of abandoning the sect and backstabbing Master; upon realizing Master's supreme power makes such a betrayal absurd, he regains lucidity and is terrified to see Mo Bei weeping for the first time in their lives; after having his Taotie book artifact consume the diced corpse, his body undergoes another differentiation into a tall, breathtakingly slender feminine figure. In Chapter 178, brings Mo Bei into his desire-manifested grand treasure vault front hall (filled with ancient Human Sovereign relics like the Five Emperors Silk-Bound Gold-Jade Coiled Dragon Seal and an Ice Feather Orchid from cycle echoes); feels secure solely because Master is near; taps into Zhou Hui's pedagogical system to work on his "homework" which his desire frames as "controlling the demon race" to pull them into their camp, broadcasting provocative gamified anti-schooling slogans across the Sacred Tree that encourage demon students to drop out to win freedom. In Chapter 180, tests his Exhortation to Learning simulation program against his Void Refinement future self; subjected to grueling Yang fire attacks deliberately designed around his hatred of physical agony, he initially expends top-grade spirit stones and draws from the sect's mineral vein via his mine spirit; realizing his future self feeds on external spiritual energy and refusing to leave a failure record in the database before Master, he decisively severs his tether to the mineral vein, enduring the inferno purely through his own Golden Core and iron will; successfully withstands the trial, absorbing Void Refinement fire qi to differentiate into a tall, handsome, valiant young man while his future self reverts to an effeminate form. In Chapter 182, successfully breaks out of his desire trial only to find his past-life phantasm—a grown, seductive, resentment-filled incarnation—already present. When Zhou Hui tells a white lie that the phantasm is merely his purged Extreme Yin Constitution dregs, Mo Xi is overjoyed by Master's praise, but quickly grows incandescent with jealousy when his past-life self embraces Master and acts spoiled with 'green tea' manipulation. Desperate to eliminate his double, he erupts in flames shouting 'I want to kill me!' until Mo Bei terrifies both of them into freezing with the threat of Yuwen Xuan catching their scent on Master. In Chapter 184, the past-life Mo Xi phantasm continues ruthlessly lodging complaints to Zhou Hui about Eldest and Second Senior Brothers, presenting the high-tier demonic manual *Heart-Tailoring and Tongue-Carving Art* given to him by Qu Qianxing in the 6th cycle and exposing Qu Qianxing and Yuwen Xuan's secret collusion and sacrifices to push the counterfeit Master's ascension. Later, upon reaching the Southern Domain's southernmost sea of Snow Glow Grass, he maliciously teases Mo Bei that her favorite grass is actually the 'graveyard grass' growing over ancient immortals' frozen corpses, which served as the raw material for their souls and bodies. In Chapter 185, the past-life "Mo Xi" phantasm suffers a despairing breakdown when the suppressing ritual vessel is discovered missing, attempting to kill Mo Bei before being instantly kicked and pinned beneath her foot. Reluctantly shares memories revealing Qu Qianxing's past-cycle discovery of the Snow Glow Grass blooming mechanism, and frantically stops Zhou Hui from eradicating Carefree Palace prematurely due to their high endgame utility value. In Chapter 186, the present-cycle Mo Xi is left behind on the Sacred Tree to handle the 'Exhortation to Learning' system while Master and Mo Bei are away; plots to hold a grand 15-year-old Golden Core promotion celebration and fleece outside sect leaders for gifts; receives a bronze bell containing a top-grade dragon marrow jade abacus and summoning runes from Guan Ye; sits on Guan Ye's lap while squabbling over the Tenth Month Festival annual debrief, causing Tao Zhu and Mo Bei to suspect a scandalous affair; fears Guan Ye will steal his project until Zhou Hui assigns Guan Ye to build the large-scale teleportation array instead, privately noting Guan Ye is Master's most doted-on disciple. In Chapter 226, authors the narrative scripts for the Immortal Arts Assembly's God-Demon War trial system, having been tasked with copywriting as the sect's "finance student" who must know how to spin stories; smuggles the disciples' blackened "boss forms" as immortal-skeleton puppets into the trial system alongside Mo Bei; when his shadow / 6th-cycle silhouette offers to reveal Master's secrets, he decisively rejects listening to anything Master wishes to keep from him, choosing to act as an obedient child to preserve Master's happiness; this earns the shadow's approval, granting him the power of communion with the Fire Abyss Sacred Tree's spirits; his past-cycle history under Ruwang Ghost Emperor is revealed: fostered by Ruwang in the 3rd cycle, he opportunistically called him "Foster Father" out of survival fear, while Ruwang found the calculating twins unendearing compared to Guan Ye; when Ruwang projects from the Genesis Machine to audit his script, Mo Xi defensively argues that a system must prioritize balance above all else to avoid bugs, prompting Ruwang to realize that through the Genesis Machine server, Mo Xi's script could make the future transpire in the past. In Chapter 233, summoned by Ran Deng to inspect Qu Qianxing's spatial telemetry data; confirms someone is hacking and rewriting his God-Demon War trial script, causing the two battlefields to overlap; abruptly asks Qu Qianxing if he is willing to fulfill the apocalyptic prophecy and become the Demon Sovereign who destroys the Heavenly Court. In Chapter 234, stationed at the Fire Abyss Sacred Tree to maintain the Study-Encouraging System and trial system God-Demon War script; feeling like an overworked game developer acting concurrently as narrative designer, systems designer, level designer, and lead programmer plagued by buggy code, player shenanigans, and a spontaneous server merge; approached by Ruwang Ghost Emperor (manifesting via projection with a spinning opera mask atop his head); refuses Ruwang's crude deus-ex-machina proposals to obliterate the Heavenly Court and Celestial Emperor with brute genesis power (roasting it as lazy writing akin to "Eldest Senior Brother ate the Heavenly Court and all our troubles"); decides to beat the Heavenly Court hacker at their own game (将计就计) by allowing the server merge to continue to gather blackmail leverage and let his terrifying martial siblings act as chaotic "uncontrollable variables" (proudly proclaiming he is the weakest direct disciple in the sect); and unhesitatingly chooses to disperse the refined genesis power among the trial participants as Master intended rather than monopolizing it for himself, surprising Ruwang.
Speech Style & Personality: Playful, restless, and calculating on the surface, yet harbors deep-seated trauma from his past as a furnace slave; terrified of being deemed "useless" and discarded or recaptured once Zhou Hui ascends. Possesses an Extreme Yin Constitution (极阴之体) and originally practiced a pure Yin technique (纯阴功法). Driven by an obsessive need to amass spirit stones and prove his utility, even exhausting his spiritual energy to near-death. Deeply touched when Zhou Hui gifts him the sect's entire spirit stone vein and tells him he never needed him to be useful, only wanting him to have a dignified future.

In Chapter 187, storms into the Court of Ceremonies in the Northern Domain to investigate Siwu's six-month disappearance and the vanishing of her Carefree Palace colleague; confronted by Xin Zhu, who reveals Siwu was rescued by Zhou Hui and is recovering at Heavenly Spirit Sect; secretly colludes with an elderly Court of Ceremonies practitioner to invoke the 'Guardian Demon-Subduing Heavenly General' as an emergency backchannel to the Heavenly Court. In Chapter 198, learns from Zhou Hui that he rewound time seven times with his *Seven-Life Reincarnation Wheel Sutra*; refuses to believe a mortal could manipulate time through diligence, hysterically accusing Zhou Hui of harvesting Cha Na and herself to steal temporal powers; attempts to flee to report to the Heavenly Court, but is instantly pinned back into her chair by Ruwang Ghost Emperor's blood-mist; relishes the clash between Guan Ye and Guan Xinguang, hoping Lin Qi will crush Guan Ye, only to be horrified when Guan Xinguang's platform is blasted down.

```yaml

In Chapter 188, receives Goddess Ren Ran's emergency transmission regarding Minor World No. 67; recognizes the world as part of the fallen Langhuan Academy and smells an opportunity for paid leave (and potential leverage against his superior's superior, Celestial Emperor Tunhuo); decides to descend personally without reporting to the Southern Heavenly Gate, requesting a 'Dao Bone' or 'Immortal Remains' from the Heavenly Dao Alliance to house his unsealed power. In Chapter 194, stays aboard the Northern Domain treasure ship to avoid direct confrontation; detects Zhou Hui's Heavenly Court-certified divine spark acquired from repairing the Ascension Stairway; berates Ren Ran's lack of intelligence; borrows cosmic fortune from Tian Yan to bestow a protective golden fortune blessing upon Guan Xinguang to rig the Artifact Refiner Qualification Examination. In Chapter 196, possesses Guan Xinguang's body at the examination hall; observes the intact spiritual energy and celestial dew of the Heavenly Spirit Sect, realizing this minor world is an off-the-books perk left by celestial colleagues that he can plunder; coerces Guan Xinguang into proceeding with the exam to place a mark on Guan Ye's Dao Bone at Platform 44. In Chapter 199, flies into a rage inside Guan Xinguang's consciousness when their platform is blasted down by Guan Ye; realizes Guan Xinguang has zero genuine forging pride or defense of his craftsmanship; violently squeezes Guan Xinguang's brain and threatens to erase his cognitive faculties entirely if he fails to maintain the spiritual energy field and stay interlocked with Guan Ye. In Chapter 200, controls his physical flesh stationed in the Immortal-Demon Realm inside a pavilion shielded by 72 arrays to avoid the temporal time lag between the Heavenly Court and the lower realm; bites his fingertip to shed a drop of pale-gold immortal blood (refined with the blood of [Redacted]) to project golden puppet threads into Guan Xinguang; attempts to probe Zhou Hui at the apex of the spectator stands, only to retract his threads in terror when Zhou Hui glances over; drives golden threads deeper into Guan Xinguang to weaponize his vanity and pride; sends golden threads along the spiritual field into Platform 44 to extract Guan Ye's spine, but finds no cracks; when Guan Xinguang counters Guan Ye's Flame Dragon Tendon, Lin Qi sends golden threads in, only to shriek in excruciating agony as Guan Ye's Liuding Divine Fire burns them to ash and Guan Ye treats the threads as siphoning straws to refine Lin Qi's pure celestial immortal energy!
Speech Style & Personality: Cynical, exhausted white-collar bureaucrat demeanor; deeply desires paid time off and treats divine missions like tabletop RPG campaigns; sharp and calculating under the surface, unwilling to swallow lower-realm excuses and always looking for political leverage to climb the celestial hierarchy.

Relationships: Dragon Prince of the Boundless Ocean in the Eastern Domain; bloodline descendant of diluted dragon blood (mixed with spirit beasts); rescued from the Abyssal Ocean by Lu Shulong; in the 3rd reincarnation cycle, served as one of several dragon prince study companions assigned to Lu Shulong, whose notorious dim-wittedness and bungled navigation repeatedly caused disastrous chaos (getting lost in the Northern Domain, clogging Sky City's drainage canals with duckweed, and almost delivering Lu Shulong into Yuwen Xuan's cooking pot). In Chapter 221, invites Lu Shulong to an open-air tavern to tender thanks and flattery; reveals that after playing the Heavenly Spirit Sect's trial system, he experienced vivid prophetic dreams of the "future"—revealing memories of past cycles (Central Domain falling to demons, demons sweeping outward, and the Heavenly Spirit Sect being destroyed by 'heroes').
Speech Style & Personality: Handsome and stately in appearance with sword-like brows, dragon eyes, and red-scaled robes, but utterly ruined by comical, round chubby critters clinging to his head and sleeves; fawning, goofy, and slow-witted like all eastern sea dragons, yet holds boundless adoration and awe for the True Dragon Lu Shulong.
```

```yaml
Character Name: Jian Nuo
Chinese Name: 简诺
English Name (optional): 
Sex: Male (he/him)
Titles / Epithets: Young Master Jian (简公子), Young Master of Sky City (天上城公子)
Sect & Role: Sky City (天上城) — Young Master / Heir
Aliases & Forms of Address: Jian Nuo, Young Master Jian
Relationships: Heir to Sky City; unilaterally considers himself the fiancé of Qingyang Qingyu (Eternal Villa); former dissolute socialite who caroused with wealthy playboys and earned VIP status at Carefree Palace; tags along with Qu Qianxing and Lu Shulong into the Demon Realm; in the 3rd reincarnation cycle, after Qu Qianxing was exiled/released to Sky City, Jian Nuo served as his primary partner and right-hand lackey, helping Qu Qianxing conquer and dominate the cultivation world (including the Heavenly Dao Alliance) in opposition to Yuwen Xuan's Abyssal Ocean Society. In Chapter 235, referenced comically by a Sky City guard participating in the trial system who questions phantom past-cycle memories of Sky City having a 'son-in-law' summoning celestial reinforcements ("Sky City only has an eldest young master! It's not like Eldest Young Master Jian Nuo was going to marry out as a bride!"). In Chapter 237, enters Sky City's embryonic core with Qingyang Qingyu and Mo Bei; attempts to impress Qingyu by offering Sky City's secrets and volunteering to be her research 'guinea pig'; experiences painful phantom memory flashes of a daughter begging for True Immortal Blood and Qingyu dying in childbirth in the previous cycle; deploys Sky City's heirloom mini-array and scrying mirror, accidentally revealing their location to True Immortal celestial soldiers before Qu Qianxing arrives.
Speech Style & Personality: Pragmatic, socially perceptive, adaptable, and surprisingly shrewd behind his playboy persona. Knows when to yield and flatter, naturally aligning with Qu Qianxing's authoritative demeanor and providing invaluable advice on political maneuvering and deception.
```

```yaml
Character Name: Guan Xinguang
Chinese Name: 关信广
English Name (optional): 
Sex: Male (he/him)
Titles / Epithets: Chief Director Guan (关官长), Guild Leader Guan (关行首)
Sect & Role: Heavenly Dao Alliance — Chief Director of the Department of Works (司工部官长) & Artifact Refinement Guild Leader (大乘期 / Mahayana Stage)
Aliases & Forms of Address: Guan Xinguang, Chief Director
Relationships: Leading figure of artifact refinement across the mortal realm; received a Celestial Edict ~100 years ago from Tian Yan to extract Guan Ye's spine / Dao Bone; upon refining it into ordinary mortal bone, panicked and silenced the subordinate clan head and all disciples in the forge; lived in mortal dread of Zhou Hui ever since. In Chapter 189, interrogated and soul-searched by Goddess Ren Ran regarding the Dao Bone; terrified when Lin Qi proposes extracting Guan Ye's new beast-bone spine; horrified to discover Heavenly Spirit Sect is launching an Artifact Refiner Qualification Examination. In Chapter 190, seen via bronze mirror in the Yingju Hall of the Heavenly Dao Alliance alongside Goddess Ren Ran; subsequently signs up for the Heavenly Spirit Sect's Artifact Refiner Qualification Examination alongside his entire Department of Works. In Chapter 192, hysterically begs Goddess Ren Ran not to make him leave the Heavenly Dao Alliance due to his deep trauma from seeing Zhou Hui pulverize Carefree Palace's VIP boxes; coerced into taking the exam by Heavenly General Lin Qi, who bestows a divine golden blessing upon him to numb his fear and stokes his vanity to assert domain over the large-scale teleportation array. In Chapter 196, arrives at the examination hall accompanied by Lin Qi who is possessing his body; horrified to discover that his participation attracted the entire cultivation realm, packing the spectator stands with four Tribulation Transcendents and dozens of Mahayana titans; steps onto the transforming examination platform and notices Guan Ye at Platform 44 radiating a steadfast aura; resolves to forge his way to the highest platform to locate the Dao Bone. In Chapter 198, while his furnace burns with Extremity Golden Flame, his platform ascends to the same level as Guan Ye's Platform 44; their opposing forging materials and mutated flames trigger an intense spatial clash across the void; despite his Mahayana cultivation and Lin Qi's celestial blessing, Guan Ye's Liuding Divine Fire overpowers his flame, causing his platform to crash down in flames and smoke. In Chapter 199, his petty calculations are unveiled: he intentionally triggered the spiritual energy clash to brand Guan Ye with the True Immortal Mark and blast all other examinees out of the sky to claim an unquestioned 1st place and mock Zhou Hui's teleportation array; after crashing down to Platform 6, he is coerced by Lin Qi's painful soul-crushing threat to force the refinement to continue; he dumps top-grade materials (Ten-Thousand-Year Dark Iron, Thousand-Year Moon Essence) into his cauldron with brute-force furnace heat, causing an ominous violet spiritual field to spill over and disrupt adjacent examinees; this disruptive aura is projected visually across the arena by Ran Deng's Observation Array, prompting spectator refiners to realize he is merely crafting a common flying sword and openly accuse him of malicious foul-play. In Chapter 200, possessed and driven by Lin Qi's golden threads, he hallucinates spectator jeers questioning his loss to a Foundation Establishment cultivator; driven mad by wounded vanity and furious pride, he vows to forge the greatest treasure and show the world he is the supreme refiner; adds counter-materials to weaponize his violet spiritual field against Guan Ye's Flame Dragon Tendon, having completely forgotten the original excuse he used to extract Guan Ye's spine; when Guan Ye begins siphoning and refining their energies, he realizes with horror that Guan Ye is using lack of spiritual energy to refine and absorb all ambient spiritual energies, turning Lin Qi's celestial threads into siphoning straws!
Speech Style & Personality: Possesses a dull, wooden, ruddy square face and a burly frame, resembling an honest, hardworking mortal blacksmith who seems incapable of lying; beneath his honest facade lies deep jealousy, cowardice, and ruthless cruelty.
```

```yaml
Character Name: Sikong Yao
Chinese Name: 司空耀
English Name (optional): 
Sex: Male (he/him)
Titles / Epithets: Shadowless Sword Venerable (绝影剑尊)
Sect & Role: Palace of Carefree Wandering (逍遥宫) / Sword Cultivator (剑修)
Aliases & Forms of Address: Sikong Yao, Shadowless Sword Venerable (绝影剑尊)
Relationships: Powerful sword immortal affiliated with the Carefree Palace; carries a sword casket on his back; formerly sought to claim Thought Pavilion as his own; hunted relentlessly by Zhou Hui across the realm for seven full years because Zhou Hui was trying to locate Carefree Palace's hidden headquarters. In Chapter 192, smuggles a large contingent of defecting slave craftsmen from Carefree Palace aboard the grand treasure ship heading to Mount Tianling, intending to use them to curry favor with Zhou Hui and secure his own pardon; senses upper-realm celestial qi from Lin Qi and secretly prepares his own escape routes. In Chapter 198, Zhou Hui reflects on hunting him for eight years: not because Sikong Yao was evil, but because his 'accident-prone constitution' (事故体质) naturally flushed out Carefree Palace crime dens wherever he went, allowing Zhou Hui to 'walk him like a dog' for clues; hides under his chair in terror during Ruwang and Zhou Hui's confrontations with Ren Ran, refusing to get involved. In Chapter 232, laments that the exploration squad has dwindled to 'one and a half' non-combatants with the healer Peilan and powerhouse Zhou Hui both immobilized; terrified by Zhou Hui's veiled warning of a 'catastrophic future' if he doesn't cooperate; deeply covets Tao Zhu's golden toad luck stat and agrees to team up with Tao Zhu and Jiu Zhai to rescue Ran Zhen.
Speech Style & Personality: Pragmatic, detached, unbothered by insults or lost face, possesses peerless speed and survival instincts. When sensing Zhou Hui's spiritual aura latching onto his shadow, he immediately cries "Holy crap!" and bolts at hypersonic speed. Realistic and candid about Zhou Hui's terrifying lethality.
```

Relationships: Ancient ancestor of the Palace of Carefree Wandering who successfully ascended to the immortal realm; left behind the Thought Pavilion (一念阁) as a self-consistent mini cave-heaven and the supreme domain technique "All Perish in a Single Thought" (一念俱灭)
Speech Style & Personality: Legendary ascended immortal whose spatial-temporal sword craftsmanship and miniature world construction were praised even by Zhou Hui.
```

```yaml
Character Name: Master Lan Pei
Chinese Name: 兰佩先生 / 佩兰先生
English Name (optional): 
Sex: Male (he/him)
Titles / Epithets: Master Lan Pei (兰佩先生), Foremost Divine Physician (当世第一医修)
Sect & Role: Rogue Cultivator / Foremost Divine Physician of the Mortal Realm (Mahayana Stage / 大乘期)
Aliases & Forms of Address: Master Lan Pei, Lan Pei, Peilan (佩兰), Old Fox
Relationships: Legendary healer who attained the Mahayana stage before Zhou Hui had even set foot upon the immortal path; pursued by Qu Qianxing and Yuwen Xuan to treat Zhou Hui. In Chapter 232, acts as the expedition's chief physician; warns of the mortal peril of absorbing excessive cosmic energy; agrees to perform a spiritual channeling (导灵) procedure to siphon and store Zhou Hui's excess divine power using Zhou Hui's intricate formulas; warns Zhou Hui against distributing divine authority to flawed living beings.
Speech Style & Personality: Perennially elusive and mysterious; the ultimate medical authority in the mortal realm whose miraculous pills and treatments are sought even by Tribulation Transcendence powerhouses. In reality, he is an ancient wild yellow fox (野狐狸)—not even a pure spirit fox—who entered the Dao through Medicine (由医入道) via astronomical repetition/practice count (练习度). Balding and scruffy when soaking in hot springs, he loves pranking visitors by transforming into an eight-foot-bearded patriarch or a sultry vixen before adopting his standard persona of a gaunt, elegant middle-aged scholar. Infinitely envious of Zhou Hui's devoted, filial disciples (especially when Yuwen Xuan brings steaming roast chickens and fine wine), lamenting that his own accomplished children never visit.
```

Fled to Fengqing City using a True Immortal spatial talisman, only to find the auction being live-streamed across the continent; his body dissolved into a grotesque puddle of melted flesh and charred bone due to the demonic brand's delayed lethality.

Speech Style & Personality: Burly giant of a sovereign who drafted the initial containment strategy against the Heavenly Spirit Sect, assuming Zhou Hui had zero commercial expertise. Drowned in bitter regret upon witnessing Zhou Hui's modern economic genius, forced into desperate, underhanded suicide-bomb sabotages to protect his monopoly over the Central Domain. Shudders in revulsion at the gorgeous youth's sickening fetishes.

```yaml
Character Name: Ran Zhen

Relationships: Current head of the premier formation clan (Ran Clan); biological father of Ran Deng; son of the previous matriarch who befriended Zhou Hui; member of the East Hedge Garden (东篱苑); arrived at Heavenly Spirit Sect alongside the Plum-Bamboo Immortal Couple and Lan Pei. In Chapter 232, discovered by Zhou Hui's divine projection to be trapped in a hellish subterranean demonic realm alongside fellow East Hedge Garden members, mangled and under demonic mind-control while stacking pebbles on an altar; revealed to be sustained alive solely through his soul-fire tether to his living daughter Ran Deng.
Speech Style & Personality: Observant, jovial, shameless academic opportunist, yet deeply sincere in his passion for formation research. Inherited Mahayana cultivation via the Ran Clan's secret generational transmission technique, as array masters in this world are cursed with tragically short lifespans (mostly dying around Golden Core). Terrified by a Heavenly Book prophecy that the Ran Clan would be destroyed in his hands, he resolved to face death honorably while excavating ancient arrays in the Abyss Sea. Deeply grateful to Zhou Hui for educating his unruly daughter, he petitioned Zhou Hui to accept Ran Clan branch disciples as a backup seed.
```

Speech Style & Personality: Simple, round-faced, and honest-looking on the outside with cute beady eyes, but calculating and shrewd inside; ecstatic that sect life provides free spirit beast milk every morning; deeply ashamed upon discovering the horrific cafeteria smell comes from his Seventh Grandpa's cooking; resolves to study human recipes in the Library Pavilion to redeem his clan's culinary honor.

```yaml
Character Name: Jing Chenjun
Chinese Name: 景宸君
English Name (optional): 
Sex: Male (he/him)
Titles / Epithets: Third Prince of Yongfeng Empire (永丰王朝第三皇子)
Sect & Role: Heavenly Spirit Sect — Disciple (Beginner Class / 初级班) / Third Prince of the Yongfeng Empire (Mortal / 凡人)
Aliases & Forms of Address: Jing Chenjun, Third Prince
Relationships: Third son of the Emperor of the Yongfeng Empire; roommate to Huang Xiao, Ji Gen, and Cheng Ziyi. In Chapter 222, his tragic 6th-cycle past is revealed: after discovering the mortal Human Sovereign bloodline barred him from cultivating immortality, he sought elixirs in the East Sea, was saved by Carefree Palace, and became their puppet proxy to kill his brothers and seize the throne, swallowing an Elixir of Immortality; transformed into a half-human, half-ghost creature upon defying Carefree Palace; during the 6th-cycle demon invasion, used the self-immolating flame art left in the Hanlin Academy by the ancient beautiful minister (Qu Qianxing) to incinerate demons at the cost of being repeatedly flayed alive; met the Second Demon Sovereign, who urged him to endure so 'That Person' (Zhou Hui) could resurrect them in a new cycle; in the 7th cycle, wakes up in the dormitory from phantom trauma, finding purpose in operating the magitech Spell-Armor Cannons with his arithmetic talents in the trial system. In Chapter 235, repeatedly enters the trial system during the fall of White Jade Capital despite possessing an uncultivated mortal body; endures corrosive demonic qi that sloughs the flesh from his shoulders and throat like a 'bloody gourd'; calibrates the defense array's spiritual aperture with precision instruments and delivers blueprints for Amplification City-Defense Cannons designed by the Artifact Refinement Workshop, lecturing high-tier cultivators against operating arrays 'by feeling' like novice cooks; surprised by his uncanny pain tolerance stemming from 6th-cycle flaying; despairs at the limits of mortals against demons until Qu Qianxing's voice echoes; and nearly dies of shock when Qu Qianxing appears as the dazzling Demon Sovereign leading the demon legion ("Senior Brother Qu! Which side are you actually on?!").
Speech Style & Personality: Elegant, courteous, disciplined, adheres strictly to refined imperial court etiquette; fair and slender. Realized mortal emperors are merely puppets of cultivation sects and resolved to seek immortality; possesses no spirit root, relying on royal defensive treasures to pose as a rogue cultivator; rejected by dozens of northern sects before an emergency teleportation accident stranded him in the Central Domain, where Heavenly Spirit Sect accepted him without checking spirit roots; placed in beginner class due to strong arithmetic/classical scores. Has since advanced to the second-year class, serving as an academy instructor and Immortals' Bazaar guide.
```

Speech Style & Personality: Shrewd, ambitious, and cooperative in the original script; seeks survival and personal advantage; fell under Yuwen Xuan's clandestine influence without realizing her life is in his hooves.
```

```yaml
Character Name: Tao Zhu
Chinese Name: 陶竹
English Name (optional): 
Sex: Male (he/him)
Titles / Epithets: Boss Tao (陶老板), Old Tao (老陶)
Sect & Role: Abyssal Ocean Society (渊海会) — Secret Boss / Artifact Trade Syndicate Leader (Mahayana Stage / 大乘期)
Aliases & Forms of Address: Tao Zhu, Boss Tao, Old Tao, Toad Immortal (蛤蟆仙君)
Relationships: Millennia-old acquaintance of Zhou Hui (dating back to when Zhou Hui was Golden Core); warned Zhou Hui against adopting brats; secret leader of the Abyssal Ocean Society; joined Zhang Hongjing's "Persuasion Delegation" to curse at Zhou Hui before his presumed demise. In Chapter 165, enters the Langhuan Forbidden Grounds with Yuwen Xuan, marveling at the endless books while calculating their resale value in spirit stones; attempts to refine and subjugate the ancient Heavenly Scripture tree by binding it in copper coins and spewing toxic green mist via his symbiotic golden toad; unknowingly clashes spells against Zhou Hui, gets struck in the belly and coughs blood, and is left utterly shocked when Zhou Hui rips open space right out of the tree trunk. In Chapter 166, watches outside cultivators commiserate over Yuwen Xuan's punishment; still unaware that he was played as a tool by Yuwen Xuan, he indignantly pities the boy for trying to give Zhou Hui a "surprise". Terrified of being implicated and hung from the peach tree alongside Yuwen Xuan through collective guilt, he hastily passes the buck when Qu Qianxing summons him, only to be bewildered when told Zhou Hui wishes to discuss traveling to the Southern Domain. In Chapter 167, visits Zhou Hui's immortal cave alongside Mo Bei and Mo Xi; dreading being implicated in Yuwen Xuan's punishment, he preemptively refuses to travel to the Southern Domain to seek medicine for the Heavenly Scripture, yet his stomach injury (from the Golden Toad damaged by Zhou Hui) tempts him; when Mo Xi sweet-talks him into accompanying him to vet a business deal in the Southern Domain using the exact same tone Yuwen Xuan previously used, Tao Zhu bristles in paranoid panic that he is walking straight into another trap. In Chapter 172, walks along the Sun Wheel Palace corridor suspecting the demons of treachery; spots a spiritual energy loophole and attempts a secret trick to snatch the Supreme Treasure first, failing the treasure's trial of desire due to his greed and getting locked inside a constricting bird cage like a chicken while Shui Huang'er mocks him as "Toad Immortal"; tries to bluff that harming Untiring will provoke human-demon war; is flabbergasted and murderous when discovering Zhou Hui could see him the entire time and simply ignored his cries to study the Heavenly Dao threads. In Chapter 173, reflects on Zhou Hui's millennia-old habit of meditating anywhere oblivious to the world (recalling how Jian Haoge had to scrub him for three days after digging him out of an ancient beast's stomach in the Abyssal Ocean, and how Peilan had to constantly rescue victims from infant Yuwen's mouth); wonders why Mo Bei and Mo Xi haven't been disqualified yet, getting mocked by Shui Huang'er. In Chapter 232, worries that Zhou Hui will turn into a colossal tree like the Fire Abyss Sacred Tree, dreading the vengeance of Yuwen Xuan and Mo Xi; prepares to flip the altar to interrupt the feast before Jiu Zhai stops him; agrees with Zhou Hui rejecting godhood by roasting high-tier immortals as unfit to govern mortal realms; protests when Zhou Hui cites his golden toad's luck stat; teams up with Sikong Yao and Jiu Zhai to rescue Ran Zhen.
Speech Style & Personality: Gruff, rotund, calculating, and candid veteran merchant powerhouse; sports a round belly and suffers from old internal injuries; numb to the world's cruelties after millennia in the harsh Central Domain; deeply regrets failing to recruit young Zhou Hui into the Abyssal Ocean Society after seeing Qianxing Hall's booming tourism economy.
```

Speech Style & Personality: Debonair, perceptive, and worldly; carries an unfurled folding fan; well-informed on Qianxing Secret Realm's booming cottage rental economy and laments not signing a real estate lease contract with Heavenly Spirit Sect; loves strutting as the "First Champion Senior" despite getting soundly thrashed by clan relatives for hiding tournament intel.

```yaml

Speech Style & Personality: Patient, dutiful, and pragmatic. While he fits the outside world's stereotype of a muscular, aggressive body-cultivator meathead, he actually acts as the public diplomatic face of Blood Demon Hall to shield its overwhelmingly female disciple population from bizarre rumors and slander. Flew back 28 shichen without pause to deliver and plant the Black Coral tree in the sect courtyard, and calmly guides his impulsive master and eager junior sisters through the Abyss Sea transit system.
```

```yaml
Character Name: Jiuzhai
Chinese Name: 九斋
English Name (optional): 
Sex: Male (he/him)
Titles / Epithets: Old Charlatan (老神棍), Master Jiuzhai, Ancestor Jiuzhai (九斋老祖)
Sect & Role: Altar of Divine Decrees (神旨坛) — Diviner / Foremost Prophetic Astrologer (Tribulation Transcendence Stage / 渡劫期)
Aliases & Forms of Address: Jiuzhai, Old Charlatan Jiuzhai, Ancestor Jiuzhai
Relationships: Acquaintance of Tao Zhu, Lan Pei, and Ran Zhen; esoteric diviner who analyzed Zhou Hui's karmic accumulation and deduced he would become a permanent qi-generating fixture of the world like Zhujiuyin or Jianmu. In Chapter 161, reveals that Zhou Hui rewound/washed the world's timeline six times in previous cycles before exercising restraint, and that in one timeline Tao Zhu passed the Abyssal Ocean Society to Yuwen Xuan, who built it into a titan rivaling the Northern Domain Alliance and Sky City; recalculates the Heavenly Dao using a massive Chart of Heaven and Earth and the Eight Trigrams. In Chapter 230, reveals that the Shenzhi Altar's lineage originated directly from the Heavenly Court prior to the God-Demon War, tasked as Chroniclers to observe and record local spacetime fluctuations; confesses that they have engaged in 'selective recording' and have not reported Zhou Hui's cycle-resetting antics to the higher-ups; explains the convention of the Celestial Emperor's quincentennial Sky-Tour sacrifice and divine feast.
Speech Style & Personality: Prophetic, cynical, and candid; speaks in an agonizingly slow, sluggish drawl unique to diviners that makes listeners itch to punch him; delivers blunt cosmological calculations that rattle even Mahayana powerhouses.
```

```yaml
Character Name: Shen Qingqian
Chinese Name: 沈青倩
English Name (optional): 
Sex: Female (she/her)

Relationships: Master of Bai Wubin and all disciples in Blood Demon Hall
Speech Style & Personality: Direct, blunt, energetic, and candid; hates convoluted explanations and pedantic rambling ("Who has the patience to listen to a mountain of details?!"). Influenced by body refinement techniques, she prefers action over overthinking. Bold and fearless regarding sect security, secretly eager to experience how a full sect siege battle plays out. Easily captivated by beautiful things like the luminescent Black Coral tree and glowing jellyfish swarms, eagerly upgrading the planned Kunpeng ferry dock into a grand Undersea Vacation Palace (海下行宫).
```

```yaml

Rather than blaming the blockading Carefree Palace, he directs all his fury toward Zhou Hui and the Heavenly Spirit Sect, eagerly hosting banquets for Daoist Huang and orchestrating the boycott of the Tianling Auction by dumping coral seeds on impoverished demon villagers.

Despite being deemed a complete nonentity by sects, he is revered by mortal villagers as a "loose immortal" (散仙) who lives a disease-free century. Speech Style & Personality: In the script, a tragic, betrayed hero turned ruthless demon lord; in reality, Bai Wubin is a thoroughly upright, filial, and hardworking disciple who loves his sect and master, leaving Zhou Hui baffled as to how the Heavenly Dao could twist him into a patricide.

```yaml

Prides himself on having seen all the grandest spectacles across the Five Domains (Ascension Fair, Eastern Sea Market, Carefree Palace Treasure Fair) and scoffs at ordinary events. Whines dramatically and clutches Qingyang Qingli's sleeve for not aggressively hyping the Immortal Bazaar, but his haughty aristocratic composure instantly crumbles into green-eyed envy upon seeing Qingyang Qingli successfully flex as the premier hero in the Immortal Chess opening cinematic.
```

```yaml

Confined to her boudoir tower since childhood, she secretly kept a sharpened hairpin hidden inside her sleeve. ```

```yaml

Engineered a mechanical sugar press using academy blueprints and Artifact Refinement Hall guidance.

```yaml

In Chapter 236, operates inside White Jade Capital alongside Mo Bei and Jian Nuo; ignores Jian Nuo's emotional overtures and collaborates with Mo Bei to activate the ancient prototype of Sky City suspended within White Jade Capital; in the 6th-cycle memories of Villa Master Qingyang and Fairy of the Sacred Mausoleum, her marriage to Jian Nuo resulted in a daughter who was forced into Soul Formation as a living corpse and gave away Sky City to the protagonist Tunhuo.
Speech Style & Personality: Direct lineage prodigy of Eternal Villa currently at the Golden Core stage; a dedicated cultivation fanatic and "top academic ace" (学霸) who loves competing with peers to earn rewards from elders. Possesses impeccable ancient noble etiquette, yet rendered speechless by Zhou Hui's overwhelming majesty and silver-gray eyes. Shattered by Zhou Hui's impossibly deep diagnostic baseline exam, but found profound comfort and camaraderie in suffering alongside Mo Xi, Guan Ye, and Lu Shulong; bonded as "comrades-in-arms forged in shared academic trauma" and chose to study the "Secret Realm Guidance System."
```

```yaml
