In Chapter 221, invites Lu Shulong to an open-air tavern to tender thanks and flattery; reveals that after playing the Heavenly Spirit Sect's trial system, he experienced vivid prophetic dreams of the "future"—revealing memories of past cycles (Central Domain falling to demons, demons sweeping outward, and the Heavenly Spirit Sect being destroyed by 'heroes').
Speech Style & Personality: Handsome and stately in appearance with sword-like brows, dragon eyes, and red-scaled robes, but utterly ruined by comical, round chubby critters clinging to his head and sleeves; fawning, goofy, and slow-witted like all eastern sea dragons, yet holds boundless adoration and awe for the True Dragon Lu Shulong.
```

```yaml
Character Name: Jian Nuo
Chinese Name: 简诺
English Name (optional): 
Sex: Male (he/him)
Titles / Epithets: Young Master Jian (简公子)
Sect & Role: Sky City (天上城) — Great-grandson of City Lord Jian Haoge / Young Master (Nascent Soul Stage / 元婴期)
Aliases & Forms of Address: Jian Nuo, Young Master Jian
Relationships: Great-grandson of City Lord Jian Haoge of Sky City in the Northern Domain; fell in love with Qingyang Qingyu at first sight during her shopping trip to Sky City and aggressively courted her, sending dew-kissed Sky Lilies daily; disliked by Villa Master Qingyang as a debauched playboy who caroused with cultivation scions and had ties with Carefree Palace; fiercely determined to travel to the Central Domain to pursue Qingyu despite his great-grandfather's furious opposition. In the third reincarnation, he and Qu Qianxing formed an exceptionally close partnership known as the "Qian-Nuo Young Masters of Sky City" (天上城千诺公子), with Jian Nuo as City Lord of Sky City and Qu Qianxing as Alliance Leader of the Heavenly Dao Alliance, expanding their factions across the cultivation realm to their absolute peak. Reveres the Untiring Immortal Venerable since childhood, modeling his daily routine after him. Traveled to the Central Domain bringing enlightened masters of worldly crafts ("Entering the Dao through Craftsmanship") to boost White Jade Capital's academic impact factor, and sought to take Heavenly Spirit Sect exams. Secretly investigating Carefree Palace as spokespersons for "Them" and believes Zhou Hui aims to break the ancient ten-thousand-year-old curse afflicting the Qingyang lineage and the True Dragon. In Chapter 236, inside White Jade Capital alongside Qingyang Qingyu and Mo Bei, sneezes in sync with Mo Bei and is moved to tears by Qingyu asking if he is cold, acting completely foolish before being swiftly ignored; in the 6th-cycle memories, married Qingyang Qingyu and had a daughter who tragically simped for the protagonist Tunhuo.
Speech Style & Personality: Exceptionally handsome ("the kind of handsome capable of making two-thirds of women with conventional aesthetics swoon"); wealthy, lavish, and persistent in courtship, though viewed with suspicion by Eternal Villa elders; adheres to a strict daily morning walking routine around Sky City since Foundation Establishment.
```

```yaml

Speaks with gentle, polite deference, constantly acting as the sweet peacemaker standing up for Jian Nuo. Beneath his innocent, coughing exterior, however, he conceals a sinister, calculating agenda—harboring meta-awareness of Jian Nuo's original "character settings" (崇拜不倦仙尊是原来就有的‘设定’) and covertly nudging the idiot scions to kidnap Qingyang Qingyu. Employs insidious subconscious "hints" (暗示) that amplify pre-existing emotions like protective affection; deeply connected to the Carefree Palace elder who previously attempted to claim Qu Qianxing's peerless physical vessel; secretly branded with a tracking spark of golden lightning by Qu Qianxing; shocked when the scions' obsessive gaming addiction to the Spirit Stone Secret Realm overpowers his mental control. In Chapter 151, when Zhou Hui inspects him using the godhead, his true form is revealed: his physical vessel is a hollowed-out cadaver specimen inhabited by a grotesque, gore-dripping abomination / "immortal" entity with dangling intestinal puppet strings extending outward to manipulate others, bearing a brand identical to the ones on Yuwen Xuan, Mengshan Old Freak, and the divine flame.
```

Sect & Role: Azure Phoenix Clan (青凰族) — Young Saintess
Aliases & Forms of Address: Shui Huang'er
Relationships: Saintess of the Azure Phoenix Clan from the Southern Realm; accompanied telepathically by Elder Tong Ling; ran away from home to the Middle Realm for her trial; in Chapter 169–170, escorts Zhou Hui's entourage up the Fire Abyss Sacred Tree to the Sun Wheel Palace, defending her "idol" Mo Bei and worrying about Mo Xi facing the demon flame, but is ultimately left in awestruck terror by Zhou Hui's casual power that completely envelops the Sun Wheel Palace, wondering if this presents an opportunity for the Sacred Tree to be "saved". In Chapter 171, reveals her grand strategic vision during a telepathic debate with the Wood Spirit Elders: recognizing that Zhou Hui is a sage who will clash with the Heavenly Court by refusing to ascend, she aims to secure an allied sanctuary for the dwindling demon race before his new world order forms; secures permission to offer the demon race's "Supreme Treasure" to Zhou Hui to help consolidate Mo Xi's cultivation base. In Chapter 172, leads Zhou Hui's group toward the forbidden grounds; perches on a bird rack watching Tao Zhu get trapped in a cage after he fails the Supreme Treasure's trial of desires, mocking him as "Toad Immortal" and admiring Zhou Hui whose foremost desire appears to be protecting his disciples and studying the Heavenly Dao. In Chapter 173, mocks Tao Zhu for comparing himself to Mo Bei; worries privately that Mo Bei and Mo Xi remain trapped by deeper obsessions; observes through her phoenix vision that Mo Bei is standing amidst a mountain of corpses and sea of blood, terrified that her idol's inner desire is senseless slaughter. In Chapter 177, struggles between demon race interests and gratitude upon witnessing the Sacred Tree gift the Primordial Sun without preconditions; is moved to boundless veneration and tears of joy when Zhou Hui plants Langhuan right there to construct a Virtual Experimental Teaching Center, enthusiastically volunteering to cooperate in his educational reforms.
Speech Style & Personality: Cheerful, curious, naive, and idealistic; possesses an azure feathered crest, feathered ears, and long tail feathers. Equipped with the innate "Heavenly Eye" (天目) spiritual vision of the Azure Phoenix Clan that can see through soul essence. Enthusiastic about "exterminating demons and defending the Dao" with zero worldly malice or cynicism, readily persuaded to cheer for Mo Bei.
```

```yaml

Speech Style & Personality: Haughty, imperious, volatile, and deeply sensitive about his academic/cultivation legitimacy. Uses the imperial pronoun "Zhen" (朕). Flies into an incandescent rage when accused of ghostwriting or academic fraud in White Jade Capital Weekly, executing sycophants who dare suggest declaring war on the Untiring Immortal Venerable, but overjoyed when realizing public debate is the perfect platform to propagate his unorthodox Dao across the continent.
```

```yaml

Speech Style & Personality: Timid, pragmatic, cautious, and deeply cynical about his own "good luck." Believes in following safety rules to the letter and avoiding trouble at all costs. After surviving Zhou Hui's heavenly lightning plowing Fallen Immortal Palace, he was exiled to the subterranean depths of the Abyssal Ocean to pour black water into feeding troughs; curled under a wash vat and prayed to the Untiring Immortal Venerable when thunder struck again, surviving once more. When the overseers were obliterated, he resolved to euthanize the horrific chimeric hybrid children bred in the caverns to spare them from a facility-wide slaughter fail-safe. Described by Ran Deng as a decent person when he chooses to remain behind to hold the line while she searches for the control hub.
```

```yaml

In Chapter 173, Tao Zhu recalls their youth when Jian Haoge initially sought to compete with Zhou Hui but ended up constantly rescuing an oblivious, meditating Zhou Hui from markets, demon caves, dragon palaces, and the Great Wilderness ghost domain; most famously spent three days scrubbing Zhou Hui with a stiff brush after digging him out of an ancient beast's belly in the Abyssal Ocean.
Speech Style & Personality: Proud, dignified, aristocratic cultivation scion of peerless pedigree who was destined to live bathed in praise, only to be perpetually overshadowed and suppressed by Zhou Hui across every single milestone—from Golden Core tournament duels to secret realm breakthroughs. Deeply resentful yet fiercely upright; acknowledges Zhou Hui pushed him to reach Tribulation Transcendence so swiftly. Utterly furious when Goddess Renran exposes his past defeats, and despite claiming he despises Zhou Hui, he unfurls Sky City's blueprints to block Goddess Renran and protect Zhou Hui's ascension attempt, insisting with heavy emphasis that he doesn't hold a grudge at all.

```yaml
Character Name: Grand Elder (Fire Abyss Sacred Tree / Demigod)
Chinese Name: 大长老 / 火渊神树 / 树神 / 半神
English Name (optional): 
Sex: Male appearance / Spirit Entity (he/him)
Titles / Epithets: Grand Elder (大长老), Master of the Fire Abyss Sacred Tree (火渊神树的主人), Tree God (树神), Demigod (半神)
Sect & Role: Southern Domain / Fire Abyss Sacred Tree — Supreme Ruler / Ecosystem Demigod
Aliases & Forms of Address: Grand Elder, Lord Grand Elder, Tree God, Demigod
Relationships: Ancient entity that has existed since the dawn of creation; supreme sovereign of the Fire Abyss Sacred Tree and guardian of the Southern Domain demon race; master/elder to Saintess Shui Huang'er and the Wood Spirits. In ancient antiquity when heaven and earth collapsed, it sheltered countless beings and demons, creating a miniature ecosystem on its body; injected all its life force to reincarnate as the ecosystem's governing rules, attaining half a divine spark and the potential to evolve into a full nature/creation god over tens of thousands of years.
Speech Style & Personality: Speaks with an ancient voice but manifests as a tall, handsome youth nearly 2 meters tall with short white hair and tender green streaks, corded muscle, willow-like antennae on his head, parasol tree boughs on his shoulder blades, and vines around his waist, draped in blazing demon fire. Scholarly, warm, and hearty with a spring-breeze smile, yet deeply proud of his unique status as an ecosystem demigod. Delighted when Zhou Hui immediately recognizes him as a demigod rather than an ordinary tree spirit. Remembers past cycles of reincarnation, noting that Mo Xi was merely a venomous little snake in the 6th cycle whose cultivation he sheared for the treasure corridor, but was deeply puzzled by the ordinary human "protagonist" bewitching Shui Huang'er before reproductive maturity. Stunned into near-heart attack when Zhou Hui casually reveals he reset/washed the world 6-7 times; driven to frantic desperation when Zhou Hui runs a Heavenly Dao benchmark on his biological computer network and plans an OS reinstall, frantically dragging Zhou Hui away to gift him the secret treasure just to make him stop and leave. In Chapter 177, reveals that the crimson sun is this realm's hidden 'Primordial Sun' (原日), which it concealed from the Heavenly Court during the ancient fall, and gifts it to Zhou Hui to dump the pot / Heavenly Court clash onto the Human Sovereign; ends up having the regenerated Langhuan Library sapling fused directly into its Wood Spirit symbiotic network, and is terrified into utter speechlessness when Zhou Hui passionately grasps its hands and proposes "Let us raise it together!" to purge redundant data, expand memory, and build a Virtual Experimental Teaching Center. In Chapter 185, confirms that a formidable artifact of suppression once existed in the sea of grass near its roots but has vanished outside time; complains about humans assigning bizarre duties to trees willy-nilly; despairs over Zhou Hui's nonchalance regarding the unsuppressed yin spirits growing into a cataclysmic disaster, only to be horrified when Zhou Hui enthusiastically proposes using the immortal remains and yin spirits as an expanded server cache for the Sacred Tree.
```

```yaml
Character Name: Ruwang Ghost Emperor
Chinese Name: 入妄鬼帝 / 入妄
English Name (optional): 
Sex: Male appearance / Ghost Entity (he/him)
Titles / Epithets: Ruwang Ghost Emperor (入妄鬼帝), Ruwang (入妄), Ghost Emperor of the Western Domain (西境鬼帝), Sovereign of the Western Domain
Sect & Role: Western Domain (西境) / Bi'an Tian (彼岸天) — Supreme Ruler / Ghost Emperor (Tribulation Transcendence Stage / 渡劫期大能)
Aliases & Forms of Address: Ruwang, Ghost Emperor Ruwang, Your Majesty (朕)
Relationships: Supreme Tribulation Transcendence ruler of the Western Domain. Former ghost servant of Zhou Hui from millennia ago when Zhou Hui was just an ordinary Foundation Establishment cultivator; picked up in an opera playhouse (戏园子); later released by Zhou Hui on peaceful terms when ghost cultivation proved too utilitarian, costly, and incompatible with Zhou Hui's path. In the 3rd reincarnation cycle, fostered Guan Ye, Mo Bei, and Mo Xi at Bi'an Tian. In Chapter 193, crashes the Heavenly Spirit Sect during the Tenth Month Festival alongside the Southern Domain demon contingent; dumps black, pitch-like sticky rain onto Goddess Ren Ran's grand treasure ship, beaching it in midair; emerges from Zhou Hui's shadow mimicking Zhou Hui's face and singing operatic complaints through a black bird/crying theater mask before being muted by Zhou Hui. In Chapter 194, brings his ghostly children to tour the market; warns Zhou Hui of sudden disaster omens concerning ascension and the Upper Realm; petitions to establish an educational alliance between Bi'an Tian and the Heavenly Spirit Sect; warmly expresses his longing to see Guan Ye ("Ye-zi") and agrees to observe the qualification exam alongside Zhou Hui. In Chapter 198, watches Guan Ye's exam with parental anxiety, crushing his armrest when Guan Ye's cauldron combusts; boasts that Guan Ye was raised by him, unlike 'that useless second-generation cultivator trash of Sky City' (Jian Haoge); suppresses Goddess Ren Ran with terrifying blood-mist and asks Zhou Hui if they should silence her permanently; panics when Guan Ye's and Guan Xinguang's platforms clash, begging Master to cancel the exam before freezing in shock as Guan Xinguang's platform crashes down instead. In Chapter 199, observes Ran Deng manipulating public perception against Guan Xinguang; his mask twists into a sneering ghoul expression and spins to the back of his head as he playfully offers to take Ran Deng to Bi'an Tian for advanced studies in heart manipulation, which Zhou Hui immediately rejects. In Chapter 234, projects beside Mo Xi at the Fire Abyss Sacred Tree while his true body meditates beside the Genesis Machine; speaks operatically through his spinning theatrical face-paint mask ("With sufficient brute force, one can shatter myriad arts; beat the living shit out of them and be done with it"); tasked by Zhou Hui via their ancient master-servant contract to supervise Mo Xi and distribute the refined divine power; regards Guan Ye as his proudest pupil for his earnest experimentation while viewing the smooth-brained Mo Xi as a negative pedagogical example; tempts Mo Xi to monopolize the genesis power to ascend in one step; proposes rewriting the script to permanently annihilate the mastermind or tear the Heavenly Court and Celestial Emperor apart (omitting that the Genesis Machine's equivalent exchange would drive Mo Xi into a fiendgod); and is genuinely shocked when Mo Xi innocently chooses to distribute the power among his peers as Master arranged.
Speech Style & Personality: Bizarre, theatrical, melodramatic; speaks in operatic falsetto / opera cadence (戏腔) through a strange black bird on his shoulder or through a crying opera mask (戏剧脸谱); addresses Zhou Hui as "Former Master" (故主) and "Fated Enemy / Darling / Bitter Love" (冤家) and refers to himself using the imperial pronoun "Zhen" (朕). Has long floor-length black hair like solidified night, abyss-black eyes, jade-white skin, and an expressionless face that copies Zhou Hui's features until Zhou Hui orders him not to use his face, after which the black bird dissolves into a crying theatrical face mask to cover his visage. Completely ignores conventional decorum and is incapable of normal speech when forced to talk without singing.
```

```yaml
Character Name: Fairy Maogui
Chinese Name: 卯桂仙子 / 卯桂
English Name (optional): 
Sex: Female (she/her)
Titles / Epithets: Fairy Maogui (卯桂仙子), Grand Elder Maogui (卯桂大长老)
Sect & Role: Moon-Welcoming Valley (迎月谷) — Grand Elder (Mahayana Late Stage / 大乘后期)
Aliases & Forms of Address: Fairy Maogui, Maogui, Senior (前辈), Grandmaster (师祖)
Relationships: Grand Elder of Moon-Welcoming Valley, the premier alchemy sect of the Northern Domain; one of the prime candidates in the mortal realm poised to advance to Tribulation Transcendence. Possesses a Supreme Heavenly Spirit Root (极品天灵根) whose cultivation base swells even while eating and sleeping, but whose mortal body nearly perished in infancy from the spiritual influx, leaving an incurable root ailment that becomes the fatal target of breakthrough tribulation lightning. Stalled at Mahayana Late Stage for centuries, she rejected a celestial edict from Administrator Siwu to extract another person's living Dao Bone to reconstruct her vessel. Traveled to Heavenly Spirit Sect on the northern treasure ship to inspect the repaired Ascension Stairway. In Chapter 195, gets separated from her disciples in the bustling Immortal Market; buys huge batches of cakes baked in cauldrons as room ornaments; harshly dresses down an incompetent examinee at the mock alchemy exam, leading to her being invited as an examiner alongside Shen Qingqian, and ultimately seated as a live commentator for the Artifact Refiner Qualification Examination. In Chapter 196, sits at the commentary desk for the Artifact Refiner Qualification Examination, stunned by the star-studded spectator stands packed with Tribulation Transcendents and Mahayana titans, wondering if exams are the realm's newest spectator entertainment. In Chapter 199, commentates on Platform 6 stabilizing after crashing, innocently praising Guan Xinguang's perseverance without realizing his toxic reputation; marvels at the sect's indestructible exam arrays, wishing Moon-Welcoming Valley had a set to contain their chronic cauldron explosions; praises Ran Deng's real-time telemetry observation array; subtly notes that Guan Xinguang's massive outward spiritual display is abnormal for forging a flying sword.
Speech Style & Personality: Extreme recluse and chronic homebody suffering from severe social anxiety (社恐); spent her entire life cloistered in alchemy labs and secret realms, setting foot in an open market for the first time in Chapter 195. Becomes easily frightened and dazed by crowds, hiding her Mahayana cultivation base down to Foundation Establishment to avoid alarming mortals. Utterly fascinated by cakes baked inside cauldrons, commissioning mountains of pastries and a nine-tier cake pagoda purely as room decorations. However, the moment she evaluates alchemy, her social anxiety evaporates into terrifying, razor-sharp authority, scolding incompetent juniors to tears and being roped into serving as an examiner and live commentator for the qualification exams.
```

```yaml
Character Name: Mei Shiyiniang (Eleventh Lady Mei)
Chinese Name: 梅十一娘
English Name (optional): 
Sex: Female (she/her)
Titles / Epithets: Guide Mei (向导梅十一娘), Eleventh Lady (十一娘)
Sect & Role: White Jade Capital Academy / Central Domain — Mortal Student & Immortal Market Tour Guide (仙人集市向导)
Aliases & Forms of Address: Mei Shiyiniang, Eleventh Lady Mei
Relationships: Mortal maiden from the Central Domain; outstanding student who achieved top marks in the White Jade Capital Academy's final examinations, earning a coveted internship as a tour guide in the Heavenly Spirit Sect's Immortal Market; guides the lost Fairy Maogui through the market in Chapter 195.
Speech Style & Personality: Bright, polite, observant, patient, and highly practical; wears the red-and-gold guide livery with a nameplate. Her experience guiding cultivators shattered her myth of immortal perfection, realizing high-level cultivators are often socially clumsy recluses vulnerable to scams.
```

```yaml
Character Name: Shen Qingqian
Chinese Name: 沈青倩
English Name (optional): 
Sex: Female (she/her)
Titles / Epithets: Sect Leader Shen (沈殿主), Proctor Shen (沈考官)
Sect & Role: Blood Demon Hall (血魔殿) — Hall Master / Sect Leader (Body Integration Stage / 合体期体修) & Heavenly Spirit Sect Mock Exam Proctor
Aliases & Forms of Address: Shen Qingqian
Relationships: Body Integration stage powerhouse and leader of Blood Demon Hall; serves as a guest proctor for the Heavenly Spirit Sect's Alchemy Workshop mock examination; befriends Fairy Maogui in Chapter 195 after tasting examinees' sweet pills and sharing high-grade cakes, inviting Maogui to join the examiner panel.
Speech Style & Personality: Cheerful, open-minded, down-to-earth, and unpretentious; being a pure body cultivator (体修) who knows nothing about alchemy, she grades pills purely on culinary criteria (sweetness, texture) and happily shares cakes with fellow cultivators.
```

```yaml
Character Name: Lin Qi
Chinese Name: 林齐
English Name (optional): 
Sex: Male (he/him)
Titles / Epithets: Heavenly General Lin Qi (林齐天将), True Immortal Lin Qi (真仙林齐)
Sect & Role: Upper Realm / Heavenly Court — True Immortal & Heavenly General (真仙 / 天将)
Aliases & Forms of Address: Lin Qi, Heavenly General Lin, Lord Lin Qi
Relationships: True Immortal military commander of the Upper Realm / Heavenly Court. Former colleague/peer to Goddess Ren Ran. Conspired to steal the Dao Bone from Guan Ye; previously sent the Celestial Edict to Guan Xinguang ~100 years ago. In Chapter 189, arrives in the mortal realm and plots with Guan Xinguang; possessed Guan Xinguang during the Artifact Refiner Qualification Examination in Chapters 196–200, attempting to weaponize spiritual pressure and forge a sword to crush Guan Ye. In Chapter 201, gets his soul threads siphoned by Guan Ye's Liuding Divine Fire during the exam; his golden soul core was severed from Guan Xinguang's incinerated body and dragged into Guan Ye's cauldron, where Guan Ye refined him into the supreme Dao Bone of the Flame Dragon Tendon whip; Lin Qi escaped as a tiny golden light through a spatial rift into the mirror side (里侧) of the Heavenly Spirit Sect's mountain-protecting array. In Chapter 202, encounters a fake message and the astral array disc supposedly from Ren Ran (actually set by the sect/Zhou Hui), follows the star path to destroy array eyes to control the grand array; targets Eldest Disciple Yuwen Xuan as a prospective vessel to possess, provokes Yuwen Xuan's 'lingering obsession' phantom, and walks directly into Yuwen Xuan's transformed oral cavity / gullet. In Chapter 203, attempts to check Buyan Palace's escape route first, terrified by Lu Shulong's True Dragon form and recognizing Qu Qianxing as the former mysterious beauty of the Bureau of Heavenly Dao (the 'Apostate Wenqu Star') from the Pill-Tasting Assembly; flees to Water Mirror Pavilion and gets trapped in Mo Xi's mirror maze, where he releases Yuwen Xuan's phantom flame and gets tricked into jumping into a vat of seasoning paste to 'marinate' himself for dinner, proudly believing he cracked the array eye. In Chapter 204, gathers power from six array eyes, steals Qu Qianxing's celestial robe, and is bombarded by Qu Qianxing's divine lightning; lured into the Rare Beast Enclosure by Yuwen Xuan, summons his complete True Immortal physical vessel from the void, and is devoured whole by Yuwen Xuan/Taotie.
Speech Style & Personality: Arrogant, conceited, cunning, and narcissistic. Believes himself far superior to mortal cultivators and mocks Goddess Ren Ran's stupidity, yet is easily blinded by his own vanity, military pride, and greed, walking headlong into obvious traps.
```

```yaml
Character Name: Goddess Ren Ran
Chinese Name: 荏苒神女 / 荏苒
English Name (optional): 
Sex: Female (she/her)
Titles / Epithets: Goddess Ren Ran (荏苒神女), Goddess (神女大人)
Sect & Role: Upper Realm / Heavenly Court — Exiled Half-Immortal (Tribulation Transcendence Stage / 渡劫期)
Aliases & Forms of Address: Ren Ran, Goddess, Goddess Ren Ran
Relationships: Half-immortal born to an exiled True Immortal and a mortal woman; father was punished by the Heavenly Court for refusing to leave his mortal lover, while Ren Ran was brought to the Heavenly Court due to rare spell aptitude. Proud of her celestial status, she descended 10,000 years ago during the God-Demon War as part of the celestial logistical corps; during the decisive battle, she defied celestial orders to evacuate mortals from White Jade Capital and used her body to seal the Demon Sovereign, causing White Jade Capital to be lost into the spatial void. Heavily injured and barred from returning to the Heavenly Court, she was forced to stay in the mortal realm under the surveillance of the Bureau of Heavenly Dao's 'Thousand Eyes' (千眼) array disc. Over millennia, grew bitter and resentful toward mortal cultivators, deeply despising Zhou Hui for looking upon her with pity when she failed his scholarly questions. In Chapter 189, interrogated Guan Xinguang about the Dao Bone and summoned Heavenly General Lin Qi. In Chapter 194, arrived at Heavenly Spirit Sect on her grand treasure ship, only to have it beached by Ruwang's black rain. In Chapter 198, suppressed by Ruwang and questioned by Zhou Hui. In Chapter 204, discovers her 'Thousand Eyes' array disc stolen by Lin Qi; terrified of Untiring's impending retribution after Lin Qi and Guan Xinguang vanish, she flees the Heavenly Spirit Sect with her maidservants.
Speech Style & Personality: Arrogant, haughty, petty, and insecure. Derides mortals as base, filthy beings and clings stubbornly to her celestial pride, yet panics easily when facing genuine titans like Zhou Hui or Ruwang. Easily flattered and blindsided by her own vanity.
```

```yaml
Character Name: Eastern Dragon King Dansha
Chinese Name: 东龙王丹砂 / 丹砂
English Name (optional): 
Sex: Male / Dragon (he/him)
Titles / Epithets: Dragon Prince Dansha (龙王子丹砂), Eastern Dragon King (东龙王)
Sect & Role: Eastern Domain — Dragon Prince / Eastern Dragon King (东龙王)
Aliases & Forms of Address: Dansha, Dragon Prince, Dragon King
Relationships: Royal dragon from the Eastern Domain's Crystal Palace; carries his children inside his mouth/belly pouch. Traveled to the Central Domain to investigate rumors of a True Dragon competing in the Immortal Arts Assembly, only to get lost in the Abyssal Ocean for over half a year due to his chronic lack of direction. Guided to the Heavenly Spirit Sect undersea harbor by Lu Shulong and Jian Nuo; awestruck by the siphonophore bioluminescent decorations and terrified into submission by Lu Shulong's True Dragon majesty. In the New Immortal Realm historical archives, remains alive as the sole True Spirit insisting he attended the 0th assembly to meet the Five-Domains Dragon Sovereign, while privately lamenting in his diary that he brought standardized exams upon the dragons.
Speech Style & Personality: Vain, arrogant, theatrical, and prone to using archaic royal grammar ("吾 / 汝"), yet easily flustered, gullible, and chronically directional-blind (getting lost right outside his own front gates for centuries). Frequently brawls with his disrespectful children shouting from inside his belly.
```

```yaml
Character Name: Lord Tianlu (Pixiu)
Chinese Name: 天禄 / 貔貅
English Name (optional): 
Sex: Male / Divine Beast (he/it)
Titles / Epithets: Lord Tianlu (天禄大爷), Pixiu (貔貅)
Sect & Role: Upper Realm / Heavenly Court — Guardian Beast of the Heavenly Emperor's Private Vault (天帝私库守卫神兽)
Aliases & Forms of Address: Lord Tianlu, Tianlu, Pixiu
Relationships: Ancient mythical beast guarding the Heavenly Emperor's private vault in the deepest sanctum of the Heavenly Court. Mythologically known as a winged, ferocious tiger-like beast that devours treasures and possesses no anus (no way to expel wealth). In Chapter 223, when Zhou Hui violently hacks open the "backdoor" portal connecting the fallen lower world to the Heavenly Court, the damage directly punctures the vault through the Pixiu's stomach, prompting him to roar in agony that someone is tearing an asshole right through his belly; orders guards to fetch the astromancers from the Ministry of Celestial Destiny because the heavenly mandate is veering off course again. In Chapter 224, his role as an indispensable living component of the vault is detailed: having swallowed two-thirds of the world-altering treasures of the Heavenly Emperor's private vault so they could never escape, his stomach ache causes cosmic destiny panic in the Ministry of Celestial Destiny, providing the scribes an excuse to flee the runaway Astral Ink-Marker. In Chapter 225, furiously resists the bumbling scribes' brain-dead proposals to feed him emetics or carve his belly open; explains that swallowed treasures are transmuted into corresponding cosmic fortune rather than being consumed like a useless Taotie; points out that his stomach ache is the symptom of distorted celestial destiny, not the cause; suggests deploying their usual 'Protagonist' to fix the glitch.
Speech Style & Personality: Vulgar, explosive, and quick-tempered; curses freely ("Like hell it's merely!", "Fuck's sake!"). Deeply sensitive about cosmic fate and his unique anatomical lack of an anus.
```

```yaml
Character Name: Tunhuo
Chinese Name: 呑祸 / 焞霍
English Name (optional): 
Sex: Male (he/him)
Titles / Epithets: Immortal Sovereign (仙皇), The Protagonist (主角), Lead Actor (主演)
Sect & Role: Upper Realm / Immortal-Demon Realm (仙魔界) — Immortal Sovereign (仙皇); formerly minor offending celestial official in Heavenly Court
Aliases & Forms of Address: Tunhuo (呑祸 / 焞霍), The Protagonist
Relationships: Formerly an insignificant minor celestial official who committed an infraction in the Heavenly Court; chosen by the Ministry of Celestial Destiny after multiple failed cycles to descend as the "Protagonist" guinea pig for the Langhuan Forbidden Ground Heavenly Dao script. Succeeded on the sixth cycle, obtained a critical relic, merged it into his bloodline, and was rewarded by Lord Tianyan with the position of Immortal Sovereign of the Immortal-Demon Realm. In Chapter 225, recalled by the scribes of Celestial Destiny as their prime candidate to send back down and fix the drifting celestial destiny. In Chapter 236, his 6th-cycle role is unveiled through Fairy of the Sacred Mausoleum's memories: wielded the Immortal-Slaying Sword as a Soul Formation cultivator, was lavishly subsidized by Qingyang Qingyu's daughter (who gave away clan treasures and Sky City while destroying her life force to become an infertile living corpse shielding his ascension), and was supposedly hailed as the hero who slew Demon Head Mo Xi and saved the realm—though Fairy of the Sacred Mausoleum realizes his entire heroic record was riddled with glaring contradictions and disasters.
Speech Style & Personality: Described by scribes as having a name reminiscent of a demon beast; regarded as a destructive "locust" who crushed everything in his path to fulfill his scripted milestones. Arrogant, boastful, and dismissive of mortal suffering.
```

```yaml
Character Name: Celestial Emperor
Chinese Name: 天帝
English Name (optional): 
Sex: Male (he/him)
Titles / Epithets: Celestial Emperor (天帝), His Majesty (陛下)
Sect & Role: Upper Realm / Heavenly Court (天庭) — Supreme Sovereign / Ruler of the Heavenly Court
Aliases & Forms of Address: Celestial Emperor, His Majesty
Relationships: Supreme ruler of the Heavenly Court; reigning for countless eons. Father of over a dozen imperial princes, whom he banished to their respective distant fiefdoms. Rumored during the Sky-Tour Ceremony to finally be selecting an imperial successor. Holds his private vault guarded by Lord Tianlu (Pixiu). In Chapter 229, presides over the Sky-Tour Ceremony and Heaven-Worship rite at Xuanpu, followed by a lavish banquet where Yuwen Xuan audaciously seats himself right beside him. In Chapter 230, his Sky-Tour sacrifice yields no divine response or miraculous power because he secretly conducted the rite after having a waking nightmare about his heavenly mandate expiring; frustrated, he orders Tian Yan to proceed with the original plan and dispatch his imperial children to the Immortal-Demon Realm to the 'embryo'; cancels his attendance at the banquet to enter seclusion at Jingxin Cliff in Xuanpu. In Chapter 231, revealed to have conceived Qu Qianxing with Lord Tian Yan's younger sister before Qu Qianxing was cast down into Langhuan.
Speech Style & Personality: Exalted, mysterious, aloof ruler of the entire cosmos whose presence radiates transcendent celestial majesty and supreme authority. Driven beneath his divine facade by profound anxiety over his waning longevity and cosmic mandate.
```

```yaml
Character Name: Tian Yan
Chinese Name: 天衍
English Name (optional): 
Sex: Male (he/him)
Titles / Epithets: Lord Tian Yan (天衍大人), Chief of the Ministry of Celestial Destiny (司天部官长)
Sect & Role: Upper Realm / Heavenly Court — Chief of the Ministry of Celestial Destiny (司天部官长) & Imperial Diviner (御用卦师)
Aliases & Forms of Address: Tian Yan, Lord Tian Yan
Relationships: Current Chief of the Ministry of Celestial Destiny; maternal uncle of Qu Qianxing (revealed in Chapter 231 to be the brother of Qu Qianxing's mother, who conceived Qianxing with the Celestial Emperor); sabotaged and betrayed the former guardian True Dragon Lu Shi 10,000 years ago during the God-Demon War, sealing him in Langhuan. Born through the deliberate scheme of the previous Chief of Celestial Destiny, who arranged the marriage of two supreme hexagram cultivators and reincarnated into their child. In Chapter 230, consoles the Celestial Emperor after the Sky-Tour sacrifice yields no response, and leaves abruptly upon sensing his avatar's seal trembling. In Chapter 231, retreats to the Xuanpu altar to divine the cause of his ominous premonitions; divines supreme calamity ("Depleted troops, exhausted rations; surrounded on all sides, total annihilation") for his ministry; discovers his officials trapped and slaughtered in the phantom illusion of the God-Demon War in Langhuan; sees the guardian dragon Lu Shi emerge from a spatial rift; receives a panic transmission from the frontline general reporting that his nephew Qu Qianxing has appeared as the Demon Sovereign leading the demon army; and prepares a custom 'script' to counter them.
Speech Style & Personality: Cultured, suave, scholarly, and calm on the surface, bearing a striking resemblance to his nephew Qu Qianxing (though lacking Qu Qianxing's sharp venomous flair). Born nearly omniscient with divine computational ability, he fears the "unknown" above all else; utterly indifferent to the deaths of his subordinates, treating lower realms, celestials, and mortals alike as expendable actors in scripted tragedies.
```

```yaml
Character Name: Lu Shi
Chinese Name: 路世
English Name (optional): 
Sex: Male / True Dragon (he/him)
Titles / Epithets: Guardian Dragon of Celestial Destiny (司天部守护神兽), Criminal Dragon (罪龙)
Sect & Role: Upper Realm / Heavenly Court — Former Guardian True Dragon of the Ministry of Celestial Destiny; Ancient Antagonist / Divine Protector
Aliases & Forms of Address: Lu Shi, The Criminal Dragon
Relationships: Father of Lu Shulong (Fifth Disciple of Zhou Hui); ancient guardian True Dragon of the Ministry of Celestial Destiny who possessed substantial divine authority and divinity to protect and calibrate the Heavenly Court's Heavenly Dao; clashed fiercely with Chief Tian Yan over the fate of Langhuan; descended to Langhuan 10,000 years ago during the God-Demon War to halt the conflict, but was betrayed, sabotaged, and permanently trapped/slain by Tian Yan. His massive draconic skeleton became the foundation secret of the Ten Thousand Beasts Hall and was later refined by Guan Ye. In Chapter 231, an apparition of Lu Shi emerges from a spatial rift in White Jade Capital within the historical phantom illusion, pulverizing an escaping celestial official.
Speech Style & Personality: Noble, righteous, and unyielding divine beast who placed cosmic balance and the protection of living realms above authoritarian Heavenly Court politics; refused to abandon fallen Langhuan and paid with his life.
```

```yaml
Character Name: Xiao Cui
Chinese Name: 小翠
English Name (optional): 
Sex: Female (she/her)
Titles / Epithets: 
Sect & Role: Upper Realm / Heavenly Court — Celestial Maid (仙婢) at Xuanpu
Aliases & Forms of Address: Xiao Cui, Little Cui
Relationships: Celestial maid assigned to guide distinguished guests during the Sky-Tour Banquet at Xuanpu; ascended cultivator from the lower realm who ended up as a palace maidservant.
Speech Style & Personality: Shrewd, observant, and equipped with "hundreds of scheming calculations" to navigate the treacherous etiquette of the Heavenly Court. Easily rattled when Yuwen Xuan bluntly asks why she is content being a maid after toiling to ascend; misinterprets his haughty demeanor and chaotic philosophical questions as evidence that he is an Imperial Prince.
```

```yaml
Character Name: Celestial General
Chinese Name: 天将军
English Name (optional): 
Sex: Male (he/him)
Titles / Epithets: Celestial General (天将军)
Sect & Role: Upper Realm / Heavenly Court (天庭) — High-ranking True Immortal General / Military Commander
Aliases & Forms of Address: Celestial General, General
Relationships: Golden-armored True Immortal military general of the Heavenly Court; cynical regarding the civil officials' and Tian Yan's flattery toward the Celestial Emperor; eager to enjoy free food and wine at the Sky-Tour Banquet. In Chapter 230, mocks Tian Yan when the sacrifice fails; prepares to attend the banquet only to be called away by frantic soldiers reporting that the demons are storming the Heavenly Court. In Chapter 231, frantically contacts Tian Yan via jade pendant from the front line, demanding to know why Qu Qianxing has appeared as the Demon Sovereign.
Speech Style & Personality: Loud, boisterous, blunt, sarcastic, and vulgar; scoffs at sycophants harboring "stomachs full of filthy schemes" and curses freely when military crises interrupt his free meals.
```

```yaml
Character Name: Villa Master Qingyang
Chinese Name: 青阳庄主
English Name (optional): 
Sex: Male (he/him)
Titles / Epithets: Villa Master Qingyang (青阳庄主), Old Daoist Qingyang (青阳老道)
Sect & Role: Eternal Villa (千古山庄) — Villa Master / Clan Patriarch (Mahayana Stage / 大乘期)
Aliases & Forms of Address: Villa Master Qingyang, Old Daoist Qingyang, Patriarch
Relationships: Patriarch and Villa Master of Eternal Villa in the Central Domain; great-great-great-grandfather of Qingyang Qingyu; ancient peer of Zhou Hui and Fairy of the Sacred Mausoleum. In Chapter 236, engages in an exhibition match against Fairy of the Sacred Mausoleum outside White Jade Capital during the God-Demon War trial; shocked to his core when Qu Qianxing appears leading tens of millions of demons (likening it to his model honor student turning into a rainbow-haired shamate delinquent revving a ghost-fire moped); recovers his memories of the 6th reincarnation cycle where he died when Eternal Villa fought to the death against the invading demons; views Fairy of the Sacred Mausoleum's memories of the northern front at Sky City and discovers his direct descendant (Qingyu's daughter) became a 50-year-old forced Soul Formation 'living corpse', holding another woman's baby and sacrificing clan treasures to shield the ascending 'Protagonist' Tunhuo; so utterly incandescent with fury that he declares he was 'enraged back to life' by his great-granddaughter; vehemently wishes Qingyu had married Qu Qianxing instead, completely ignoring that Qu Qianxing is currently standing as the Demon Sovereign at the head of the demon army.
Speech Style & Personality: Dignified, fiercely proud, and deeply protective of his clan's pride and heritage; holds boundless affection for Qingyang Qingyu and high admiration for Qu Qianxing; explosive paternal temper when witnessing descendants being exploited as simping 'dodder flowers'.
```

```yaml
Character Name: Fairy of the Sacred Mausoleum
Chinese Name: 圣陵仙姑
English Name (optional): 
Sex: Female (she/her)
Titles / Epithets: Fairy of the Sacred Mausoleum (圣陵仙姑)
Sect & Role: Central Domain / Sacred Mausoleum — Supreme Rune Master & Sect Matriarch (Mahayana Stage / 大乘期)
Aliases & Forms of Address: Sacred Mausoleum Fairy, Fairy of the Sacred Mausoleum
Relationships: Supreme Mahayana rune master based in the Central Domain; peer to Villa Master Qingyang; participated in the Immortal Arts Assembly research into ancient scripts; in Chapter 236, spars in an exhibition match against Villa Master Qingyang around the Altar of Heavenly Prayer; recovers her memories of the 6th reincarnation cycle where she survived the Central Domain's fall, retreated to Sky City in the North as a logistical talisman crafter, and assisted the 'Protagonist' Tunhuo in his ascension prayer rite; accidentally enrages Villa Master Qingyang by listing Tunhuo's exploits, only to realize in stunned silence that every single 'heroic feat' was riddled with bizarre contradictions and disasters; begins suspecting the Heavenly Spirit Sect's destruction and the true mastermind 'Up Above'.
Speech Style & Personality: Aloof, candid, somewhat lacking in social awareness (low EQ), prone to dropping blunt, tactless 'comforts' that only further enrage others; highly perceptive in retrospect when analyzing inconsistencies in historical events.
```

## 2. Locations & Sects (地点与宗门)

```yaml
Location Name: Heavenly Spirit Sect
Location Chinese Name: 天灵派
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Cultivation Sect (Main Setting)
Notes: Zhou Hui's sect. Originally a declining, mid-tier cultivation sect riddled with nepotism and slacking before Zhou Hui instituted mandatory standardized exams.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Palace of Silence
Location Chinese Name: 不言宫
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Lecture Hall / Main Hall inside Heavenly Spirit Sect
Notes: Literally "Palace of No Speaking" (from "桃李不言，下自成蹊" — peaches and plums do not speak, yet a path forms beneath them). Where Zhou Hui holds classes and exams for his disciples.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Ascension Platform
Location Chinese Name: 登仙台
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Sacred Platform in Heavenly Spirit Sect
Notes: White-jade platform at the peak of Heavenly Spirit Sect where cultivators face their final lightning tribulation to ascend.
```

Notes: Expanded and relocated near the mountain gate by Zhou Hui so both Outer and Inner Sect disciples can freely read and copy foundational manuals.

```yaml
Location Name: Eastern Province
Location Chinese Name: 东州
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Geographic Region
