```

```yaml
Location Name: Sky City
Location Chinese Name: 天上城
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Floating Cultivation Metropolis (Late Arcs)
Notes: A major hub of academic and array-based cultivation in the later chapters.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Heavenly Gate
Location Chinese Name: 天门
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Cosmic Boundary / Ascension Gate
Notes: The portal connecting the mortal cultivation world to the upper realm, which vanished for millennia after Zhou Hui's era.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Immortal-Demon Realm
Location Chinese Name: 仙魔界
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Upper Realm
Notes: The higher plane where ascended immortals and demons reside and clash. In Chapter 200, revealed as the collective name for a vast expanse of sub-spaces nestled adjacent to the Heavenly Court connecting to the Celestial Realm, minor worlds, and the Demon Domain. Shifting constantly and inhabited by demons and rogue immortals, it is governed by 'Celestial Emperors' (仙皇) who obey Heavenly Court edicts while enjoying untethered power. Used as a transit hub for ascending cultivators, and where Heavenly General Lin Qi leaves his True Immortal flesh inside a pavilion shielded by 72 formations to eliminate the temporal time lag with lower realms.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Xuanpu (The Hanging Gardens)
Location Chinese Name: 悬圃
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Mythical Celestial Garden / Sacred Peak in the Upper Realm
Notes: Mythical celestial garden complex in the Heavenly Court, historically associated in Chinese mythology with the peaks of Mount Kunlun. Features jewels growing from soil plots, pavilions of gold and jade, a grand sacrificial altar, and cloud pools where the Celestial Emperor hosts the Sky-Tour Banquet. Guarded by the elite Imperial Celestial Guards and protected by a divine beast. In Chapter 229, Yuwen Xuan sneaks through an interdimensional fissure from the primordial spiritual sea into Xuanpu to crash the banquet.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Jingxin Cliff (Purification Cliff)
Location Chinese Name: 净心崖
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Seclusion Sanctum / Sacred Cliff in Xuanpu
Notes: High, tranquil seclusion cliff situated within Xuanpu in the Upper Realm; where the Celestial Emperor enters closed-door seclusion following the failure of the Sky-Tour sacrificial rite in Chapter 230.
```
```

```yaml
Location Name: Heavenly Dao Alliance
Location Chinese Name: 天道盟
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Cultivation Coalition / Inter-Sect Governing League
Notes: The supreme coalition formed by the cultivation world's largest orthodox sects, acting like the United Nations of the cultivation realm to mediate disputes, repel extraterrestrial demons, and manage human/non-human affairs. Ostensibly noble, but riddled with political self-interest.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Mountain Gate Ancient Peach Tree
Location Chinese Name: 天灵派山门 / 万年桃木
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Sect Landmark / Mountain Gate
Notes: Colossal tens-of-thousands-of-years-old peach tree standing at the entrance to Mount Tianling valley that serves as the Heavenly Spirit Sect's mountain gate; blossoms every 300 years and bears fruit every 600 years; during ordinary years resembles a withered giant tree; where Zhou Hui hangs Yuwen Xuan when punishing him.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Abyssal Ocean
Location Chinese Name: 渊海
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Geographic Region / Ocean
Notes: Vast, mysterious primordial sea where oceanic demon beasts, shoals, and islands are situated; featured in sect examination questions.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Hall of Embracing Emptiness
Location Chinese Name: 怀虚殿
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Sect Facility / Guest Banquet Hall in Heavenly Spirit Sect
Notes: Hospitality hall within the Heavenly Spirit Sect where Yuwen Xuan prepared food and wine to receive visiting emissaries like He Yong and Su Xin.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Palace of Carefree Wandering
Location Chinese Name: 逍遥宫
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Major Commercial Trading Guild / Entertainment Network (北境数一数二的大商会)
Notes: Premier mercantile and entertainment guild prominent across the Northern and Southern Domains. Renowned for its rare treasures, elixirs, guildhalls, fine taverns, auction houses, and the decennial Curio Fair (奇珍会). Celebrated with the saying "Above lies the Heavenly Immortal Realm; below sits the Carefree Palace" (上有天仙境，下有逍遥宫). Operates strictly under Alliance rules in orthodox territories.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Palace of Fallen Immortals
Location Chinese Name: 堕仙宫
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Illicit Commercial & Entertainment Den / Front Organization (逍遥宫马甲)
Notes: The shadowy, illicit alter-ego and sockpuppet identity of the Palace of Carefree Wandering operating in the lawless Central Domain. Sells exotic goods, dangerous contraband, and illicit entertainment away from the strict regulations of orthodox sects. Currently hosting an assembly to exploit the newly unearthed secret realm.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Blade-Flying Peak / Blade-Flying Peak Secret Realm
Location Chinese Name: 刃飞峰 / 刃飞峰秘境
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Mountain Peak / Ancient Secret Realm
Notes: An ancient secret realm capped below the Golden Core stage; in the original novel's plot script, the future protagonist was destined to be trapped here alongside youngest junior sister Mo Bei and develop romantic feelings.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Severing-Heaven Cliff
Location Chinese Name: 断天涯
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Dangerous Cultivation Landmark / Cliff in the Abyssal Ocean
Notes: Precipitous sea cliff in the Abyssal Ocean where True Immortal cultivation heritage was inscribed; where Zhou Hui sat for 999 days to memorize the True Immortal art during his Golden Core days.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Yuqing Sect
Location Chinese Name: 玉清派
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Ancient Orthodox Cultivation Sect
Notes: Major cultivation sect whose elder attempted to humiliate and eliminate young Golden Core Zhou Hui by tricking him into studying the incomprehensible True Immortal art on Severing-Heaven Cliff.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Meditation Hall
Location Chinese Name: 静心堂
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Sect Facility / Meditation Chamber in Heavenly Spirit Sect
Notes: Dedicated quiet meditation hall within the Heavenly Spirit Sect situated directly above a thick spiritual stone vein, providing concentrated ambient spiritual energy to nourish disciples suffering from spiritual depletion or needing breakthrough recovery; where Mo Xi recuperated after exhausting his energy in Exam 1.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Mount Tianling Spirit Stone Vein
Location Chinese Name: 天灵山灵矿
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Subterranean Geological Landmark / Spirit Stone Mine
Notes: Massive subterranean spiritual vein running beneath Mount Tianling; features a subterranean cavern where pure spirit stones form an exposed shimmering "river" that can be gathered without even mining; gifted in its entirety by Zhou Hui to Mo Xi to persuade him to abandon his harmful pure Yin furnace cultivation technique.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Grand Curio Fair / Treasure Gathering
Location Chinese Name: 珍宝会
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Premier Commercial Flagship Auction / Black-Market Gathering
Notes: The largest flagship auction event hosted by the Palace of Carefree Wandering (逍遥宫), where top-tier treasures, artifacts, and living cultivators as "furnaces" were auctioned to high-level figures. Shattered and cleaved in half with heavenly lightning by Zhou Hui upon reaching Tribulation Transcendence to avenge human decency and settle his youthful grievance.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Thousand Harmony Gang / Myriad Coalition Gang
Location Chinese Name: 万合帮
Location English Name (optional): Thousand Harmony Gang
Type: Rogue Cultivator Alliance / Sect in the Central Domain (散仙帮派联盟)
Notes: Coalition and guild formed by rogue cultivators in the Central Domain; known as the "Number One Gang of the Central Domain" (中境第一大帮). Led by Sect Leader Zhang Hongjing. Features low entry barriers, minimal commitments, and high autonomy, but is in reality a loose collective where leaders have all obligations and no perks.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Thousand Beasts Hall / Myriad Beasts Hall
Location Chinese Name: 万兽堂
Location English Name (optional): Thousand Beasts Hall
Type: Beast-Taming Cultivation Sect in the Central Domain (御兽门派)
Notes: Prominent sect in the Central Domain specializing in beast taming, possessing unique ancestral techniques and generous benefits, ideal for cultivators pursuing the beast-tamer path. Possesses rigid internal hierarchy where resources and secret realm slots are monopolized by elite seeds and corrupt managers like Bai Tuo.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Abyssal Ocean Society
Location Chinese Name: 渊海会
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Major Commercial Artifact Trade Syndicate in the Central Domain (中境最大商团)
Notes: The Central Domain's largest commercial artifact trade syndicate, secretly owned and operated by Mahayana cultivator Tao Zhu. Specializes in excavating, buying, and brokering ancient relic treasures recovered from the Fiendgod Battlefield in the Abyss Sea. An influential powerhouse that even Carefree Palace treats with utmost courtesy.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Eternal Villa
Location Chinese Name: 千古山庄
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Aristocratic Cultivation Manor / Sect in the Central Domain
Notes: Wealthy, prestigious cultivation manor in the Central Domain with high-grade techniques and elite training environments, demanding high recruitment qualifications and making exit difficult once joined.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Heavenly Book Tower
Location Chinese Name: 天书塔
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Ancient Sacred Landmark / Pagoda
Notes: Tower created 7,000 years ago when a supreme cultivator pinned down a scroll of the Heavenly Book with a pagoda artifact as a great sage ascended; heavily guarded by the Heavenly Dao Alliance with strict entry limits; reveals disparate fragments of the future at enormous spiritual energy costs.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Thought Pavilion
Location Chinese Name: 一念阁
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Miniature Cave-Heaven / Historic Pavilion (小洞天 / 楼阁)
Notes: Exquisite three-story pavilion located at the heart of Xiaoyao Lake inside the Palace of Carefree Wandering. Crafted by the ascended True Immortal Yinian as a self-contained miniature cave-heaven with self-consistent rules. Time inside stands perpetually frozen, surrounded by a serene bamboo grove made of broken swords. Snatched intact by Zhou Hui and turned into a miniature paper model on his desk.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Xiaoyao Lake
Location Chinese Name: 逍遥湖
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Sacred Lake / Central Landmark of Palace of Carefree Wandering
Notes: Massive lake located at the very heart of the Palace of Carefree Wandering in the Eastern Domain; Thought Pavilion sat in its center. Turned into a pool of stagnant dead water when Thought Pavilion was confiscated by Zhou Hui.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Hall of Pure Heart
Location Chinese Name: 清心殿
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Sacred Hall in Palace of Carefree Wandering
Notes: Hall where Carefree Palace ancestral masters reside and where disciples/elders go to seek life-preserving protective talismans.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Water Mirror Pavilion
Location Chinese Name: 水镜楼
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Subterranean Communication Facility in Heavenly Spirit Sect (地下通信设施)
Notes: Subterranean pavilion located inside a karst cavern beneath Mount Tianling; features tiered underground spring pools enchanted by Zhou Hui into "water mirrors" that serve as long-distance video communication relays connecting to disciples and agents across the Five Domains.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Abyss Sea City
Location Chinese Name: 渊海城
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Coastal Metropolis / Commercial Hub in Central Domain (中境重要海港商市)
Notes: Major port city and trading metropolis in the Central Domain where merchants gather from across the Five Domains; site of grand regular auctions and seasonal trade arriving from the Southern Domain.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Ice Eye
Location Chinese Name: 冰眼
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Natural Wonder / Subterranean Spring in Secret Realm (秘境极寒奇观)
Notes: A miraculous natural spring nestled within the secret realm that remains infinitely frigid without ever freezing solid, situated in an artificial microclimate mimicking the scorching Southern Domain. In the original novel script, Mo Bei fell in, suffered heat poison, and was treated by Zhou Hui using its water, inadvertently revealing Zhou Hui's fatal weakness.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Mount Qianxing
Location Chinese Name: 千星山
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Mountain Peak / Secret Realm Sanctuary in Central Domain (秘境驻地)
Notes: Formerly "Nameless Peak" where the ancient secret realm manifested; cleaved flat at the summit by Zhou Hui's casual sword stroke; renamed Mount Qianxing by Heavenly Spirit Sect; enchanted by Zhou Hui as an absolute peace zone where all violence and offensive spells are suppressed.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Qianxing Hall
Location Chinese Name: 千星堂
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Sect Facility / Reception Hall on Mount Qianxing (千星山大厅)
Notes: Exquisite reception hall erected atop Mount Qianxing to administer public entry into the secret realm; crafted by Eldest Senior Sister Ran Deng via paper-cut origami and enlarged with spiritual breath to honor Qu Qianxing's handsome face; adorned with luminous orbs resembling a thousand stars.
```

```yaml
Location Name: East Hedge Garden
Location Chinese Name: 东篱苑
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Elite Academic Society / Archaeological Research Circle (顶级修仙研究学术团体)
Notes: An exclusive research society and academic club composed of the cultivation world's topmost powerhouses at the Tribulation Transcendence and Mahayana stages. Having reached the very summit of cultivation where ordinary advancement ceases, its members obsess over the unknown and spend their lives conducting archaeological excavations in the ancient god-demon battlefield deep within the Abyss Sea. Derided by orthodox politicians like Su Mei as 'a pack of lunatics.' Zhou Hui was once an active member before adopting Yuwen Xuan, which was where he first befriended the Ran Clan.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Yongfeng Empire
Location Chinese Name: 永丰王朝
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Imperial Mortal Dynasty in the Northern Domain (北境最大凡人帝国)
Notes: The largest mortal empire in the world, situated in the Northern Domain. Because the Northern Domain possesses too many rival major sects, none willing to submit to another, the sects propped up a mortal emperor to ensure impartial administration and child-disciple tributary recruitment; equipped the imperial family with mortal-usable defensive treasures.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Xiafei Sect
Location Chinese Name: 霞飞门
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Major Cultivation Sect in the Northern Domain (北境大门派)
Notes: Prominent orthodox cultivation sect in the Northern Domain; ancestral sect of Cheng Ziyi.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Mount Tianling Mountain Range
Location Chinese Name: 天灵山脉
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Mountain Range (Heavenly Spirit Sect Territory)
Notes: The extensive mountain range encompassing Mount Tianling valley; owned entirely by the Heavenly Spirit Sect. Zhou Hui plans to have the new disciples reshape its regional spirit veins and spiritual energy flow to build their own immortal caves.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Natural Chasm Mountain Range
Location Chinese Name: 天堑山脉
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Mountain Range / Geographic Barrier in Central Domain
Notes: Mountain barrier shielding Mount Tianling from the full brunt of the chaotic Central Domain spiritual energy storms.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Retrieval Platform
Location Chinese Name: 觅书台
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Sect Facility / Magical Query Station inside the Library Pavilion (藏书阁检索台)
Notes: The magical book query and retrieval platform inside the Library Pavilion. Disciples write desired book categories and keywords upon it; ink ascends to form flying ink birds as guides and stepped ink brushstroke ladders to transport the disciple to high shelves. The stability and safety of the ink ladder depend entirely on the caller's calligraphy quality (poor handwriting generates jagged, hazardous caterpillar-like ledges).
```

```yaml
Location Name: Shadow Cinema
Location Chinese Name: 残影院
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Sect Facility / Holographic Demonstration Wing in the Library Pavilion (藏书阁多媒体影像区)
Notes: Dedicated audio-visual and holographic instructional wing inside the Library Pavilion where disciples can repeatedly watch phantom projection tutorials left by masters or manifested from Zhou Hui's memories.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Cookhouse
Location Chinese Name: 庖屋
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Sect Facility / Demonstration Kitchen in the Library Pavilion (藏书阁残影院分支)
Notes: Spacious, spotless demonstration kitchen inside the Library Pavilion's Shadow Cinema; features cooking stations where holographic culinary instructors demonstrate recipes that disciples can smell and taste (using specialized testing chopsticks such as Taotie's Tongue, Divine Chef's Tongue, or Mortal's Tongue), as well as modern fast-cook appliance counters.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Beast Enclosure
Location Chinese Name: 兽圜
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Sect Facility / Spirit Beast Pens in Heavenly Spirit Sect (天灵派灵兽圈养区)
Notes: Sect enclosure where spirit beasts and livestock are bred, raised, and managed; source of dairy and culinary ingredients (such as daily spirit beast milk); where Huang Xiao applied to apprentice to monitor food quality.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Spirit Herb Fields
Location Chinese Name: 灵田
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Sect Facility / Agricultural Medicinal Plots in Heavenly Spirit Sect (天灵派灵植种植区)
Notes: Lush agricultural fields and herb gardens within the Mount Tianling valley overseen by the Alchemy Department and Lu Shulong; used for cultivating medicinal flora and vegetables; where apprentice sword cultivators are deployed as human irrigation sprinklers.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Array Hall
Location Chinese Name: 阵法厅
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Sect Facility / Formation Research Hall in Heavenly Spirit Sect (藏书阁阵法部门 / 冉氏驻地)
Notes: Formation and array research department inside the Heavenly Spirit Sect, where Ran Clan disciples study under Ran Deng; site of the Mount Tianling 3D topographical map and the secret "conspiracy" meeting witnessed by Cheng Ziyi.
```
```yaml
Location Name: Tianyi Pavilion / Tianyi Workshop
Location Chinese Name: 天衣阁 / 天衣坊
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Sect Facility / Tailoring & Uniform Logistics Department in Heavenly Spirit Sect (后勤制衣部门)
Notes: Dedicated logistical tailoring workshop in the Heavenly Spirit Sect responsible for producing and issuing seasonal clothing and standardized disciples' uniforms ("school uniforms"); features master tailors and apprentices.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Fengqing City
Location Chinese Name: 风清城
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Major Commercial Metropolis in Southeastern Central Domain (东南重镇)
Notes: Prosperous trade city situated in the southeastern corner of the Central Domain; site of the largest inter-domain teleportation array connecting the Central Domain to external domains; traditionally hosted the annual Winter Solstice Auction due to centuries of blood-paved trade routes converging upon it.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Sovereign Suite
Location Chinese Name: 人皇套房
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Luxury Accommodation / VIP Lodging in Qianxing Secret Realm (秘境浮岛套房)
Notes: Extravagant VIP suite situated in a pavilion atop the floating island of Qianxing Secret Realm, named after the 'Human Emperor' (人皇) in Heavenly Spirit Sect's over-the-top room naming scheme; features a floor-to-ceiling transparent glazed wall overlooking the entire tiered realm landscape and highest-density ambient spiritual energy.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Blood Demon Hall
Location Chinese Name: 血魔殿
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Cultivation Sect / Orthodox Body-Refinement Sect in Southwest Central Domain (中境西南角宗门)
Notes: Despite its ominous, demonic-sounding name and the worldly stereotype of muscle-bound meathead body cultivators, Blood Demon Hall is an orthodox sect where 80-90% of the members are actually ordinary, healthy, pretty girls who love beauty and shopping! To shield female disciples from bizarre external rumors, male disciples like Bai Wubin handle outside diplomatic PR. Quotas to attend major auctions for shopping sprees are fiercely contested annually via martial arena tourneys (打擂台). Located inside a spatial rift along the periphery of the ancient God-Demon ruins, surrounded perpetually by void darkness and defended by formidable defensive formations.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Black Coral (Coral Tree Teleportation Node)
Location Chinese Name: 黑珊瑚 / 珊瑚树
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Spatial Teleportation Artifact / Projection Hub (深海传送法宝)
Notes: A miraculous nine-meter-tall enchanted coral tree refined by Zhou Hui to coordinate Kunpeng passenger pickups. While its skeletal core is black, its outer mantle is pristine white, spreading in bilateral symmetry like a colossal luminous feather that glows in the dark void. Anchored by an enchanted sundial base with concentric time rings and a dual 'Surface' (表) and 'Inner' (里) face. Flipping the dial inverts the spatial perception 180 degrees, safely transporting visitors between the continental plate and the Abyss Sea floor.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Sea-Calming Needle Coral Reef
Location Chinese Name: 定海神针珊瑚礁
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Colossal Oceanic Landmark & Transit Terminal Hub in Abyss Sea (渊海大型渡口)
Notes: A gargantuan natural coral reef in the Abyss Sea that grows uninterrupted from the seabed to the ocean surface, resembling the legendary Sea-Calming Needle (定海神针). Naturally tiered in expansive horizontal terraces, originally used by Zhou Hui as an experimental jellyfish farm, and later financed by Boss Tao Zhu to become the primary multi-species transit hub and ferry terminal for the Tianling Winter Solstice Auction.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Jellyfish Farm
Location Chinese Name: 水母田
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Oceanic Aquaculture Facility in Abyss Sea (深海水产养殖区)
Notes: A vast, stratified underwater farm cultivated by Zhou Hui in the Abyss Sea beneath the Central Domain. Houses astronomical swarms of glowing, chain-linking deep-sea jellyfish that survived the ancient God-Demon War by feeding on demonic qi. Abandoned by Zhou Hui after discovering their primitive biology simply excreted whatever demonic qi they ingested without purifying it; later rediscovered by Yuwen Xuan as an infinite, safe food supply for his ravenous pet Kunpeng A-Da.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Undersea Vacation Palace
Location Chinese Name: 海下行宫
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Outpost / Resort Residence of Blood Demon Hall in Abyss Sea (血魔殿深海行宫)
Notes: An ambitious deep-sea base and leisure retreat planned by Sect Leader Shen Qingqian of Blood Demon Hall near their anchored Black Coral coordinate on the seabed of the Abyss Sea. Overlooks luminous drifting jellyfish chains and serves as both a Kunpeng ferry dock, a disciple trial hunting ground, and an undersea resort palace.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Rare Beast Enclosure / Spirit Beast Pens
Location Chinese Name: 珍兽苑 / 灵兽苑
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Sect Facility / Beast Breeding Grounds in Heavenly Spirit Sect (天灵派后山灵兽圈养区)
Notes: Facility located in the Heavenly Spirit Sect's back mountain near Heaven-Cleaving Chasm where exotic and ferocious beasts are bred and reared; serves as Yuwen Xuan's immortal cave abode and giant personal food pantry. Composed of pocket-dimension scrolls and screens ("scroll-world ranches") housing herds of Kui Bulls, Zhu Yan apes, and giant tortoises. Notorious among disciples as a lethal, unrecorded labor hazard.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Altar of Divine Decrees
Location Chinese Name: 神旨坛
Location English Name (optional): Shenzhi Altar
Type: Cultivation Sect / Prophetic Shrine & Spacetime Chronicle Post
Notes: An ancient and esoteric sect renowned across the realms for celestial divination, astrology, and interpreting cosmic omens; where the eccentric diviner Jiuzhai resides. In Chapter 230, revealed to have originated from the Heavenly Court prior to the God-Demon War, dispatched to the lower realm as Chroniclers to monitor and record spacetime fluctuations. Practiced 'selective recording' to shield local affairs (including Zhou Hui's cycle-resetting antics) from celestial scrutiny.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Qiao Family Village
Location Chinese Name: 乔家村
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Impoverished Farming Settlement near Fengqing City (风清城辖下凡人村落)
Notes: A rural agricultural village populated by mortals and low-tier demi-humans (hybrid beastfolk); defended their ancestral lands against the predatory Zheng Clan's coercive demands to plant Solomon's Seal (huangjing); spearheaded by Village Chief Qiao Linggen to trade their harvest at the Tianling Winter Solstice Auction.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Zheng Clan
Location Chinese Name: 郑家
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Cultivation Clan / Predatory Merchant Family in Fengqing City (风清城修仙家族)
Notes: A ruthless local cultivation clan in Fengqing City that uses economic monopolies, cartel price-fixing, and debt foreclosures to bully surrounding mortal farming settlements into uprooting subsistence crops for lucrative medicinal herbs; attempted to seize Qiao Family Village's ancestral farmland.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Coral Convention Center / Coral Hall
Location Chinese Name: 珊瑚会馆 / 珊瑚行馆
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Grand Commercial Complex & Undersea Exhibition Center in Abyss Sea (渊海深海会馆)
Notes: A colossal mountain-sized coral palace refined and constructed within the Sea-Calming Needle coral reef in the Abyss Sea beneath Mount Tianling; illuminated by glowing jellyfish streetlights and organized with segregated transit bubble corridors and tax-free commodity appraisal offices for the Winter Solstice Auction.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Mount Yunzhong
Location Chinese Name: 云中山
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Mountain / Agricultural Cultivation Territory in Central Domain (中境产粮名山)
Notes: A cultivation region renowned for high-grade agricultural produce, specifically spiritual wheat (灵麦) and spiritual sugar (糖/灵霜); merchants from Mount Yunzhong traveled to the Heavenly Spirit Sect Immortals' Bazaar seeking to establish partnerships or launch finished food products like Rice Cooker Sponge Cakes.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Talent Exchange Center
Location Chinese Name: 人才交流中心
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Commercial Facility / Labor Recruitment Agency in Golden Core Bazaar (金丹市集就业机构)
Notes: A modern-style labor recruitment and employment exchange facility situated across from the Golden Core Auction House in the Heavenly Spirit Sect Immortals' Bazaar; features prominent job postings like urgent recruitment notices for body cultivators to move bricks for piece-rate daily wages.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Yuke Xuan
Location Chinese Name: 羽客轩
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Cultivation Sect / Aristocratic Family Clan in Eastern Domain (东境修仙世家大门派)
Notes: A prominent, large-scale cultivation sect in the Eastern Domain centered around an aristocratic clan, similar to Eternal Villa but vastly larger. Possesses immense aristocratic pride and an aloof self-image of romantic neutrality; while naturally leaning slightly toward the Palace of Carefree Wandering due to shared Eastern Domain roots, they refuse to act as Carefree Palace's lackeys. Traveled to the Tianling Winter Solstice Auction via immortal crane-feather carriage to flaunt their distinguished pedigree.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Blood Demon Hall Bone Palace
Location Chinese Name: 血魔殿骸骨建筑 / 魔物骸骨
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Sect Headquarters / Ancient Archdemon Skeleton in Archdemon Ruins (魔神遗迹空间缝隙)
Notes: The unique architectural complex of Blood Demon Hall, built directly inside the blood-red colossal skeleton of an ancient demon fiend lodged in a spatial rift. Its skull forcefully smashed open the spatial rift; Shen Qingqian's cave dwelling and watchtower sit upon the chewed-off back of the head; the cervical spine houses administrative and functional halls; and the ribcage encloses the central plaza housing the Black Coral tree and surrounding disciple dormitories.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Mountain That Eats People
Location Chinese Name: 会吃人的山 / 骨骸后山
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Mysterious Mountain Landmark / Spatial Hazard behind Blood Demon Hall (血魔殿后山)
Notes: A sheer, rampart-like vertical mountain blocking the rear of the spatial rift directly behind the demon skeleton. Twice as high as the demon's raised skull; where Flame Crystal Flowers grow inside two interconnected caves. Dubbed by Shen Qingqian as the 'mountain that eats people' because any building erected on it or campsite pitched upon its slopes inexplicably vanishes without a trace. Bizarrely immovable even by Body Integration powerhouses.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Huaixu Hall
Location Chinese Name: 怀虚殿
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Sect Assembly & Reception Hall in Heavenly Spirit Sect (天灵派待客大殿)
Notes: A grand reception hall of Heavenly Spirit Sect where external merchant delegations, village chiefs, and contractual partners assemble to meet with sect leadership like Qu Qianxing and Mo Xi.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Kunpeng Undersea Palace / Coral Coronet Traveling Palace
Location Chinese Name: 鲲鹏珊瑚行宫 / 珊瑚花冠
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Mobile Underwater Residence / Traveling Palace on Kunpeng's Head (鲲鹏背部行宫)
Notes: An exquisite open-air traveling palace woven from living coral adorning the head of the Kunpeng A-Da. Designed by Ran Deng and Guan Ye, shielded by an atmospheric bubble array, equipped with a study, meditation room, alchemy room, a hydraulic water circulation spiritual fountain, low bookshelves, and panoramic open railings for observing deep-sea scenery and feeding fish.
```yaml
Location Name: Longmen Town
Location Chinese Name: 龙门镇
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Mortal & Merchant Town in Central Domain (中境城镇)
Notes: A regional township in the Central Domain where local merchants and clans gather; home town of Lou Wu and Mayor Mei Xianyu; closely intertwined with the neighboring Shengmo Sect via clan kinship; entirety of its leadership and merchants colluded to steal cake recipes from the Immortal Bazaar, resulting in a town-wide lockdown and lightning shackles enforced by Heavenly Spirit Sect's coral tree.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Shengmo Sect
Location Chinese Name: 声漠宗
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Regional Cultivation Sect in Central Domain (中境本地门派)
Notes: A local cultivation sect situated near Longmen Town; heavily aligned with the Palace of Carefree Wandering; deeply suspicious and opportunistic; sent disciples to the Immortal Bazaar as moles to report back to Carefree Palace while orchestrating the theft of baking recipes through kinship connections in Longmen Town.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Peerless Alliance
Location Chinese Name: 无双盟
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Cultivation Coalition in Southwest Central Domain (中境西南角宗门联盟)
Notes: An alliance formed during a century-class beast tide by Shen Qingqian's original small sect and neighboring small sects for mutual survival. After the crisis, its Alliance Leader hooked up with Shen Qingqian's senior sister (the Sect Master) and attempted to annex the constituent sects, demanding female elders and managers (including Shen Qingqian) marry his disciples as dowry goods. Shen Qingqian broke away with loyal disciples, fled their pursuit, and eventually founded Blood Demon Hall.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Ancient Teleportation Hall
Location Chinese Name: 古代传送阵大厅
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Ancient Ruin / Teleportation Facility in Abyss Sea (渊海古代遗迹大厅)
Notes: An ancient teleportation assembly hall constructed deep within the Abyss Sea by mortal human emperors during the God-Demon War prior to the extraterrestrial fiends' invasion, designed to rapidly mobilize cultivators to battle. Destroyed during ancient assaults and filled with ancient dust and clean human skeletons; where the colossal Taotie head rolled into after Zhou Hui kicked it through the spatial rift.
```

```yaml
Location Name: White Jade Capital Ruins
Location Chinese Name: 白玉京遗迹 / 白玉京
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Ancient Capital of Cultivators / Fallen Mythological Metropolis (上古修仙之都)
Notes: The legendary ancient metropolis of prehistoric cultivators that vanished during the ancient God-Demon War tens of thousands of years ago; discovered by Zhou Hui and Yuwen Xuan at the terminus of the white jade corridor extending from the ancient teleportation hall in the Abyss Sea. An exquisite, breathtaking celestial city sprawling below the fractured precipice.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Twelve Pavilions and Five Cities
Location Chinese Name: 十二楼五城
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Landmark Architecture / Urban Layout of White Jade Capital (白玉京城郭布局)
Notes: The legendary architectural configuration of White Jade Capital. Twelve colossal sky-scraping pavilions tower along the perimeter, connected by suspended aerial walkways that bind the five inner city bastions into a unified celestial metropolis.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Thirteenth Pavilion
Location Chinese Name: 十三楼
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Grand Gate / Imperial Court of White Jade Capital (白玉京城门与仙人王庭)
Notes: According to ancient mortal legends, a mysterious Thirteenth Pavilion situated straight down the Main Avenue of White Jade Capital, serving as the court of the immortals and the headquarters of the Asking Dao Alliance. In reality, it manifests as the colossal Grand Gate of White Jade Capital, inscribed with True Immortal Script and guarded by an ancient spectral army that requires a trial of strength to reach.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Asking Dao Alliance
Location Chinese Name: 问道盟
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Prehistoric Cultivation Coalition / Predecessor of the Heavenly Dao Alliance (上古修仙联盟)
Notes: The ancient inter-sect governing coalition of the cultivation world during the era of White Jade Capital before the God-Demon War. The modern Heavenly Dao Alliance inherited its organizational framework from the Asking Dao Alliance, though changing "Asking" (问道) to "Heavenly" (天道) radically altered its philosophical ethos.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Demonic Dao Alliance / Demonic Syndicate
Location Chinese Name: 黑道盟
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Humorous Cynical Satire / Demonic Criminal Syndicate (黑化天道盟)
Notes: Shen Qingqian's panicked label for what the Heavenly Dao Alliance would turn into if word of White Jade Capital's discovery ever leaked out—an underworld cartel of ruthless, plundering demonic sects slaughtering everyone to seize ancient divine treasures.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Black Abyss Lake
Location Chinese Name: 黑渊湖
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Dangerous Ancient Ruin / Demonic Lake in Abyss Sea (渊海魔神遗迹危险水域)
Notes: A perilous lake located within the ancient Fiendgod battlefield ruins of the Abyss Sea. Surrounding its shores are rich deposits of Foundation Establishment artifact refinement materials, but due to crushing demonic qi, only Nascent Soul cultivators and above can withstand the environment. Because Foundation Establishment materials are like "chicken ribs" to Nascent Soul powerhouses, nobody ventures there until Zhou Hui designs quests to lure cultivators into harvesting materials and fueling White Jade Capital.
```

```yaml
Location Name: White Jade Capital Academy / Academy of White Jade Capital
Location Chinese Name: 白玉京学宫 / 学宫
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Ancient Educational & Cultivation Palace Complex in White Jade Capital (上古修仙学宫)
Notes: The prestigious academy located in the foremost of White Jade Capital's Five Cities; features magnificent halls, sprawling plazas, elegant student quarters, and the rear Stele Forest (碑林) containing the Student Stele (生徒碑). In antiquity, it gathered elite scholars across the realm, researched 'Entering the Dao through Learning' and 'Entering the Dao through Teaching', and required the accumulation of Scholar's Qi (才气) alongside spiritual energy.
```

```yaml
Location Name: White Jade Capital Preparatory School
Location Chinese Name: 白玉京预备学堂
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Primary School / Grassroots Educational Facility for Mortal Children (天灵派预备学堂)
Notes: A 6-year primary school established near the Kunpeng transit terminal by Heavenly Spirit Sect for mortal children aged seven and older hailing from cooperative partner villages and towns; operates four shichen (8 hours) a day with 6 classes daily; offers Night Literacy Classes (扫盲班) for adult villagers in the evening. Outstanding graduates are eligible for advanced studies and priority hiring at the Immortals' Bazaar and Heavenly Spirit Sect.
```

```yaml
Location Name: White Jade Capital Central Domain Northwest Sixth School
Location Chinese Name: 白玉京中境西北第六学堂
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Primary Educational Facility / Undersea Coral Campus in Abyss Sea (白玉京西北分校)
Notes: An undersea branch campus of the White Jade Capital schools situated within an exquisite multicolored coral structure on the seabed of the Abyss Sea in the northwestern Central Domain; marked by a colossal white jade monument stele; where Eleventh Lady Mei, Fourteenth Lady, and the undercover Carefree Palace "Grand-Aunt" registered for enrollment. Commuters arrive via Kunpeng-propelled underwater bubble transit and can register for dormitory boarding services.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Second Southeast Academy of White Jade Capital
Location Chinese Name: 白玉京东南第二学堂 / 白玉京中境东南第二学堂
Location English Name (optional): Second Southeast Academy of White Jade Capital
Type: Primary Educational Facility / Undersea Coral Campus in Abyss Sea (白玉京东南分校)
Notes: Primary mortal academy established by Zhou Hui. An undersea branch campus of the White Jade Capital schools located in the southeastern Central Domain near Fengqing City; serves mortal children from partner villages including Qiao Family Village; where Eleventh Lady Qiao and an undercover Carefree Palace orphan girl registered for enrollment before Zhou Hui's moonlight-bright "Rector" avatar. Textbooks are distributed before term start with entrance placement exams scheduled post-New Year.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Carefree Pavilion
Location Chinese Name: 逍遥阁
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Commercial Branch Outlets / Luxury Entertainment Complexes of Carefree Palace (逍遥宫连锁网点 / 娱乐会所)
Notes: Commercial storefronts and luxury entertainment complexes established by Carefree Palace across every major immortal market and city, foremost among them the premier entertainment pavilion in Sky City offering high-end wine, dance, rare auctions, and covert gray vices. Backed by stable supply lines, they offer quality-guaranteed pills, after-sales service for magical artifacts, and custom bespoke orders, serving as the default shopping and auction hub across the realms.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Blood Cliff of the Western Domain
Location Chinese Name: 西境血崖
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Karst Abyss & Demonic Stronghold in Western Domain (西境险地)
Notes: A perilous blood-red cliff and subterranean karst chasm in the Western Domain where flesh-eating crows peck at rotting carrion; houses the inverted subterranean palace of the Ruwang Ghost Emperor and the blooming grounds of the mythical Spider Lily (彼岸花).
```

```yaml
Location Name: Beast Soul Tower
Location Chinese Name: 兽魂塔
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Ancient Pagoda / Forbidden Sacred Ground in Thousand Beasts Hall (万兽堂禁地)
Notes: Sacred ancestral pagoda situated at the very center of Thousand Beasts Hall; suppresses the ancient ferocious beast soul retrieved from the upper realm; site of the lethal Beast Soul Succession Trial where disciples are lured to devour portions of the untamed beast soul.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Sky City
Location Chinese Name: 天上城
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Floating Immortal Metropolis / Supreme Power in Northern Domain (北境最高血统浮空仙城)
Notes: The supreme floating immortal city in the Northern Domain, whose City Lord boasts the highest bloodline in the modern cultivation realm; in the Heavenly Dao's original script, Eternal Villa dispatched a flying vessel to Sky City to report the demonic calamity, leading to the protagonist being lavishly hosted by Sky City and catching the eye of its young miss, the "Red Rose."
```

```yaml
Location Name: Pingkang Ward
Location Chinese Name: 平康坊
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Commercial & Entertainment District in Sky City (天上城城北最繁华坊区)
Notes: The most bustling and prosperous commercial and entertainment district in the north of Sky City; home to popular entertainment venues and retail storefronts, including the newly opened Sky City Immortal Chess Hall.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Spirit Palace Secret Realm / Spirit Stone Palace
Location Chinese Name: 灵宫秘境 / 灵石宫殿
Location English Name (optional): Spirit Palace Secret Realm
Type: Ancient Secret Realm / Pure Spirit Stone Palace in Eternal Villa (千古山庄上古秘境)
Notes: An ancient secret realm belonging to Eternal Villa, completely constructed from massive, monolithic, radiant spirit stones stacked directly atop an ancient mineral vein. Originally erected during the ancient Demon-God War when a Demon Eye opened beneath the mineral vein; the immense spiritual energy pinned down the demonic vanguard. Later, Eternal Villa ancestors built a magnificent palace of spirit stones equipped with ninety-nine Reverse-Spirit Formations to continuously pump spiritual energy into the Demon Eye for 9,999 years to dissolve it. In the original Heavenly Dao script, Mo Xi was fated to unseal this Demon Eye after the sect's fall, causing the palace to be corrupted into a demonic lair that destroyed Eternal Villa. Opened by Villa Master Qingyang for Qingyang Qingyu and Mo Xi to study secret realm construction, where Zhou Hui planned to crack open the vein and install a Tower Defense System.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Department of Works
Location Chinese Name: 司工院
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Subordinate Engineering Department / Bureau in Heavenly Dao Alliance (天道盟下属机构)
Notes: Major administrative department and engineering body within the Heavenly Dao Alliance in the Northern Domain, overseeing civil engineering, array construction, and artifact refinement across the domain. Oversees subordinate forges and smithies where Guan Ye was once abused, overworked, and brutally crippled by having his spine ripped out. In Chapter 188, targeted by Ran Deng and Yuwen Xuan for decapitation to clear all engineering obstacles, monopolize cultivation labor resources, and avenge Fourth Brother.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Water Mirror Tower
Location Chinese Name: 水镜楼
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Communications & Media Operations Center in Heavenly Spirit Sect (天灵派通信与直播商务枢纽)
Notes: A specialized facility of the Heavenly Spirit Sect equipped with numerous water mirror arrays; dedicated to external communications, live array broadcasts of major events such as the Immortal Arts Conference, and managing the sect's commercial advertising operations and sponsorship requests.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Fengqing City
Location Chinese Name: 风清城
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Ancient Commercial Metropolis / Inter-Domain Transit Hub in Southern Central Domain (中境南部古代重镇)
Notes: A prominent commercial city and regional hub situated in the southern Central Domain, linked to the Southern Domain and White Jade Capital via an ancient spatial teleportation array preserved from the God-Demon War era. Historically under the deep economic sway of the Carefree Palace; its teleportation array is currently situated in the City Lord's Estate at the city center. Site of a major regional preliminary arena of the Immortal Arts Assembly where Mo Bei led a disciple contingent and claimed the championship.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Azure Phoenix Clan
Location Chinese Name: 青凰族
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: High-Ranking Avian Demon Clan in Southern Realm (南境禽类妖族)
Notes: A revered, high-ranking avian demon clan native to the Southern Realm, celebrated as a symbol of benevolence, harmony, and righteousness. In antiquity during the God-Demon War, their ancestors allied with human cultivators and fought alongside human heroic spirits through the cross-border teleportation arrays. Blessed with the innate spiritual "Heavenly Eye" (天目) capable of piercing illusions and perceiving the fundamental essence of souls.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Sacred Tree of Fire Abyss
Location Chinese Name: 火渊神树
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Ancient Divine Tree / Sacred Arboreal Realm in Southwestern Southern Realm (南境西南巨木神树)
Notes: A colossal, primordial divine tree situated in the southwestern corner of the Southern Realm, flourishing for tens of thousands of years since the War of Gods and Demons. Revered as the seat of supreme authority in the Southern Realm, where various beast and avian demon clans roost, chief among them the Azure Phoenix Clan whose holy maidens and priestesses worship and tend to it. In Chapter 168, its full vertical anatomy is detailed: (1) Summit canopy glows a luminous golden-red, blazing with Golden Crow flames into the clouds, inhabited exclusively by the Phoenix clan; (2) Sub-canopy features eternal autumn red and yellow boughs teeming with spiritual energy and spirit beasts; (3) Middle tier consists of emerald green foliage forming multi-layered stacked forests where trees, vines, demon clans, and rare beasts thrive; (4) Ground trunk emerges from soil sprawling across tens of thousands of li, its dark core sheltering shade-loving insects, resting upon a tens-of-thousands-of-years-old dead leaf bed; (5) Subterranean roots transition deep underground into silver-white and pure silver luminescent roots spreading throughout the Southern Domain, with taproots leading to pure-demon pocket worlds (the Demon Realm); (6) Ocean-submerged roots penetrate the continental crust into the pitch-black Abyssal Ocean, burning like Golden Crow flames whose thermal clash with icy waters creates the Southern Domain's sole forbidden zone, where divine beings have been glimpsed in myth.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Azure Phoenix Hall
Location Chinese Name: 青凰殿
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Sacred Palace / Clan Seat atop Fire Abyss Sacred Tree (青凰族王庭大殿)
Notes: The royal palace and administrative hall of the Azure Phoenix Clan situated in the high canopy of the Fire Abyss Sacred Tree in the Southern Domain; where Saintess Shui Huang'er and the elders direct clan affairs, and where demon clans register to participate in trial educational programs and study Heavenly Spirit Sect cultivation manuals.
```

```yaml
Location Name: The Severed World / Counterfeit World of the Sixth Cycle
Location Chinese Name: 第六世的另外半边世界 / 伪物世界
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Shattered Realm / Spatiotemporal Anomaly of the Sixth Reincarnation Cycle (第六世分裂世界)
Notes: The severed half of the world created when the fifth cycle's heavenly lightning tribulation ripped the cosmos in two. While Zhou Hui remained in one half desperately gathering fragments to mend reality, the direct disciples' primary forms fell into the other half. There, an impostor puppet sculpted from dust and Zhou Hui's residual aura presided over the disciples. Retaining residual emotions and chaotic trauma from the third and fourth cycles, the disciples banded together to investigate the fake Master by staging the 'Heavenly Dao script' (a dogblood harem novel scenario) like a puppet theater, until Yuwen Xuan and Qu Qianxing ultimately obliterated that half-world to return its materials for Zhou Hui to mend.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Moon-Welcoming Valley
Location Chinese Name: 迎月谷
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Cultivation Sect (Northern Domain / 北境)
Notes: The premier authoritative alchemy sect (丹修门派) of the Northern Domain. Because it requires rare medicinal herbs from various climates, the sect establishes outposts and plantations across all domains, rotating disciples to manage them; consequently, its disciples have broad exposure to external territories and eagerly adapt local resources.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Cloud Azure Sect
Location Chinese Name: 云苍门 / 云苍派
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Cultivation Sect (Central Domain / 中境)
Notes: A minor local cultivation sect located in the Central Domain; represented by Daoist Heju at the Immortal Arts Assembly, seeking to import Heavenly Spirit Sect's standardized textbooks to improve disciple foundational education.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Huanfei Sect
Location Chinese Name: 幻飞门
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Cultivation Sect (Northern Domain / 北境)
Notes: One of the premier, top-ranked cultivation sects in the Northern Domain belonging to the "Three Sects, Six Factions, One Valley"; founded by the ancient Mahayana powerhouse Fairy Ruopei; possesses the sacred Moon Phase Mirror.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Three Sects, Six Factions, One Valley
Location Chinese Name: 三门六派一谷
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Regional Cultivation Hegemony / Elite Sect League (Northern Domain / 北境)
Notes: The collective title for the ten foremost, ruling elite cultivation sects of the Northern Domain; includes the Huanfei Sect (幻飞门), Moon-Welcoming Valley (迎月谷), and other supreme sects commanding northern cultivation society.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Feisha Sect
Location Chinese Name: 飞沙门
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Cultivation Sect (Western Domain / 西境)
Notes: Prominent cultivation sect in the Western Domain; one of whose traveling disciples submitted an academic paper on the Central Domain's heavenly tribulation to the journal *Spells* that was blatantly ghostwritten by his sect master / elder.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Ascension Stairway
Location Chinese Name: 升仙梯
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Ancient Primeval Relic / Sacred Cosmic Staircase
Notes: A mythical staircase connecting the mortal world to the upper immortal realm, dating back to the primeval wild era before cultivators existed when only Great Shamans walked the earth. In antiquity, mortals of great virtue, achievement, or courage could ascend by climbing its steps and withstanding the heavenly thunder trial. Destroyed during the Demon-God War, leaving ascension possible only through arduous cultivation. Unearthed by Zhou Hui beneath the Abyssal Ocean.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Abyssal Ocean / Abyssal Ocean Ruins
Location Chinese Name: 渊海 / 渊海遗迹
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Ancient Battlefield Ruins / Subterranean Sea (Central Domain)
Notes: A perilous subterranean ocean abyss and ancient God-Demon War battlefield filled with chaotic spiritual storms, corrupted demonic miasma, and ancient ruins. Houses Carefree Palace's covert biological/soul experimentation facility and the buried ruins of the Ascension Stairway.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Boundless Sea
Location Chinese Name: 无尽海
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Vast Oceanic Region (Eastern Domain / 东境)
Notes: A perilous, resource-rich primordial ocean covering nearly eighty percent of the Eastern Domain; teeming with floating island-sized tortoises, spirit beasts, demon beasts, and indigenous foreign races.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Heavenly Scripture Pagoda
Location Chinese Name: 天书塔
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Ancient Pagoda / Prophecy Monument in Northern Domain (北境上古建筑 / 预言圣地)
Notes: An eighteen-story pagoda located in the Northern Domain, resting upon thin paper steps pinned down like a paperweight. Built roughly a thousand years ago following the Demon-God War when an ancient sage ascended and the Upper Realm lowered a Heavenly Scripture as a staircase. Inside its single floor is a pool where tossing in spirit stones reveals a mirrored downward staircase leading to a subterranean hall with a thin paper floor and a viewing mirror (窥镜) to read the Heavenly Scripture prophecies.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Featherfolk
Location Chinese Name: 羽民
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Indigenous Foreign Race of the Eastern Domain (东境本土异族)
Notes: An ancient non-human race native to the Boundless Sea of the Eastern Domain, inspired by the Classic of Mountains and Seas. Humanoid beings with feathered wings instead of arms, capable of long-distance flight; biologically distinct from demon beasts (possessing no demon cores). Enthusiastic players and puzzle-solvers of Immortal Chess, harboring quiet hopes that Zhou Hui's open Ascension Stairway and White Jade Capital will grant foreign races a legitimate seat in the realm's cosmic order.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Ascension Terrace
Location Chinese Name: 升仙台
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Landmark Platform / Receiving Terrace of White Jade Capital (白玉京城外平台)
Notes: An expansive platform adorned with exquisite engravings protruding outward beyond the city gates of White Jade Capital into the open void above the Abyssal Ocean ruins. Historically modeled after the upper realm to receive all seeking living beings without discrimination; connects directly to the uppermost segment of the Ascension Stairway, which assesses cultivators' spirit root development, potential, and spiritual energy pathways.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Red Maple Cave
Location Chinese Name: 红枫洞
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Cultivation Sect in Southwestern Central Domain (中境西南炼器门派)
Notes: An artifact refinement sect located in the southwestern Central Domain, founded by Ancestor Wushan. Specializes in forging heavy weapons using a masculine fire-attribute technique that is physically detrimental to female cultivators. Historically suffered severe disciple shortages due to recurring beast tides wiping out young male cultivators.
```

```yaml
Location Name: White Egret Valley
Location Chinese Name: 白鹭谷
Location English Name (optional): Bailu Valley
Type: Former Cultivation Sect in Southwestern Central Domain (中境西南古门派)
Notes: The original sect of Shen Qingqian and Lu Ningning in the southwestern Central Domain. After its male lineage withered, it appointed Lu Ningning as Valley Master, whose muddle-headed father never properly trained her and focused only on marrying her off. Following the father's death in a beast tide, Lu Ningning surrendered the entire valley as a wedding dowry to La Jiu, allowing it to be absorbed into the Peerless Alliance while Shen Qingqian defected to found Blood Demon Hall.
```

```yaml
Location Name: The 67th Southern Heavenly Gate
Location Chinese Name: 第六十七扇南天门
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Celestial Gateway / Heavenly Court Administrative Outpost (上界天门)
Notes: One of numerous celestial gates in the upper realm connecting to lower minor worlds; despite being designated as a "Southern" gate, it does not actually face south. Positioned on lower cloud tiers near an Ascension Platform beneath the radiant humanoid "god," functioning as an administrative branch for lower-realm governance and ascender processing.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Miniature World
Location Chinese Name: 小小世界
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Miniature Self-Contained World / Pocket Cosmos (自成一体的小世界)
Notes: A fragile, self-contained world only the size of a soccer ball, complete with its own sun, moon, mountains, seas, mortal civilizations, beasts, spirits, and internal spiritual qi cycle. Forcibly torn from its native coordinates by True Immortals as a curiosity and nearly 'purified,' it was rescued in passing by Zhou Hui after he slew the upper-realm god. Preserved inside Zhou Hui's barren trial-piece pocket dimension where 1 day outside equals 100 years inside, allowing its damaged cosmic laws to gradually recuperate across multiple generational cycles of civilizations under the watch of its creator Goddess.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Langhuan Forbidden Grounds
Location Chinese Name: 琅嬛禁地
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Ancient Sacred Grounds / Forbidden Secret Repository in the Abyssal Ocean (渊海深处禁地)
Notes: A colossal, ancient forbidden site hidden in the deepest abyss of the Abyssal Ocean, named after the mythical celestial library of the Heavenly Emperor (琅嬛). It forms a complete minor world where every rock, fallen leaf, pond droplet, and bough embodies literature, poems, and philosophical debates. In Chapter 165, revealed to enshrine the genuine Heavenly Scripture in the form of a titanic, snapped ancient tree whose roots form the bedrock and boughs form the sky of Langhuan, behind which a fresh sickly sapling sprouted that communicated with the Heavenly Scripture Pagoda. Sighted by Boss Tao Zhu when guided by Yuwen Xuan; secretly sought by Yuwen Xuan to eliminate incriminating cosmic evidence regarding his role as one of the behind-the-scenes masterminds of the Heavenly Dao script.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Central Domain Cultivation Sect Accreditation Council
Location Chinese Name: 中境修仙门派审核会
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Regional Cultivation Educational & Sect Accreditation Body
Notes: An administrative and accreditation council established in the Central Domain and chaired by the Untiring Immortal Venerable (Zhou Hui). Reviews and approves cultivation sects wishing to establish direct disciple recruitment partnerships with the White Jade Capital Academies. Approved partner sects include Eternal Villa and the Thousand Harmony Gang.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Sun Wheel Palace
Location Chinese Name: 日轮宫
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Sacred Demon Palace / Core of Fire Abyss Sacred Tree (火渊神树核心 / 妖族禁地)
Notes: The very core and crown of the Fire Abyss Sacred Tree in the Southern Domain. Manifests externally as a colossal sphere of roaring demon fire hanging at the apex of the tree canopy, blazing perpetually even during the darkest hours when neither sun nor moon shines. Radiates concentrated demonic power that nurtures and guides the birth of new demons. Stores secret demon treasures; its demon flame scorches all living beings, requiring immense demonic power or dozens of times more spiritual energy for human cultivators to endure, naturally suppressing the power of visitors. In Chapter 169, Shui Huang'er guides Zhou Hui's party here as the highest honor of hospitality, where Mo Xi is prompted by Zhou Hui to step inside by his own strength to confront his illusions.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Lumbini Holy City
Location Chinese Name: 蓝毗尼圣城
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Ancient Demonic Metropolis / Capital in the Demon Realm (魔域圣城)
Notes: A prominent holy city in the Demon Realm where the Demon Emperor hosts grand celebrations and where high-ranking Demon Sovereigns gather.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Heavenly Emperor's Private Vault
Location Chinese Name: 天帝私库
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Sacred Landmark / Celestial Treasury in Heavenly Court (天庭最深处重地)
Notes: The heavily guarded private treasury of the Heavenly Emperor located in the deepest sanctum of the Heavenly Court; guarded by elite celestial soldiers and the ancient beast Lord Tianlu (Pixiu). Connected via a metaphysical backdoor to the fallen lower world.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Department of Celestial Medicine
Location Chinese Name: 天医署
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Celestial Bureaucracy / Medical Agency in Heavenly Court (天庭医疗机构)
Notes: The official healthcare and physician department of the Heavenly Court responsible for attending to celestial officials and divine beasts.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Ministry of Celestial Destiny
Location Chinese Name: 司天部
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Celestial Bureaucracy / Astrological Ministry in Heavenly Court (天庭司天推演机构)
Notes: The supreme astrological, divination, and fate-reckoning ministry of the Heavenly Court, staffed by astromancers and fortune-tellers who track cosmic destiny and the heavenly mandate across all realms. In Chapter 224, revealed as the department responsible for scripting the Heavenly Dao across the three thousand worlds, acting as the universal standard (Celestial Yardstick / 天尺) to bind lower realms. Staffed by immortal scribes notorious for procrastination ("pigeons attained human form"), recycling tropes, and authoring third-rate melodrama scripts to toy with fallen celestial sinners. Employs the Astral Ink-Marker (墨斗) in the Star Pool to track fates via Star Array Discs; descended into panicked buck-passing when the marker snapped a line threatening to bisect the Emperor Star.
```

```yaml
Location Name: Minor World No. 67
Location Chinese Name: 第六十七号小世界
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Minor World / Lower Realm Coordinate (司天部编号世界)
Notes: The bureaucratic designation assigned by the Heavenly Court's Ministry of Celestial Destiny to the novel's main world (the Langhuan world). Slated for decommissioning/scrapping; almost the entire Ministry took part in authoring its massive, bug-ridden Heavenly Dao script because multiple high-profile sinners were cast into it and it involved the Wordless Heavenly Scripture. Ran through several failed simulations until one cycle cleared, after which it automatically rebooted.

```yaml
Location Name: Place of Origin
Location Chinese Name: 起源之地
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Primordial Cosmic Node / Multi-Universal Collision Nexus (宇宙碰撞起源处)
Notes: The primordial collision point of several universes and the true place of origin of Zhou Hui's world, discovered by Zhou Hui behind the gatekeeper giant's abdominal breach. Filled with blinding light, cataclysmically dense spiritual energy capable of detonating high-level cultivators who breathe it in, and a bizarrely contoured metal machine/apparatus that Zhou Hui gazes upon obsessively.

```yaml
Location Name: Star Pool
Location Chinese Name: 星池
Location English Name (optional): 
Type: Cosmic Reservoir / Celestial Firmament Landmark in Heavenly Court (天庭星穹水池)
Notes: A cosmic celestial reservoir situated high above the Heavenly Court where the Astral Ink-Marker operates ceaselessly to snap ink lines of fate across the celestial firmament.
```

---



## 3. Cultivation Realms, Manuals, Items & Terms (修真设定与专有名词)

### Cultivation Realms (境界 — Listed Lowest to Highest)

| Chinese Term | Standard English Translation | Stage Subdivisions |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| 炼气期 (练气期) | Qi Condensation Stage | Early (初期), Middle (中期), Late (后期), Peak / Great Perfection (大圆满), Levels 1–10 (一层–十层) |
| 筑基期 | Foundation Establishment Stage | Early (初期), Middle (中期), Late (后期), Peak (大圆满) |
| 金丹期 | Golden Core Stage | Early (初期), Middle (中期), Late (后期), Peak (大圆满) |
| 元婴期 | Nascent Soul Stage | Early (初期), Middle (中期), Late (后期), Peak (大圆满) |
| 化神期 | Soul Formation Stage | Early (初期), Middle (中期), Late (后期), Peak (大圆满) |
| 炼虚期 | Void Refinement Stage | Early (初期), Middle (中期), Late (后期), Peak (大圆满) |
| 合体期 | Body Integration Stage | Early (初期), Middle (中期), Late (后期), Peak (大圆满) |
| 大乘期 | Mahayana Stage | Early (初期), Middle (中期), Late (后期), Peak (大圆满) |
| 渡劫期 | Tribulation Transcendence Stage | Final mortal stage before Ascension (飞升) |
| 散仙 | Loose Immortal | Cultivator who failed/bypassed normal ascension or shed their mortal body |

### Books, Manuals & Exam Materials (典籍与考卷 — Format in `*Italics*`)

| Chinese Name | English Translation | Notes |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| 《七世转轮诀》 / 《七世转轮功》 | *Seven-Life Reincarnation Wheel Sutra* (*Seven-Life Cyclic Metempsychosis Art* / *Seven Cycles Revolving Wheel Method*) | Zhou Hui's self-created core temporal cultivation technique; harnesses the deluge of spiritual energy unleashed upon the demise of a Tribulation Transcendence cultivator to perform a temporal leap back to his save point (the founding of Heavenly Spirit Sect), erasing the traces of past timelines and wiping his disciples' memories with each cycle. In Chapter 198, Zhou Hui confirms to Goddess Ren Ran and Ruwang that he used this art to rewind the world seven times, with his peerless strength deriving from the exponential accumulation across seven lifetimes; also reveals that his disciples retain muscle memory and self-invented insights from previous cycles (such as Guan Ye's Liuding Divine Fire formula) |
| 《五千年飞升三千年修仙》 | *Five Thousand Years of Ascension, Three Thousand Years of Cultivation* | Parody of China's infamous Gaokao workbook *Five Years of Gaokao, Three Years of Mock Exams* (《五年高考三年模拟》). Abbreviated in-story as *Five-Ascension Three-Cultivation* (五飞三修). |
| 《退休日程安排计划》 | *Retirement Schedule Plan* | Qu Qianxing's secret notebook that gets mercilessly postponed for centuries |
| 《山河地理志》 | *Records of Mountains and Rivers* | Mortal geography and gazetteer text stored in Zhou Hui's modern university library memory |
| 《清冷师尊死遁后和魔尊在一起了》 | *After Faking His Death, the Aloof Master Ended Up with the Demon Venerable* | Trashy/scandalous cultivation romance web novel spotted by Ran Deng in Zhou Hui's modern library memory; only the cover had text while inside was blank |
| 《飞灵剑法》 | *Flying Spirit Sword Technique* | Sword art manual self-created by Zhou Hui; the third stance is the Spirit Cloud Sword (灵云剑) |
| 《飞雪剑法》 | *Flying Snow Sword Art* | Sword art practiced by Mo Bei; Zhou Hui instructed her to synthesize its ninth form using ambient moisture |
| 《黑渊百草全书》 | *The Complete Compendium of Hundred Herbs of the Black Abyss* | Rare botanical and herbal compendium found by trial disciples in the Heavenly Spirit Sect Library Pavilion |
| 《万兽决》 | *Ten Thousand Beasts Manual* | Core foundational cultivation manual of Thousand Beasts Hall, authored by Patriarch Yusi; complete volume returned by Yuwen Xuan to Xia Bing |
| 《论供需关系对修仙界农作物种植的影响》 | *On the Impact of Supply and Demand Relationships on Crop Cultivation in the Cultivation World* | Zhou Hui's mandatory 30,000-word midterm research thesis topic assigned to direct disciples during their social practicum |
| 《基础灵气论》 | *Fundamental Theory of Spiritual Energy* | Foundational treatise authored by Zhou Hui analyzing the mechanics, transformation, and conservation of spiritual energy; assigned to Yuwen Xuan to copy 20,000 times as punishment for claiming "no spiritual energy means no danger" |
| 《引魂功》 | *Soul-Guiding Art* | Advanced cultivation manual synthesizing the Laws of Space, created by Zhou Hui across his fifth and sixth reincarnations; requires a pure Yin / spatial aptitude constitution; passed down to Mo Bei |
| 《真仙心经》 | *True Immortal Mind Sutra* | Ancient core cultivation manual passed down in Eternal Villa since before the War of Gods and Demons, reportedly allowing a cultivator to attain the realm of a True Immortal without ascending; incomplete and extraordinarily difficult to deduce or supplement due to severed contact with the True Immortal Realm |
| 《白玉仙法》 | *White Jade Immortal Technique* | Core ancestral cultivation art of Eternal Villa; originated from True Immortals, naturally cleanses and purifies demonic qi, turning practitioners into human air purifiers |
| 《白玉京周报》 | *White Jade Capital Weekly* | Popular weekly newspaper founded and established by Eldest Senior Sister Ran Deng, featuring sect publicity, academic paper commentary, tournament reports, and juicy gossip from across the cultivation world |
| 《白玉京日报》 | *White Jade Capital Daily* | Upgraded daily edition of the continental newspaper established by Ran Deng and Qu Qianxing to keep pace with the explosion of academic rebuttal submissions, debate essays, and tournament highlight reports |
| 《修仙界周报》 | *Cultivation World Weekly* | Copycat/counterfeit weekly newspaper published by Carefree Palace (逍遥宫) to compete with *White Jade Capital Weekly* |
| 《法术》 | *Spells* | Academic research journal founded by Untiring Immortal Venerable Zhou Hui to publish peer-reviewed treatises on spell mechanics, magical theory, and cultivation phenomena; peer-reviewed by Tribulation Transcendence powerhouses across multiple domains |
| 《飞升可控论》 | *Controllable Ascension Theory* | Central Domain thesis positing that ascension is a natural, controllable physical migration to higher-energy realms (such as the True Immortal Realm) once cultivation reaches maturity, popularized after Zhou Hui captured heavenly lightning in his palm |
| 《飞升奖赏说》 | *Ascension Reward Hypothesis* | Traditional Northern Domain orthodox dogma maintaining that ascension is a divine moral reward granted by True Immortals and the Heavenly Dao in recognition of a cultivator's strength and virtue, with heavenly lightning serving as the final trial |


| 全门大考 | Grand Unified Examination | Sect-wide standardized exam instituted by Zhou Hui across both Inner and Outer Sects to dismantle nepotism and ensure meritocratic fairness |

| Chinese Term | English Translation | Notes |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |

| 真仙诅咒 | Curse of the True Immortals | Retributive curses placed by upper-realm True Immortals upon the bloodlines of Saint Lord Qingyang, his mortal companions, and rebellious mortal cultivators who severed the Ascension Stairway 10,000 years ago; targets specific bloodlines (such as the Ran clan with abbreviated lifespans, the True Dragon bloodline, and Blood Demon Hall) in the Heavenly Dao's karmic script |
| 笔猴 | Brush Monkey | Spiritual beast raised by Zhou Hui whose shed hair is used to craft talisman brushes; used in Exam 1's math/alchemy word problem |
| 筑基丹 | Foundation Establishment Pill | Expensive pill required by ordinary disciples to break through to Foundation Establishment |
| 赤精丹 | Crimson Essence Pill | Basic Qi-replenishing medicinal pill |
| 九味散 | Nine-Ingredient Powder | Medicinal powder crafted from Brush Monkey hair ash |
| 本命法宝 | Lifebound Treasure / Natal Artifact | A cultivator's core soul-bound magical weapon |
| 灵石 | Spirit Stone(s) | Standard currency and spiritual energy source (Low/Middle/High/Supreme Grade) |
| 灵根 | Spirit Root(s) | E.g., Four-Element Spirit Root (四灵根), Heavenly Spirit Root (天灵根), Gold Heavenly Spirit Root (金系天灵根) |

| 内门弟子 | Inner Sect Disciple | Full-fledged disciples of the sect; eligible for monthly Spirit Stone stipends |

| 域外天魔 | Extraterrestrial Demon | Eldritch/otherworldly fiends beyond the mortal realm |
| 芥子空间 | Mustard-seed Spatial Dimension | Sub-space artifact; Zhou Hui's personal mustard-seed space features 10:1 time dilation (10 days inside = 1 day outside) |
| 回春阵 | Spring Returning Formation | Basic Qi Condensation array that gathers vitality / wood-attribute spiritual energy |
| 聚灵阵 | Spirit Gathering Formation | Standard Qi Condensation array designed to draw ambient spiritual energy |
| 木灵阵 | Wood Spirit Formation | Wood-attribute spirit-gathering array beneficial for spirit herb fields |
| 回灵阵 | Spirit Replenishing Formation | Fast-recovery spirit-gathering array |
| 系统 | System | UI manifested by Zhou Hui based on game/system mechanics to assign quests and track sect development |
| 日晷钟 | Sundial Clock | Sect timekeeping instrument dividing a shichen (2 hours) into 120 minutes |
| 黄册 | Yellow Registers | Sect census records and official disciple registry ledgers (historically imperial household registers bound in yellow paper) |
| 极品灵石 | Supreme-Grade Spirit Stone(s) | Highest quality spirit stones with pure, concentrated spiritual energy |
| 薄鱼 | Bo Fish | Mythical one-eyed fish from the *Classic of Mountains and Seas*, whose appearance foretells drought |
| 蜃妖 | Shen Demon | Mythical clam/mirage dragon demon capable of generating illusions |
| 何罗 (何罗鱼) | Heluo (Heluo Fish) | Mythical fish with one head and ten bodies from the *Classic of Mountains and Seas*, edible to cure illness and madness |
| 蛊雕 | Gu Diao | Ferocious mythical chimera beast from the *Classic of Mountains and Seas*, resembling a horned bird or aquatic predator with an infant-like cry |
| 文鳐鱼 | Wenyao Fish | Mythical winged flying fish from Mount Taiwu in the *Classic of Mountains and Seas*, whose appearance foretells abundant harvests; its small, spine-dense bones are carved by Guan Ye as raw forging material during the qualification exam |
| 三昧真火 | Samadhi True Fire (True Samadhi Fire) | Legendary Daoist spiritual fire capable of incinerating supreme spiritual materials and spiritual bodies; exceptionally stable and widely used by alchemists and refiners, but can suffer rare furnace blowout mutations |
| 极罗地火 / 极罗金焰 | Extremity Earth Fire / Extremity Golden Flame | A gentle, versatile subterranean alchemy and forging flame; has the lowest mutation rate among flames, but when mutated into Extremity Golden Flame, becomes one of the Ten Divine Flames best suited for artifact refining. Used by Guan Xinguang in the qualification exam |
| 六丁神火 | Liuding Divine Fire (Six Ding Divine Fire) | One of the legendary Daoist divine flames (notably Taishang Laojun / Lord Laozi's furnace flame); Guan Ye produces it in Chapter 198 by treating mutated Samadhi True Fire with seven frost-covered wood blocks and combustion accelerant, a secret technique he independently invented in the 6th cycle |
| 事故体质 | Accident-Prone Constitution | Zhou Hui's evaluation of Sikong Yao: wherever he goes, trouble and perilous crises erupt, yet they contain enough maneuverability to be converted into serendipity if handled adeptly |
| 气运守恒定律 | Law of Conservation of Karmic Luck | Sikong Yao's pseudo-scientific theory stolen from Heavenly Spirit Sect physics discussions: when one person gains luck, someone else must lose it |
| 白鹿追云车 | White Deer Cloud-Chasing Carriage | Immortal treasure carriage crafted by upper-realm True Immortals, drawn by eight snowy spirit deer generating rainbow light; possessed by Su Xin |
| 偷光镜 | Light-Stealing Mirror | Magical bronze mirror treasure capable of piercing through Golden Core-grade formations to spy on caves or exam papers; possessed by Su Xin |
| 七叶琥珀 | Seven-Leaf Amber | Rare spiritual plant/amber that grows in the subterranean flames of the Southern Border's desolate valleys; used to refine flames for Purple Gold |
| 紫金 | Purple Gold | High-grade spiritual metal used for forging and refining advanced Foundation Establishment weapons |
| 七星剑 | Seven-Star Sword | High-grade magical sword refined from Purple Gold, suitable for Foundation Establishment Late Stage cultivators |
| 珊瑚台 | Coral Platform | Floating coral examination platform encased in protective spiritual bubbles inside the Palace of Silence, dynamically rising or falling based on real-time exam scores |

| 炉鼎 | Cultivation Furnace | Human cultivator exploited as a vessel for dual-cultivation or forced spiritual energy extraction |

| 矿灵 | Mine Spirit | Sentient spiritual beast formed within the core of a massive spiritual stone vein; refining or consuming it allows one to command the spiritual energy of the entire vein and bind it to one's soul |
| 矿精 | Mine Essence | Pure, concentrated crystallized spiritual essence formed deep within the innermost core of a spiritual vein |
| 阴水 | Yin Water | Freezing, pitch-black liquid condensed from pure Yin energy, fed to child furnaces in illicit production workshops to force Yin accumulation, often freezing children solid |

Those who tread evil paths and use unscrupulous methods to raise cultivation; 3. Those who already have a master and sect. The first two face immediate execution upon stepping into sect territory |
