| 红莲业火 | Red Lotus Karmic Fire | Legendary Daoist spiritual flame of immense heat and karmic purifying power, used by Blood Demon Hall to cultivate mature thousand-year Flame Crystal Flowers |
| 凤凰之沙 | Phoenix Sand | Rare spiritual mineral/sand associated with the mythical Phoenix, used alongside Red Lotus Karmic Fire to nourish fire-attribute spiritual flora |
| 水晶仙人棋 | Limited Edition Crystal Immortal Chess | The one-of-a-kind premier inaugural auction lot at the Tianling Winter Solstice Auction; crafted from rare crystal materials personally handpicked by Mahayana grandmaster Tao Zhu and blessed by Untiring Immortal Venerable Zhou Hui; features exquisite figurines of the top Central Domain cultivators (including Qingyang Qingli and Bai Wubin) paired with autographed holographic cards |
| 水循环法器 | Water Circulation Artifact | Hydraulic fluid circulation artifact forged by Guan Ye based on technical manuals from Zhou Hui's modern memories, maintaining spiritual spring flow and turning stagnant water into living currents |
| 命石 | Life Stone | Soul-bound token traditionally surrendered by mortal/demon vassal villages to demon kings or high sects as collateral for protection |
| 眷属 | Vassals / Vassal Dependents | Subordinate mortal or half-demon clans bound to higher cultivation factions or demon kings for protection |
| 饕餮之魂 | Soul of the Taotie | Soul fragment of the ancient ferocious beast Taotie from the God-Demon War; implanted into Yuwen Xuan as an embryo; a dormant portion lodged inside the fiend head was awakened by Xuan'er's infant clothing |
| 六世尸骸 | Six Cycles of Reincarnation Skeletons | Six identical human skeletons found near the Taotie head; when restored with flesh and blood, they revealed the living appearance of a row of "Yuwen Xuans", representing the past six reincarnations devoured by the Taotie beast soul |
| 黑羊形态 | Black Lamb Form | The bizarre transformation assumed by Eldest Senior Brother Yuwen Xuan after devouring the ancient Taotie skeleton in the teleportation hall; fuses soul and bone into a tiny, pitch-black, plump lamb that weeps pitter-patter tears |
| 白羊形态 (小白羊) | White Lamb Form (Little White Lamb) | The corporeal form assumed by the dormant half of the ancient Taotie Remnant Soul after Zhou Hui equips it with a custom-forged magical artifact; appears as a docile, fluffy white lamb with a chiming bell around its neck, but harbors chaotic cosmic devouring eyes and pugnacious combat bleats |
| 骨灰煎饼 | Corpse-Ash Donut Pancake | A donut-shaped flatbread pancake fried by Zhou Hui using flour, spiritual water, and the ground skeletal ashes of Yuwen Xuan's past six lifetimes; looped around Yuwen Xuan's neck like an oversized Elizabeth collar to force him to reconstruct his original human body |
| 真仙文字 | True Immortal Script | The ancient sacred script of True Immortals; identical to the text on Broken Sky Cliff and inscribed upon the Grand Gate of White Jade Capital, deciphered by Zhou Hui to create his cultivation methods |
| 圣主 | Sacred Lord | The supreme title of reverence used by the ancient spectral army of White Jade Capital to salute Zhou Hui upon opening the city gates |
| 青鸟 | Azure Bird | Mythical celestial avian phantom that descended from the high towers of White Jade Capital carrying the jade signet to entrust to Zhou Hui |
| 观天镜 | Sky-Gazing Mirror | Large celestial observation mirror on the Star-Gazing Platform in Buyan Palace used by Qu Qianxing to track astral trajectories and divine destinies |
| 占星盘 | Astrolabe | Astrological instrument on the Star-Gazing Platform spun by Qu Qianxing to compute fate charts, birth records, and mathematical talents of mortal populations |
| 怨气虫 | Resentment Bugs | Spiritual creatures / ethereal vermin coalesced from the lingering negative emotional residue and humiliation of high-level cultivators |
| 万年桃木枝 | Peach Branch Switch | Disciplinary switch snapped by Zhou Hui from the ancient tens-of-thousands-of-years-old peach tree before the mountain gate to thrash Yuwen Xuan |
| 鬼车 / 九头鸟 | Guiche / Nine-Headed Bird | Mythical ten-headed ferocious bird of ill omen whose tenth head was severed by a deity, leaving a perpetually bleeding neck; its enormous beast soul was slain and sealed in the Beast Soul Tower by Zhou Hui at Soul Formation; fortified by a drop of True Immortal Blood |
| 真仙之血 | True Immortal Blood | Pure celestial blood without divine or demonic corruption, containing boundless divine power; extracted by Zhou Hui from the sealed Nine-Headed Bird's soul |
| 白玉京折扇史研究 | A Study on the History of Folding Fans in White Jade Capital | Academic paper presented by Hermit Zui Liu for the Immortal Magic Conference Organizing Committee, humorously linking Eternal Villa's cultivation to folding fan spirits |
| 真仙心经 | True Immortal Mind Sutra | Incomplete ancestral core cultivation manual of Eternal Villa, originating from pre-God-Demon War research into True Immortals; supposedly allows one to achieve True Immortal status without ascending |
| 星苔 | Star Moss | Rare bioluminescent spiritual moss from deep Southern Domain caves; demands a humid environment and dense spiritual qi; sells for sky-high auction prices |
| 养生壶 | Health-Preserving Kettle / Wellness Pot | Small automated alchemy kettle adapted from Zhou Hui's modern memories, automatically brewing medicinal tea to augment cultivation |
| 模块拼装洞府 | Modular Assembly Cave-Dwelling | Standardized DIY cave-dwelling modular building block system developed by Ran Deng and commercialized by Mo Xi; inspired by a history of toy building blocks in the Library Pavilion |
| 云车 | Cloud Chariot | Luxurious flying carriage cruising upon celestial clouds; pinnacle-tier travel conveyance in the cultivation world |
| 《法术》 | *Arts & Sorcery* (*Spells & Sorcery*) | Academic journal founded and chief-edited by Untiring Immortal Venerable Zhou Hui, compiling high-level research papers and theses by Late Mahayana and Void Refinement grandmasters |
| 《论灵力和仙力的不同》 | *On the Differences Between Spiritual Energy and Immortal Energy* | Academic thesis penned by Villa Master Qingyang published in *Arts & Sorcery*, elucidating practical methods to refine ancient Demon-God War relic shards and extract sealed immortal energy |
| 仙人之骨 | Immortal's Bones | Combat arena map replicating Immortals' Chess terrain, featuring black sands, beast skeletons, shattered array remnants, and a colossal ancient immortal skeleton |
| 断桥 | Broken Bridge | Qi Condensation stage arena map in the Immortal Arts Assembly corresponding to Hermit Zui Liu's academic paper *A Study on the History of White Jade Capital Folding Fans* |
| 擂主 | Platform Master | Reigning champion of an arena platform in the Immortals' Gathering tournament holding ten consecutive victories |
| 灵台 | Spirit Terrace | Elevated rest and recovery pavilion hovering above each tournament arena platform, equipped with defensive arrays, spirit food, and healing pills |
| 彩虹软剑 | Flexible Sword (Rainbow Flexible Sword) | Boundless rainbow flexible sword crafted specially by Untiring Immortal Venerable Zhou Hui for sixth disciple Mo Bei; imbued with the rule of "weakening against the weak, strengthening against the strong" (遇弱则弱，越强则强), automatically scaling to drag all opponents down to the wielder's exact cultivation realm and enforcing a test of pure sword skill |
| 剑心 | Sword Heart | Pure crystallization of martial intent and swordsmanship condensed from battle; golden light that manifests on the soul of a true sword cultivator |
| 不定向传送阵 | Omni-Directional Spatial Teleportation Array | Ancient White Jade Capital spatial formation capable of teleporting targets to locations without fixed teleportation beacons, requiring massive spiritual energy and an intermediary spatial anchor |
| 骨灰大饼渣渣 | Bone-Ash Patty Crumbs | Crumbs from the flatbread pancake baked with Yuwen Xuan's past reincarnations' cremated ashes; used by Yuwen Xuan as a visceral, indelible spatial anchor on targets |
| 弑仙剑 | Immortal-Slaying Sword | Ancient weapon from the God-Demon War designed to slay True Immortals through stepped cultivator blood sacrifices |
| 仙眷者 | Favored of the Immortals | Practitioners who cultivate by receiving direct empowerment and oracles from True Immortals via sacrificial rites |
| 祀体 | Sacrificial Vessel | Human or beast selected as ritual blood food and vessel for deities or ferocious beasts |
| 第八世 | The Eighth Life / Eighth Cycle | Shocking revelation from the Taotie white remnant soul that the current timeline is actually the eighth reincarnation, contradicting Zhou Hui's Seven Lives Transmigration count |
| 司南 | Sinan (Sinan Ladle-Compass) | Lord Siwu's lifebound magical treasure refined over 999 years with 999 rare materials under True Immortal guidance; manipulated by heavenly barrier/script to attack its own master |
| 白泽 | Baize | Omniscient mythical divine beast from antiquity capable of understanding all speech |
| 真龙 | True Dragon | Primordial sacred dragon of the Upper Realm; Lu Shulong's true identity, possessing innate immunity to memory-wiping time reversals |
| 锁龙鼎 | Dragon-Locking Cauldron | Ancient bronze cauldron passed down from True Immortals, forged specifically to restrain, siphon, and venomously pollute dragon power |
| 丹毒药剂 | Pill Poison Agent | Pharmaceutical combat toxin distilled from alchemical impurities and pill dross, used strategically as an aerosolized weapon in enclosed arena environments |
| 神树频道 | Sacred Tree Channel | Official Wood Spirit telecommunications and livestream broadcasting channel operating over the Sacred Tree root network |
| 千种玉 | Thousand-Species Jade | Supreme structural jade material utilized for building the Ascension Terrace and later repurposed for the Palace of Silence and the Library Pavilion; renowned for its extraordinary resistance against heavenly tribulation lightning |
| 任务面板 | Sect Quest Panel | System UI function in the Heavenly Spirit Sect unlocked for students in the Intermediate Class and above to view sect quests and disciple map locations in real time |
| 散雷 | Scattered Lightning | Heavenly tribulation lightning deployed as a wide-area carpet bombardment of numerous slender lightning bolts rather than a single concentrated lightning strike |
| 月相镜 | Moon Phase Mirror | Ancient divining mirror possessed by Fairy Ruopei of the Huanfei Sect, capable of peering into distant scenes and establishing communication across vast domains without spatial hindrance |
| 拘灵阵 | Spirit-Binding Array | Defensive formation array given to Mo Bei by Ran Deng, sealing a target's spiritual meridians to prevent the circulation of any cultivation methods, rendering cultivators as powerless as mortals |
| 五驭 / 五驭课 | Five Charioteering Arts / Five Charioteering Arts Class | White Jade Capital academy curriculum based on the classic Six Arts, utilizing body-refinement manuals provided by Shen Qingqian to teach mortals posture, breathing, and physical conditioning |
| 妖火 | Demon Fire | Sacred and lethal flame of the demon race blazing perpetually atop the Fire Abyss Sacred Tree and enclosing the Sun Wheel Palace; capable of incinerating living beings and inflicting extreme spiritual agony on the soul, but resonates with pure Yang spiritual energy and can be melted or dissolved by peerless powerhouses |
| 共生灵木 / 共生者 | Symbiotic Sacred Tree / Symbiotic Partner | Ancient symbiotic bond shared between demon beasts and spiritual flora/trees; demons safeguard the tree from harm, while the tree provides divine shelter and spiritual protection during a demon's critical breakthrough trials |
| 妖目 | Demon Eye | The innate spiritual vision of the demon race (such as the Azure Phoenix Clan) capable of perceiving concealed spiritual energy flows, defensive matrix boundaries, and soul essence |
| 天衣 | Heavenly Robe | Exquisite garment refined by Qu Qianxing using True Immortal Blood and the Tribulation Transcendence avatar of Carefree Palace; feels like human skin; can focus all attention onto the wearer or render them completely overlooked; confirmed capable of deceiving even Zhou Hui; stolen by Ran Deng to bribe Yuwen Xuan |
| 典礼院 | Court of Ceremonies | Specialized department within the Heavenly Dao Alliance tasked with communicating with the Upper Realm / Heavenly Court, conducting sacrifices, and receiving Immortal Edicts; formerly overseen by Chief Siwu, now effectively under Xin Zhu's control |
| 护法降魔天将 | Guardian Demon-Subduing Heavenly General | Ever-victorious celestial general directly under the Heavenly Court commanding forces against the demon race; invoked by Goddess Ren Ran via an emergency prayer ritual as a backchannel to the Upper Realm |
| 神宴 / 神餐 | Divine Feast / Sacrificial Offerings | Celestial delicacies prepared for the quincentennial Sky-Tour sacrifice; containing divine power that even True Immortals cannot easily withstand; leached into papier-mâché shells once consumed by deities |
| 神权 | Divine Authority | Sovereign power and cosmic permissions originating from the Genesis Machine, designed by an advanced ancient civilization to regulate cosmic explosion energies; monopolized by the Heavenly Court |
| 权限 | Administrative Clearance / Permissions | Multi-tiered operational authorization levels on the Genesis Machine. The Heavenly Court established Tier-1 clearance for itself, Tier-2 clearance for True Immortals cultivating celestial energy, basic clearance for cultivators utilizing spiritual energy, and zero clearance for mortals |
| 导灵 | Spiritual Channeling / Draining | Advanced medical and spiritual surgical procedure used to siphon, vent, and redistribute overwhelming, lethal quantities of spiritual or divine energy from a cultivator's vessel |
| 妖庭 | Demon Court | Ancient historical regime of the demon sovereigns that preceded human dominance; governed by the principle that whoever fulfilled the criteria to claim cosmic permissions held sovereign authority |

## 4. Modern Slang, Idioms & Exam Memes (梗与文化注释表)

> **Tooltip Rule**: Whenever one of these terms (or any similar Chinese idiom/slang) is adapted into English, attach a Markdown hover tooltip so the reader can see the original Chinese pun/idiom on hover:
> `[adapted English text](# "Literal CN: ... — explanation")`

| Chinese Term | Recommended English Adaptation | Hover Tooltip Explanation |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| 贴贴 | cuddle up / snuggle close | `"Literal CN: tiētiē (贴贴) — modern ACG/internet slang for being physically affectionate, clingy, or snuggling up close"` |
| 天上一天，地上一年 | a day in heaven is a year on earth | `"Literal CN: tiānshàng yītiān, dìshang yīnián (天上一天，地上一年) — traditional Taoist mythological proverb: time flows vastly slower in the celestial realm compared to the mortal world"` |
| 钉小人 | cursing voodoo dolls | `"Literal CN: dìng xiǎorén (钉小人) — nailing paper effigies of enemies to a tree trunk, a traditional hexing practice"` |
| 咸鱼 | total slacker / salted fish | `"Literal CN: salted fish (咸鱼) — slang for someone who has completely given up on ambition and just wants to lie flat"` |
| 内卷 (卷) | rat race / cutthroat grinding | `"Literal CN: involution (内卷) — modern slang for hyper-competitive, exhausting academic or workplace grinding"` |
| 五年高考三年模拟 (五飞三修) | *Five Thousand Years of Ascension, Three Thousand Years of Cultivation* | `"Parody of China's notorious college entrance exam workbook 'Five Years of Gaokao, Three Years of Mock Exams' (五年高考三年模拟)"` |
| 送命题 | death-trap question | `"Literal CN: life-forfeiting question (送命题) — a trick question where every wrong answer leads to doom (opposite of a freebie 'bonus question' 送分题)"` |
| 送分题 | freebie question | `"Literal CN: point-gifting question (送分题) — an easy warm-up question meant to give students free points"` |
| 压轴题 | final boss question | `"Literal CN: finale question (压轴题) — the hardest, highest-point comprehensive problem at the very end of a Chinese exam paper"` |
| 偏科 | lopsided grades / hyper-specialized | `"Literal CN: lopsided subjects (偏科) — excelling in one school subject while failing miserably at others"` |
| 漏题 / 押题 | exam leak / spotting exam questions | `"Literal CN: question-leaking / question-betting (漏题/押题) — trying to predict or obtain exam questions ahead of time"` |
| X教 | cult | `"Original CN self-censored as 'X-religion' (X教) for platform filters — means evil cult (邪教)"` |
| 摸底考试 | baseline assessment exam | `"Literal CN: bottom-feeling exam (摸底考试) — diagnostic/placement test given at the start of a school year to determine students' baseline competency"` |
| 恐怖如斯 | truly terrifying to this degree | `"Literal CN: terrifying to such an extent (恐怖如斯) — iconic web novel meme popularized by Battle Through the Heavens (斗破苍穹)"` |
| 夺舍 | body-snatching / soul possession | `"Literal CN: seizing the dwelling (夺舍) — standard Xianxia term for taking over another living cultivator's physical body"` |
| 卷王 | Involution King / champion of grinding | `"Literal CN: involution king (卷王) — modern slang for someone who out-works, out-studies, and out-grinds everyone else in hyper-competitive environments"` |
| 躺平 | lie flat | `"Literal CN: lying flat (躺平) — modern Chinese youth philosophy of refusing to participate in the hyper-competitive rat race"` |
| 寄了 | a goner / GG'd | `"Literal CN: sent off / GG (寄了) — modern Chinese gaming slang meaning dead, failed, or game over"` |
| 偷了家 | backdoored / sacked base | `"Literal CN: stole the home base (偷了家) — gaming/MOBA slang for backdoor base raiding while the defender is away"` |
| 又双叒叕 | again and again and again | `"Literal CN: yòu shuāng ruò zhuó (又双叒叕) — internet meme stacking characters containing '又' (again) in increasing clusters to emphasize endless repetition"` |
| 探花 | Tanhua / Imperial Third Scholar | `"The third highest ranking scholar in the imperial civil service exams, traditionally awarded to candidates with outstanding talent and remarkable physical beauty"` |
| 状元 | Zhuangyuan / Imperial First Scholar | `"The very top scoring scholar in the imperial civil service examination"` |
| 酒囊饭袋 | wine-sack rice-bag / useless bureaucrats | `"Literal CN: wine sacks and rice bags (酒囊饭袋) — useless gluttons who consume resources without contributing any real ability"` |
| 恋爱脑 | love-brain / lovestruck | `"Literal CN: love-brain (恋爱脑) — internet slang for someone completely blinded by infatuation, tossing aside reason, loyalty, and morals"` |
| 样样通，样样松 | jack-of-all-trades, master of none | `"Literal CN: proficient in everything, loose in everything (样样通，样样松) — knows a little of everything, but master of nothing"` |
| 恨铁不成钢 | resent iron for not turning into steel | `"Literal CN: resent iron for not becoming steel (恨铁不成钢) — bitter exasperation at someone failing to live up to their potential"` |
| 罚抄 | punitive copying / lines copying | `"Literal CN: penal copying (罚抄) — assigning lines or copying mistakes as academic punishment"` |
| 身在福中不知福 | not realizing one's blessings | `"Literal CN: living in blessings yet unaware of one's fortune (身在福中不知福) — taking extraordinary good fortune or privileges for granted"` |
| 安静如鸡 | quiet as quail / quiet as chickens | `"Literal CN: quiet as a chicken (安静如鸡) — modern internet slang for abruptly shutting up and falling dead silent out of fear or awkwardness"` |
| 掩耳盗铃 | plugging one's ears while stealing a bell | `"Literal CN: plugging ears to steal a bell (掩耳盗铃) — deceiving oneself; burying one's head in the sand"` |
| 病急乱投医 | grasping at straws in desperation | `"Literal CN: turning to any doctor in panic (病急乱投医) — seeking any possible remedy when facing an emergency"` |
| 水到渠成 | letting the water flow naturally to form a canal | `"Literal CN: when water flows, a canal is formed (水到渠成) — idiom meaning when conditions are ripe, success naturally follows"` |
| 殃及池鱼 | collateral damage | `"Literal CN: disaster strikes the innocent fish in the moat (殃及池鱼) — from the idiom 'when the city gate catches fire, the fish in the moat suffer', meaning innocent bystanders harmed by others' actions"` |
| 放一只羊是放，放一群羊也是放 | herding one sheep is no different from herding a whole flock | `"Literal CN: 放一只羊是放，放一群羊也是放 — idiom meaning once you undertake a task for one person, extending it to a whole group requires little extra effort; might as well shepherd the entire herd"` |
| 择日不如撞日 | there is no better day than today / no time like the present | `"Literal CN: choosing a day is not as good as bumping into a day (择日不如撞日) — there is no time like the present; seizing the immediate opportunity"` |
| 厚此薄彼 | favor one while slighting the other | `"Literal CN: treat this one thick and that one thin (厚此薄彼) — showing blatant partiality or favoritism"` |
| 垫底 | cushioned the bottom of the ranks / dead last | `"Literal CN: cushioned the bottom (垫底) — being at the very bottom of the score rankings, acting as a cushion for everyone else"` |
| 摸鱼 | slacking off / loafing | `"Literal CN: catch fish in murky waters (摸鱼) — modern Chinese slang for slacking off or loafing on the job"` |
| 三天打鱼两天晒网 | fishing for three days and drying the nets for two | `"Literal CN: 三天打鱼两天晒网 — working in fits and starts; lacking consistency and discipline"` |
| 钱串子 | money-grubber | `"Literal CN: string of copper coins (钱串子) — slang for someone obsessed with making money"` |
| 挂B | cheater / hacker / cheat-user | `"Literal CN: 挂B (guà bī) — gaming slang for someone using hacks/cheats or having absurd unfair advantages"` |
| 仓鼠癖 | hamster hoarder mentality | `"Literal CN: hamster addiction (仓鼠癖) — hoarding tendencies; obsessively collecting and hoarding resources for a rainy day"` |
| 天选之子 | Child of Destiny / Chosen One | `"Literal CN: chosen child of heaven (天选之子) — the chosen one of destiny / traditional overpowered Xianxia protagonist trope"` |
| 卖乖 | acting cute / playing innocent | `"Literal CN: selling good behavior (卖乖) — acting obedient or cute to win favor, wheedle benefits, or deflect blame"` |
| 死遁 | faking one's death | `"Literal CN: death escape (死遁) — common web novel trope where a protagonist stages a fake death to escape an identity, master, or tragic relationship"` |
| 照猫画虎 | blindly copying them / drawing a tiger using a cat as a model | `"Literal CN: drawing a tiger using a cat as a model (照猫画虎) — imitating something poorly or superficially without understanding the essence"` |
| 冤种 | scapegoat disciples / unfortunate suckers | `"Literal CN: unfortunate sucker / wronged party (冤种) — modern internet slang for an unlucky dupe or sucker who is exploited or taken advantage of"` |
| 压力测试 | stress-testing | `"Literal CN: pressure test (压力测试) — engineering and software testing under extreme loads"` |
| 公测 | open beta / open beta testing | `"Literal CN: public test (公测) — open beta testing of software or games with massive user bases"` |
| 喜新厌旧 | abandon the old for the new | `"Literal CN: love the new and loathe the old (喜新厌旧) — idiom meaning to cast aside old companions or objects in favor of novel ones"` |
| 好大喜功 | obsessed with grandstanding and chasing glory | `"Literal CN: fond of great things and seeking merit (好大喜功) — ambitious for lavish achievements and eager to show off"` |
| 看眼色 | read the room | `"Literal CN: look at facial expressions / color (看眼色) — observing someone's expression to understand the mood or atmosphere"` |
| 挂山门 | hanging from the mountain gate / suspension from the mountain gate | `"Literal CN: 挂山门 (guà shānmén) — traditional martial sect punishment of stringing an errant disciple from the sect gate for public display"` |
| 挂了号 | registered on the ledger / on the radar | `"Literal CN: registered on the ledger (挂了号) — gained the personal notice or scrutiny of someone in authority"` |
| 蹭气运 | leeching karmic luck / rubbing off on luck | `"Literal CN: rubbing off on luck (蹭气运) — gaming/cultivation slang for borrowing or freeloading off someone else's lucky aura"` |
| 无利不起早 | refusing to rise early without personal profit | `"Literal CN: not rising early without profit (无利不起早) — never taking action or expending effort unless there is direct personal gain"` |
| 偷偷学习 | secretly studying | `"Literal CN: secretly studying (偷偷的学习) — classic Chinese high school trope of classmates pretending to slack off while secretly pulling all-nighters to study"` |
| 登徒子 | lecherous scoundrel | `"Literal CN: Dengtuzi (登徒子) — traditional literary archetype of a lascivious rake or wall-scaling peeping Tom"` |
| 葫芦娃救爷爷 | Calabash Brothers saving Grandpa | `"Literal CN: 葫芦娃救爷爷 — Chinese pop-culture meme from the classic animation 'Calabash Brothers', describing foolishly rushing in one by one to rescue someone, only to be picked off and captured sequentially"` |
| 升级文 | progression web novel | `"Literal CN: 升级文 (shēngjí wén) — web novel genre focused on steady leveling up, stat increases, and power progression"` |
| 来都来了 | since we're already here / might as well | `"Literal CN: 来都来了 (lái dōu lái le) — one of China's iconic Four Great Excuses: 'Since we've already come all this way, we might as well...'"` |
| 不卷仙尊 / 不卷仙人 | Un-Grinding Immortal / Un-Grinding Immortal Venerable | `"Literal CN: 不卷仙尊 (Bùjuǎn Xiānzūn) — Su Xin's derisive pun on Zhou Hui's title 'Untiring Immortal Venerable' (不倦仙尊), replacing '倦' (untiring) with '卷' (juǎn, involution/grinding) to mock him as an uncompetitive backwater bumpkin"` |
| 天贶日 | Festival of Heavenly Gifts | `"Literal CN: 天贶日 (tiān kuàng rì) — Festival of Heavenly Gifts on the 6th day of the 6th lunar month; traditional day for airing scriptures and robes"` |
| 鸟不拉屎的地方 | place where birds don't even shit | `"Literal CN: 鸟不拉屎的地方 (niǎo bù lā shǐ de dìfang) — colorful Chinese idiom for an isolated, godforsaken backwater"` |
| 人中龙凤 | dragon and phoenix among men / dragon among men | `"Literal CN: 人中龙凤 (rén zhōng lóng fèng) — dragon and phoenix among men, an outstanding genius destined for greatness"` |
| 豹眼苍髯 | leopard-like round eyes and graying beard | `"Literal CN: 豹眼苍髯 (bào yǎn cāng rán) — fierce round leopard eyes and graying beard; classic physical trope for a gruff, imposing warrior like Zhang Fei"` |
| 在心头蹦迪 | dancing on one's nerves / heart | `"Literal CN: 在心头蹦迪 (zài xīntóu bèngdí) — dancing in someone's heart/nerves; deliberately pushing all their buttons and driving them crazy"` |
| 乐子人 | agent of pure chaos / fun-seeker | `"Literal CN: 乐子人 (lèzǐrén) — someone who lives purely for the amusement, drama, and chaotic entertainment of a situation"` |
| 塞狗粮 | stuffing dog food down everyone's throats | `"Literal CN: 塞狗粮 (sāi gǒuliáng) — excessive public displays of romantic affection, forcing onlookers ('single dogs') to consume 'dog food'"` |
| 重点院校 | prestigious key academy | `"Literal CN: 重点院校 (zhòngdiǎn yuànxiào) — top tier university, high school, or elite educational institution"` |
| 闭门造车 | working behind closed doors | `"Literal CN: 闭门造车 (bì mén zào chē) — working in isolation from reality, building a cart behind closed doors without matching it to the track outside"` |
| 偷光镜 | Light-Stealing Mirror | `"Literal CN: steal light mirror (偷光镜) — reference to the ancient Chinese idiom 'chisel the wall to steal light' (凿壁偷光), adapted into a magical treasure enabling the user to peek through formations and view others' exam papers"` |
| 临时抱佛脚 | clinging to Buddha's feet at the last minute / last-minute cramming | `"Literal CN: clinging to Buddha's feet at the last second (临时抱佛脚) — frantically cramming or seeking divine intervention at the eleventh hour"` |
| 动态自适应测试 / 计算机自适应考试 | adaptive testing / dynamic difficulty adjustment | `"Zhou Hui's computerized standardized testing algorithm dynamically generating questions based on the examinee's performance and answers"` |
| 夜夜箫歌 | partying every single night / nightly revelry | `"Literal CN: music and revelry every night (夜夜箫歌) — constant celebration, banquet music, and feasting"` |
| 打退堂鼓 | beating a retreat / backing out | `"Literal CN: beating the drum to dismiss court (打退堂鼓) — idiom meaning to withdraw, chicken out, or give up midway"` |
| 否认三连 | denial trifecta | `"Literal CN: 否认三连 (fǒurèn sānlián) — modern Chinese internet meme: 'Not me, wasn't there, impossible / No, impossible, can't be done'"` |

| 心得 | reflection essay / study insights | `"Literal CN: 心得 (xīndé) — reflection essay / insights / experiential study notes; mandatory academic reflection assignment assigned by Qu Qianxing to keep disciples from idling after tribulations"` |
| 各人自扫门前雪 | each sweeps the snow before their own doorstep | `"Literal CN: 各人自扫门前雪 (gè rén zì sǎo mén qián xuě) — each sweeps the snow before their own doorstep; common idiom for minding one's own business and ignoring others' troubles"` |
| 自己找糖吃 | finding candy for oneself / shipper candy | `"Literal CN: 自己找糖吃 (zìjǐ zhǎo táng chī) — finding sugar/candy for oneself; modern ACG/fandom slang for seeking out sweet romantic shipping moments between characters"` |
| 师徒 | Master and Disciples | `"Literal CN: 师徒 (shītú) — Master and Disciples; chapter title referring to Zhou Hui and his disciples gathering warmly in the cave, contrasted with Northern Domain masters and disciples suffering academic peer-review trauma"` |
| 遗照 | funeral portrait | `"Literal CN: 捧着遗照 (pěngzhe yízhào) — holding a deceased ancestor's funeral portrait; comedic exaggeration for Mo Xi's solemnity holding the mirror showing Mo Bei"` |
| 暴风吸入 | hurricane inhalation / vacuuming down food | `"Literal CN: 暴风吸入 (bàofēng xīrù) — hurricane inhalation / vacuuming down food at gale-force speed; internet slang for ravenously inhaling food/drink"` |
| 第二轮太阳 | second sun | `"Literal CN: 第二轮太阳 (dì-èr lún tàiyáng) — a second sun; referring to the blinding white light of the ascension tribulation visible across continents"` |
| 改了十三遍 | revised thirteen times | `"Literal CN: 改了十三遍 (gǎi le shí-sān biàn) — revised thirteen times; the eternal graduate student nightmare of infinite peer review revisions and rejections"` |
| 烧起来了 | Flaming Up / Heat is On | `"Literal CN: 烧起来了 (shāo qǐlái le) — Flaming Up / Things Are Heating Up / A Flame War Has Begun; chapter title referring to academic drama igniting in the press as rejected powerhouses publish scathing open flame-war exposes in the newspaper"` |
| 导师代笔 | thesis advisor ghostwriting | `"Literal CN: 导师代笔 (dǎoshī dàibǐ) — thesis advisor / mentor ghostwriting for students; academic misconduct where senior masters write papers under their disciples' names"` |
| 伪鱼 | Fake Fish | `"Literal CN: 伪鱼 (Wěi Yú) — Fake Fish; pen name adopted by Fairy Ruopei in White Jade Capital Weekly to flame reviewers and ghostwriters"` |

| 看热闹 | watching the circus / front-row drama | `"Literal CN: 看热闹 (kàn rènao) — watching the bustling scene / spectator drama; enjoying chaotic conflict from a safe spectator seat with popcorn"` |
| 没有营养的争论 | Pointless Arguments | `"Literal CN: 没有营养的争论 (méiyǒu yíngyǎng de zhēnglùn) — Pointless Arguments / Nutrition-less Squabbles; chapter title referring to the chaotic domain-wide flame war ignited in White Jade Capital Weekly"` |
| 哗众取宠 | sensational grandstanding | `"Literal CN: 哗众取宠 (huázhòng-qǔchǒng) — seeking the clamor of the crowd to win favor; sensationalism / playing to the gallery"` |
| 地图炮 | regional carpet-bombing | `"Literal CN: 地图炮 (dìtúpào) — map cannon / area-of-effect carpet-bombing; internet slang for indiscriminate attacks or generalizations targeting an entire group or region"` |

| 修仙界周报 | Cultivation World Weekly | `"Literal CN: 《修仙界周报》 (Xiūxiān Jiè Zhōubào) — Cultivation World Weekly; knockoff copycat newspaper published by Carefree Palace mimicking White Jade Capital Weekly"` |

| 飞升可控论 | Controllable Ascension Theory | `"Literal CN: 飞升可控论 (fēishēng kěkòng lùn) — Controllable Ascension Theory; thesis proposing that ascension is an autonomous, controllable physical migration to higher-energy realms rather than divine judgment"` |

| 无用的备用方案 | The Futile Backup Plan | `"Literal CN: 无用的备用方案 (wúyòng de bèiyòng fāng'àn) — The Futile Backup Plan; chapter title referring to Carefree Palace's backup plan to invite Goddess Ren Ran being instantly rendered completely irrelevant by Zhou Hui's excavation of the Ascension Stairway"` |
| 一周八卦 | Weekly Gossip | `"Literal CN: 《一周八卦》 (Yī Zhōu Bāguà) — Weekly Gossip; sensational investigative gossip tabloid founded by Mahayana powerhouse Fairy Xia with Ran Deng's guidance, taking in the fugitive former editor of Cultivation World Weekly"` |

| 差异化 | product differentiation | `"Literal CN: 差异化 (chāyìhuà) — modern business/marketing terminology for product differentiation / niche positioning; taught by Zhou Hui to Fairy Xia to develop unique publications"` |

| 学习动力 | The Motivation to Learn | `"Literal CN: 学习动力 (xuéxí dònglì) — The Motivation to Learn; chapter title referring to Zhou Hui using the threat of a diagnostic baseline exam to force slacker Ran Deng to maintain the palace lantern and refine her divine sense/spiritual energy"` |
| 贝戋 | shameless / cheeky rascal | `"Literal CN: 贝戋 (bèi-jiān) — split glyph for 贱 (jiàn), meaning shameless, cheeky, or rascal; self-censored split character describing Yuwen Xuan's smug face as the black lamb"` |

| 死遁 | faking death to escape | `"Literal CN: 死遁 (sǐdùn) — death escape; literary/trope term for faking one's death to escape a fatal plot or danger; Zhou Hui's contingency plan for Ran Deng"` |

| 哭成了狗 | crying like a dog / weeping uncontrollably | `"Literal CN: 哭成了狗 (kū chéng le gǒu) — crying like a dog; modern Chinese slang for weeping hysterically and shedding uncontrollable tears"` |

| 金仙印 | Golden Immortal Seal | `"Literal CN: 金仙印 (jīnxiānyìn) — Golden Immortal Seal; supreme celestial mark of an immortal appearing as a shimmering golden-red auspicious cloud on the forehead, guaranteeing progress to True Immortal without bottlenecks or tribulations, which inadvertently multiplies Ran Deng's soul-flame homework"` |

| 知其然不知其所以然 | knowing the what without knowing the why | `"Literal CN: 知其然不知其所以然 (zhī qí rán bù zhī qí suǒyǐ rán) — knowing what happens without understanding the underlying principles; Zhou Hui's clinical critique of Goddess Renran's shallow foreign-volunteer-style worldview"` |

| 真神神格 | True God Godhood / Divine Spark | `"Literal CN: 真神神格 (zhēnshén shéngé) — the authentic, permanent divine spark and rule authority over the Ascension Stairway acquired by Zhou Hui, which Jian Haoge and other titans previously mistook for a temporary boon"` |
| 青阳圣主 | Saint Lord Qingyang | `"Literal CN: 青阳圣主 (Qīngyáng Shèngzhǔ) — Saint Lord Qingyang; the ancient ruler of White Jade Capital ten thousand years ago who made the fateful decision to seal the mortal world from invading True Immortals and demons during the God-Demon War"` |

| 真仙诅咒 | Curse of the True Immortals | `"Literal CN: 真仙诅咒 (zhēnxīan zǔzhòu) — generational vendetta and curses placed by True Immortals upon mortal lineages who resisted or hindered them in the ancient God-Demon War"` |
| 特别邀请 | A Special Invitation | `"Literal CN: 特别邀请 (tèbié yāoqǐng) — chapter title referring to Zhou Hui inviting Blood Demon Hall to inspect White Jade Capital Academy under the pretext of pedagogical evaluations to verify karmic bloodlines"` |
| 五驭 | Five Arts of Charioteering (P.E.) | `"Literal CN: 五驭 (wǔyù) — the Five Arts of Charioteering from the classical Six Arts; adapted by Zhou Hui and Shen Qingqian into the foundational physical education and body-refinement curriculum for female students at White Jade Capital Academy"` |

| 教学成果 | Teaching Achievements | `"Literal CN: 教学成果 (jiàoxué chéngguǒ) — Teaching Achievements / Educational Outcomes; chapter title referring to the mortal students proving the efficacy of White Jade Capital Academy's physical education and cultivation curriculum on the Ascension Stairway"` |
| 才刚刚开始 | Only Just Begun | `"Literal CN: 才刚刚开始 (cái gānggāng kāishǐ) — Only Just Begun; chapter title referring to Zhou Hui and Lu Shulong noting that the trials of the students, the spread of the modern curriculum, and the political awakening of mortals have only just begun"` |
| 飞升？ | Ascension? | `"Literal CN: 飞升？ (fēishēng?) — Ascension?; chapter title referring to Zhou Hui temporarily ascending to the upper realm to inspect conditions, leaving Qu Qianxing in charge of the sect"` |
| 世界尽头…… | The End of the World... | `"Literal CN: 世界尽头…… (shìjiè jìntóu……) — The End of the World... / The Edge of the World; chapter title referring to Zhou Hui finding the Door God at the boundary of the upper realm and discovering that the end of the universe is taking civil service exams"` |

| 一些甩锅 | Passing the Buck | `"Literal CN: 一些甩锅 (yīxiē shuǎiguō) — Passing the Buck / Shifting Blame; chapter title referring to Lu Shulong, Jian Nuo, and Qu Qianxing trading blame over the demonic fissure and visiting the Spirit Stone Secret Realm"` |

| 压力测试 | stress test | `"Literal CN: 压力测试 (yālì cèshì) — stress test; modern software engineering and systems testing term used by Qu Qianxing to justify mobilizing Heavenly Spirit Sect disciples into the Demon Realm rift"` |

| 编制 | tenure / official establishment | `"Modern Satire: 编制 (biānzhì) — official government establishment / coveted civil service tenure in China; dangled by the Heavenly Court as a carrot to lure newly ascended mortal cultivators into fighting as disposable frontline cannon fodder"` |
| 填线宝宝 | line-filler babies | `"Gaming / Internet Slang: 填线宝宝 (tiánxiàn bǎobao) — line-filler babies / trench-fillers; disposable low-cost troops drafted solely to hold a defensive line or absorb enemy casualties, applied to newly ascended cultivators in the Heavenly Army"` |
| 小人长戚戚 | The Petty Man Is Forever Beset with Anxiety | `"Allusion / Chapter Title: 小人长戚戚 — from the Analects of Confucius (君子坦荡荡，小人长戚戚): 'The noble man is open and composed; the petty man is forever beset with anxiety'; title of Chapter 239 describing Tian Yan's paranoia, cowardice, and overdrawing of power"` |
| 折纸 | Folding Paper | `"Cosmology / Spatial Technique: 折纸 (zhézhǐ) — folding paper; higher-dimensional spatial technique employed by Lord Tian Yan using celestial tentacles to drag and overlap distinct spatial dimensions into a single coordinate"` |
| 浑沌 | Hundun | `"Mythology / Primordial Beast: 浑沌 (Hùndùn) — formless primordial ferocious beast of cosmic chaos, more perilous than Taotie; lacks fixed physical form, resembling void gas, imperceptible even to True Immortals; devours reality in silence until bursting apart to birth a new minor world; items tainted with its qi gain supreme concealment, imperceptibility, assimilation, and spatial attributes"` |
| 天蛛丝 | Celestial Spider Silk | `"Artifact Material / Lore: 天蛛丝 (tiānzhūsī) — Celestial Spider Silk; rare artifact thread, specifically the strand infused with Hundun qi left tethered to Langhuan by Tian Yan tens of thousands of years ago to gather intelligence, originally intended to refine an undetectable spatial Heavenly Robe"` |
| 人面疮 | Human-Faced Sore | `"Esoteric / Espionage: 人面疮 (rénmiànchuāng) — Human-Faced Sore; parasitic sentient second face molded by Lord Tian Yan from Tian Shou's own soul, growing on the back of Tian Shou's head beneath his helmet and hair to spy on the frontline and report to Tian Yan"` |
| 虎符 | Tiger Tally | `"Heavenly Court / Military Authority: 虎符 (hǔfú) — Tiger Tally; ancient imperial bronze tally divided into halves, conferring supreme military command of the Heavenly Court's legions; jealously guarded by Heaven-Protecting Heavenly King Tian Shou"` |
| 文曲 | Wenqu (Wenqu Star) | `"Astrology / Fate Suppression: 文曲 (Wénqǔ) — the Wenqu constellation fate star, used by Lord Tian Yan to suppress Qu Qianxing's innate divine authority and lock his celestial potential before banishing him into Langhuan"` |
| 别扭的龙 | An Awkward Dragon | `"Chapter Title: 别扭的龙 (bièniu de lóng) — An Awkward Dragon / A Stubborn Dragon; title of Chapter 240 referring to Lu Shulong's awkward, petty, and deeply devoted nature, his hidden cycle trauma, and his draconic rage upon learning the Heavenly Court targeted Master"` |
| 打野 | farming the jungle / jungling | `"Gaming Slang: 打野 (dǎyě) — jungling / clearing neutral mobs on the edge of the map; applied humorously to Lu Shulong's bone dragon slacking off and wiping out peripheral demon nests far away from the central battlefield"` |
| 咩咩叫 | bleat like a sheep | `"Colloquial Humor: 咩咩叫 (miēmiē jiào) — bleating like a sheep; Lu Shulong's triumphant daydream of using his newly unleashed True Dragon strength to beat Eldest Senior Brother Yuwen Xuan until he bleats"` |
| 真龙传承 | True Dragon Inheritance | `"Cosmology / Biology: 真龙传承 (zhēnlóng chuánchéng) — True Dragon Inheritance; instantaneous awakening and mastery of cosmic powers and ancestral techniques, triggered once a True Dragon recognizes their biological draconic lineage"` |
| 高维度生物 | higher-dimensional being | `"Cosmology / True Dragon: 高维度生物 (gāo wéidù shēngwù) — higher-dimensional entity; True Dragons whose true forms, divine souls, and memories transcend single-dimensional time and space, explaining why time rewinds cannot erase Lu Shulong's accumulated memories"` |
